### [1] [Signature of Magnon-Raman Phonon-Polariton Condensation in a Cavity via Transverse Pumping](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26601)
*Subrata Chakraborty,Shishir Kumar Pandey*

Main category: physics.optics

TL;DR: 本文提出一个理论平台，在光学微腔中通过连续波激光泵浦实现磁振子-拉曼声子-极化激元玻色-爱因斯坦凝聚（magnon-Raman phonon-polariton condensate），并推导出稳态相图，展示了可调谐的宏观量子现象控制方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统极化激元平台依赖直接偶极耦合（direct dipole coupling），受光学选择定则（optical selection rules）和太赫兹腔（THz cavities）的限制，难以控制偶极禁戒（dipole-forbidden）或拉曼活性（Raman-active）的晶格和自旋激发，因此需要新的理论方案。

Method: 将包含强耦合磁振子（magnon）和拉曼活性声子（Raman-active phonon, MRP）模式的磁性介质嵌入光学微腔（optical microcavity）中，施加连续波横向激光泵浦（continuous-wave transverse laser pumping），推导出稳态相图（stationary-state phase diagram）。

Result: 展示了在临界条件下不同MRP凝聚相（MRP condensation phases）的涌现特征。通过外磁场（external magnetic field）可调谐裸磁振子频率，横向泵浦频率和外磁场依赖的磁振子可作为高度灵活的控制参数。

Conclusion: 该平台为在腔量子光学（cavity quantum optics）、晶格动力学（lattice dynamics）和量子磁学（quantum magnetism）的交叉领域探索和控制宏观量子现象提供了新的控制手段。

Abstract: Polaritons---hybrid light-matter quasiparticles---provide a versatile platform for dynamically controlling a wide range of condensed matter systems. While conventional polaritonic platforms rely on direct dipole coupling, controlling dipole-forbidden or Raman-active lattice and spin excitations remains challenging due to optical selection rules and the limitations of THz cavities. Here, we propose a theoretical platform for realizing a continuous-wave magnon--Raman phonon--polariton condensate. By embedding a magnetic medium hosting strongly coupled magnon and Raman-active phonon (MRP) modes inside an optical microcavity under continuous-wave transverse laser pumping, we derive the stationary-state phase diagram and demonstrate the emerging signature of distinct MRP condensation phases under critical conditions. Furthermore, since the bare magnon frequency is tunable via an external magnetic field, we show that the transverse pump frequency and the external magnetic field-dependent magnon simultaneously as highly flexible control parameters for exploring and controlling macroscopic quantum phenomena at the interface of cavity quantum optics, lattice dynamics, and quantum magnetism.

</details>

### [2] [From S2 to LISA: Astrometric Bounds on Extreme-Mass-Ratio Inspirals and Bursts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26127)
*Andrea Caputo,Valerie Domcke*

Main category: astro-ph.GA

TL;DR: 本文利用GRAVITY天文台对S2恒星轨道扰动的天体测量限制，约束了银河系中心恒星级黑洞尖峰（stellar-mass black-hole cusp）的归一化强度，进而推导出极端质量比螺旋（EMRI）和极端质量比爆发（EMRB）事件率的上限，并将其外推至宇宙学尺度，评估了LISA对这些引力波信号的探测能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 银河系中心超大质量黑洞（massive black hole）周围的恒星轨道为探测致密天体种群及毫赫兹引力波（millihertz gravitational waves）源（如EMRI和EMRB）提供了独特局部探针，但此前缺乏基于高精度天体测量数据的严格上限约束。

Method: 基于GRAVITY天文台对S2恒星轨道扰动的最新天体测量限制（astrometric limits），对距离银河系中心约0.02 pc范围内的恒星级黑洞尖峰归一化强度设置上限；采用10倍太阳质量（10 M⊙）的Bahcall-Wolf种群作为基准，计算EMRI与EMRB事件率上限；并假设尖峰归一化与中心黑洞质量呈自相似标度（self-similar scaling），将校准结果外推至宇宙学种群。

Result: 得到银河系EMRI率上限约2.4×10² Gyr⁻¹，可探测EMRB率上限约0.2 yr⁻¹，落在以往理论估计的宽泛范围内；在自相似标度假设下，EMRI背景在所有场景下均可被LISA探测到，而EMRB背景仅在低质量星系核中质量分层（mass segregation）高效时才能达到LISA灵敏度。

Conclusion: 研究结果突出了高精度恒星天体测量（precision stellar astrometry）与毫赫兹引力波观测之间的互补性，为理解银河系中心致密天体种群及未来LISA探测提供了关键的观测约束。

Abstract: Stellar orbits around the massive black hole at the center of our galaxy provide a unique local probe of the compact-object population in the Galactic Centre and, consequently, of the sources of millihertz gravitational waves: periapse passages lead to extreme-mass-ratio bursts (EMRBs) while successful captures lead to extreme-mass ration inspirals (EMRIs). In this paper we use recent astrometric limits from the GRAVITY observatory on perturbations of the orbit of the star S2 to place upper limits on the normalisation of a stellar-mass black-hole cusp within ${\sim} \,0.02\,\mathrm{pc}$. For a benchmark $10\,M_\odot$ Bahcall--Wolf population anchored to this data, we obtain upper limits of ${\sim} \,2.4\times10^{2}\,\mathrm{Gyr}^{-1}$ on the EMRI rate and ${\sim} \, 0.2\,\mathrm{yr}^{-1}$ on the detectable EMRB rate in the Milky Way, which fall within the broad range of previous theoretical estimates. Assuming a self-similar scaling of the cusp normalisation with central black-hole mass, we extend this calibration to cosmological populations. The resulting EMRI background is detectable by LISA across all scenarios considered, whereas the flatter EMRB background can reach LISA sensitivity when mass segregation is efficient in low-mass galactic nuclei. Our results highlight the complementarity of precision stellar astrometry and millihertz gravitational-wave observations.

</details>


### [3] [Stellar rotation of S301 as a macroscopic gyroscope to test general relativity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26134)
*Pau Amaro Seoane,Xian Chen,Alejandro Torres-Orjuela,Reinhard Genzel,Frank Eisenhauer,Thomas Ott,Stefan Gillessen,Guillaume Bourdarot,Diogo C. Ribeiro,Matteo Sadun Bordoni,Simran Joharle,Felix Mang,Andreas Burkert,Jorge Cuadra,Diego Calderón,Hagai B. Perets,Tsvi Piran,Thorsten Naab,Re'em Sari*

Main category: astro-ph.GA

TL;DR: 本文通过分析S301恒星（S-star）围绕银河系中心（Galactic Center）的轨道运动，研究广义相对论（general relativity）效应——特别是自转轴的测地进动（geodetic precession）——对恒星光谱线展宽投影变化的影响，并提出该效应可作为检验爱因斯坦理论在强引力场中预言的新手段。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 银河系中心恒星轨迹为检验广义相对论（general relativity）提供了实验场；现有研究尚未充分量化S301恒星自转轴（rotational axis）的相对论性进动（relativistic precession）作为可观测量（observable）的可能性。

Method: 作者纳入一阶后牛顿修正（first-order post-Newtonian corrections）计算轨道运动学；在初始取向与观测几何各向同性（isotropic）分布的假设下，采样赤道速度（equatorial velocity）与旋转椭率（rotational ellipticity）网格，统计40年跨度内投影旋转线展宽绝对变化量 $|Δv \sin i|$ 的似然分布。

Result: 相对论性测地漂移（geodetic shift）与 $v_{\rm rot}$ 成线性关系，经典四极漂移（quadrupole shift）与旋转速度无关，而依赖于椭率参数 $q$。扁球（oblate）恒星的最大绝对速度偏移饱和于 $46.1\,\kms$，中位数偏移范围在 $3\,\kms$ 到 $6.3\,\kms$ 之间，主要由测地进动驱动。

Conclusion: S301的自转相当于一枚飞行的陀螺仪（flying gyroscope），其漂移若被测量，可在先前无法触及的区域内检验爱因斯坦理论；文中提供的 $|Δv \sin i|$ 时域可观测性（time-domain observable）可作为红外光谱仪（infrared spectrograph）测试人马座A*（Sgr~A$^\ast$）周围Schwarzschild度规（Schwarzschild metric）的目标量。

Abstract: Stellar trajectories around the Galactic Center provide a testing environment for general relativity. The intrinsic rotation of these stars evolves under covariant transport in curved spacetime and classical Newtonian quadrupole torques. We analyze the recently observed S301 S-star to quantify the relativistic precession of its rotational axis. Its 8.7-year period and eccentricity of $e = 0.982$ localize geodetic precession and Newtonian quadrupole torques to a step function at periapsis. We incorporate first-order post-Newtonian corrections into the orbital kinematics to calculate the spatial trajectory. Sampling an isotropic distribution of initial orientations and viewing geometries over a 40-year period across a grid of equatorial velocities and rotational ellipticities, we calculate the statistical likelihood of an absolute shift in the projected rotational line broadening, $|Δv \sin i|$. The relativistic geodetic shift scales linearly with $v_{\rm rot}$ and the classical quadrupole shift is independent of rotation speed, scaling with $q$. The absolute maximum velocity shift saturates at $46.1\,\kms$ for oblate stars. The absolute median shifts, driven by geodetic precession, range from $3\,\kms$ to $6.3\,\kms$. We calculate the time-domain observable $|Δv \sin i|$ to provide a target for infrared spectrographs testing the Schwarzschild metric around Sgr~A$^\ast$. The spin of S301 acts as a flying gyroscope whose drift, if measured, can test Einstein's theory in a regime that has not previously been accessible.

</details>


### [4] [Effects of a disk structure on stellar motion at the Galactic Center](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26713)
*Arianna Foschi,Frederic H. Vincent,Thibaut Paumard,Guy Perrin*

Main category: astro-ph.GA

TL;DR: 本文研究银河系中心超大质量黑洞人马座A*（Sagittarius A*）周围的薄盘状延展质量分布对恒星轨道的影响。通过解析方法推导盘状结构的加速度及其对轨道要素的长期扰动，发现盘结构会导致半正焦弦的长期漂移、额外面内进动和面外进动。后者在S301恒星上的效应可能与Lense-Thirring进动竞争，并可用于对银心盘状非发光质量设定上限，其结果显著区别于球形分布假设下的估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 以往研究假设人马座A*周围的延展质量呈球对称分布，但实际延展质量可能更扁平甚至形成盘状结构。本文旨在检验这种盘状结构如何影响银河系中心恒星（特别是S2和S301以及顺时针恒星盘）的轨道运动。

Method: 解析推导具有幂律面密度Σ∝r^{-γ}的薄盘的加速度，利用该加速度计算密切轨道方程（osculating equations）及轨道要素的变化，分析盘取向与轨道面夹角对各要素演化的影响。

Result: 盘结构导致三种长期效应：半正焦弦（semi-latus rectum）的长期漂移、额外面内进动（in-plane precession）和面外进动（out-of-plane precession）。半正焦弦漂移在黑洞的低阶后牛顿描述和球形质量分布中均不存在。面外进动在S301运动上可与SgrA*自旋引起的Lense-Thirring进动竞争，而在S2运动上则可用来对银心盘状非发光质量设定上限。

Conclusion: 利用恒星轨道探测中心黑洞（特别是其自旋）时，必须约束盘状结构的效应。一旦获得盘质量估计，即可量化该结构对S301运动的影响及其与未来SgrA*自旋测量之间的简并关系。

Abstract: Stellar orbits are key for probing the environment of the supermassive black hole at the Galactic Center, Sagittarius A$^*$. So far, the mass around SgrA$^*$ has been assumed to be spherically distributed. However, the extended mass may instead be flattened, creating disk-like structures. We investigate the effects that a thin disk structure would have on stars at the Galactic Center, focusing on star S2 and S301 and the clockwise stellar disk. We derive analytically the acceleration exerted by a disk with power law density $Σ\propto r^{-γ}$. We use this acceleration to compute the osculating equations and the variations of the orbital elements, showing how the latter depend on the orientation of the disk with respect to the orbital plane. We find that the disk structure induces a secular shift in the semi-latus rectum, an extra in-plane precession and an out-of-plane precession. The former is neither present at the low-order post Newtonian description that we use for the black hole, nor when a spherical mass distribution is considered. The latter can be competitive with the Lense-Thirring precession induced by the spin of SgrA$^*$ on S301 motion, depending on the mass, the radial extent and the orientation of the disk. Since the Lense-Thirring precession is negligible in S2 motion, the out-of-plane precession can be used to place upper limits on the non luminous mass of disk-like structures at the Galactic Center. The limits might significantly differ from those obtained for spherical distributions and depend on the disk parameters. These results highlight the importance of constraining disk-like structures when using stellar orbits to probe the central black hole, in particular its spin. Once mass estimates are at hand, one can quantify the disk's effect on S301 motion and the resulting degeneracy with a future measurement of SgrA$^*$ spin.

</details>

### [5] [The boundary-driven multispecies harmonic process](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26262)
*Francesco Casini,Rouven Frassek,Cristian Giardinà*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了多维谐振过程（multispecies harmonic process）的多物种版本，将其与开放有理海森堡自旋链（open rational Heisenberg spin chain）的哈密顿量相联系，推导了R-矩阵和K-矩阵的算子形式，并构造了双行转移矩阵（double-row transfer matrix），同时定义了三种对偶模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将谐振过程从单物种推广到多物种情形，使其与更高秩的可积自旋链建立联系，从而拓展非平衡统计力学中可积系统的理论框架。

Method: 将过程的马尔可夫生成元（Markov generator）识别为开放有理海森堡自旋链的可积哈密顿量；推导算子形式的R-矩阵和K-矩阵；遵循Sklyanin方法构造双行转移矩阵；定义吸收粒子模型、隐藏参数模型和热传导模型三种对偶模型。

Result: R-矩阵可分解为两个R-算子，分别对应左移和右移粒子；建立了多物种谐振过程与可积自旋链之间的严格对应关系；提出了三种对偶模型。

Conclusion: 该工作将谐振过程的多物种版本纳入可积系统框架，为研究非平衡稳态性质和边界驱动系统的精确解提供了新的理论工具。

Abstract: We introduce the multispecies version of the harmonic process on a one-dimensional chain in contact with boundary reservoirs. This process is a continuous-time Markov chain where each site can host an unbounded number of colored particles. The symmetric bulk dynamics is put in contact with reservoirs, which inject and remove particles driving the system out-of-equilibrium. The Markov generator of the process is identified with the integrable Hamiltonian of an open rational Heisenberg spin chain of higher rank. We derive the underlying R- and K-matrices in operator form and construct the double-row transfer matrix following Sklyanin. Similar to the monospecies case the R-matrix factorises into two R-operators, each factor corresponding to left and right moving particles. We further define three dual models: an absorbing particle model, a hidden parameter model and a heat conduction model.

</details>


### [6] [Quantum Turing Patterns](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26331)
*Kazuki Ikeda*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文在Lindblad晶格动力学中构造量子图灵模式（quantum Turing patterns），建立了其非线性序与量子涨落的严格理论，证明了半经典极限下投影相干态的广泛布拉格序（Bragg order），并揭示了波长选择与量子纠缠（quantum entanglement）之间的直接关联。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有图灵模式理论局限于经典非线性系统，缺乏对量子涨落及其与空间序结构相互作用的严格理解。

Method: 利用Lindblad晶格动力学构造完全正（completely positive）的有限程耦合族，通过一阶矩方程的失稳分析得到周期条纹分支，并基于反射不动周期胞空间证明局部渐近稳定性；同时发展非自治高斯Lyapunov流（nonautonomous Gaussian Lyapunov flow）方法分析协方差收敛性。

Result: 微观协方差以$O(N^{-1/2})$速率收敛到非自治高斯Lyapunov流，证明了对足够大$N$的严格部分转置（partial transpose）不确定性违例；在均匀高斯扇区中，单一无量纲比率同时控制图灵稳定性行列式与反对动量的对数负性（logarithmic negativity），将波长选择与量子纠缠直接关联；差分传输将最强反对动量关联从红外移至所选图灵尺度。

Conclusion: 数值延续与二维模拟展示了条纹（stripe）、点状（spot）和迷宫（labyrinth）形态，其傅里叶模和涨落谱均集中于相同选定波数，验证了理论预测。

Abstract: We construct quantum Turing patterns in Lindblad lattice dynamics and establish a rigorous theory of their nonlinear order and quantum fluctuations. For an explicit completely positive family with finite-range couplings, the first-moment equations undergo a supercritical instability at a nonzero wave number and admit analytic site- and bond-centered commensurate stripe branches. These branches are locally asymptotically stable in their reflection-fixed period-cell spaces, and projected coherent states exhibit extensive Bragg order on every bounded time interval in the semiclassical limit. We prove $O(N^{-1/2})$ convergence of microscopic covariances to a nonautonomous Gaussian Lyapunov flow, transferring strict partial-transpose uncertainty violations to sufficiently large $N$. In the homogeneous Gaussian sector, a single dimensionless ratio controls both the Turing stability determinant and the logarithmic negativity of opposite momenta, relating wavelength selection directly to quantum entanglement. Differential transport shifts the strongest opposite-momentum correlations from the infrared to the selected Turing scale. Numerical continuation and two-dimensional simulations display stripe, spot, and labyrinth morphologies whose Fourier modes and fluctuation spectra concentrate at the same selected wave numbers.

</details>


### [7] [Towards First Quantisation Formalism for AKSZ Theories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26394)
*Leon Menger,Pavel Mnev*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个从高维AKSZ理论（AKSZ theory）构造一维图论（graph theory）版本的方法，该一维理论本身也是AKSZ理论，其配分函数（partition function）重现了原理论的费曼图（Feynman diagram）求和，并在BV-BFV形式论（BV-BFV formalism）框架下进行了分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将高维量子场论（quantum field theory）的费曼图展开转化为一个几何上自洽的一维理论，从而统一地理解费曼图的生成机制，并为量子化（quantization）和规范固定（gauge-fixing）提供新的视角。

Method: 基于AKSZ理论，构造一个耦合到一维超引力（1d supergravity）的靶空间（target space）理论，利用度量图（metric graph）上的形式（form）及其积分实现费曼图求和，并采用BV-BFV形式论进行规范固定和经典分析。

Result: 证明了该一维理论的配分函数通过积分重现了原AKSZ理论的费曼图求和；经典层面上，顶点对应的拉格朗日子流形（Lagrangian submanifold）定义了Weinstein辛范畴（symplectic category）中的循环L_∞-代数（cyclic L_∞-algebra）；以非阿贝尔陈-西蒙斯理论（non-abelian Chern–Simons theory）为例，给出了顶点拉格朗日量（Wigner Lagrangian, L_W）的具体形式。

Conclusion: 该一维AKSZ图理论为高维场论的费曼图生成提供了一种几何形式的“第一量子化”（first quantisation）描述，且与BV-BFV形式论兼容，为从辛几何（symplectic geometry）角度理解量子场论提供了新工具。

Abstract: \noindent Given an AKSZ theory $\mathbb{T}$ on a manifold $M$, with target a graded vector space $Y$, we formulate a 1-dimensional theory $\mathbb{t}$ on graphs (the ``first quantisation picture for $\mathbb{T}$''), whose partition functions reproduce the Feynman graphs of $\mathbb{T}$. More precisely, the theory $\mathbb{t}$ is itself a 1d AKSZ theory with the target built out of $M$, and involving a coupling to 1d supergravity. It yields a form on the space of metric graphs (with length $T$ of an edge and its de Rham differential $\mathrm{d} T$ interpreted as the zero-modes of the graviton and gravitino, respectively); its integral yields the sum of Feynman graphs of $\mathbb{T}$. We study the theory $\mathbb{t}$ in the BV-BFV formalism; a gauge-fixing of $\mathbb{T}$ corresponds to a gauge-fixing of $\mathbb{t}$. Such a theory has been successfully extended to Hermitian symmetric Lie algebras in mathematics with physical and geometrical backgrounds. This article is devoted to developing it to Hermitian reductive Lie algebras in a purely geometric way. The three vortex models obtained in this article fulfill that when the Hermitian reductive Lie algebra ${\mathfrak g}$ equi-collapses to a Hermitian symmetric Lie algebra ${\mathfrak h}$, they revert respectively to those in ${\mathfrak h}$.

Abstract: We constrain the Emergent Fractional Fractal (EFF) cosmological model through a joint likelihood analysis of recent cosmological observations at the background and perturbation levels. We consider three combinations of datasets: (i) late-time (LT) observations including PantheonPlus Type Ia supernovae, $H(z)$ measurements, and growth-rate measurements $fσ_8$; (ii) LT combined with DESI DR2 BAO and Big Bang nucleosynthesis (BBN); and (iii) LT combined with DESI DR2 BAO and CMB distance priors. <details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Abstract: This paper addresses the important problem of estimating the noise spectral density of next-generation gravitational-wave detectors, such as LISA and the Einstein Telescope (ET), where cross-channel correlations must be accounted for to avoid biased parameter estimation of gravitational-wave signals. Using the Schwarz--Pick theorem, already employed to derive the quantum chaos bound in black hole physics, we provide a bound on the rate of the tidal response with respect to the frequency. For a neutron star the dynamical Love number is bounded in terms of the static one and of the frequency of the first internal mode, with the single-mode ($f$-mode) model saturating the bound. This analysis aims to define the various stages of cosmic evolution and explore the characteristics of dark energy. The analysis of energy conditions reveals that the essential conditions for achieving a non-singular bounce are violated. Overall, the $f(Q, T)$ gravity model, once reconstructed, effectively captures the cosmic dynamics surrounding the bounce. It offers a cohesive theoretical framework that reliably explains the Universe's evolution during both its early and late stages.

Method: 首先将SFDM的MONDian表述展示为相对论性RAQUAL理论在爱因斯坦框架下牛顿低加速度极限的产物；然后将SFDM的协变相互作用项变换到Jordan框架，与BECDM的相互作用结构进行逐项映射和比较。

Result: SFDM的声子-重子相互作用映射为BECDM的导数耦合到爱因斯坦张量，并加上一个微小的非最小耦合到里奇标量。两模型的相互作用项在爱因斯坦-希尔伯特项的线性扰动下等价，但动力学项不等价——BECDM的标准二次动力学项无法映射为SFDM所需的非解析动力学项。

Abstract: Motivated by their common condensed-matter inspiration and their shared aim of reconciling MOND-like phenomenology on galactic scales with particle dark matter on larger scales, we investigate the relation between Superfluid Dark Matter (SFDM) and Bose--Einstein Condensate Dark Matter (BECDM). Since SFDM is formulated in the Newtonian regime whereas BECDM is fully relativistic, we first show that the MONDian formulation of SFDM arises as the Newtonian, low-acceleration limit of a Relativistic AQUAdratic Lagrangian (RAQUAL) theory in the Einstein frame. We then transform its covariant interaction sector to the Jordan frame and compare it with BECDM. The phonon--baryon interaction of SFDM maps onto the BECDM derivative coupling to the Einstein tensor, supplemented by a small non-minimal coupling to the Ricci scalar. The interaction sectors are therefore equivalent up to a linear perturbation of the Einstein--Hilbert term. Their kinetic sectors, however, remain inequivalent: the standard quadratic kinetic term of BECDM cannot be mapped onto the non-analytic kinetic term required by SFDM. The two models are consequently related but not dynamically equivalent. This mapping provides a covariant interpretation of the SFDM interaction and clarifies which theoretical properties can be transferred between the two frameworks.

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究由有效黑洞几何构建的薄壳虫洞不仅依赖于时函数（lapse function），还依赖于面积半径（areal radius）的选择。本文旨在揭示高分子修正和精质场环境如何共同影响薄壳虫洞的物质分布、径向响应、热力学记账和光学表现。

Method: 采用Israel结条件（Israel junction conditions）分析静态喉的能密度和切线条件；将径向动力学写为有效势问题（effective-potential problem），引入非面积扇区的动量流项；对线性正压（linear barotropic）、可变幻像型（variable phantomlike）和可变Chaplygin气体（variable Chaplygin gas）三种状态方程进行线性稳定性分析；结合局部第一定律和熵记账进行热力学分析；通过光学分析比较虫洞与黑洞的外部临界曲线。

Result: 正分支上的静态喉均具有负表面能密度，切线类空和固有强能量组合受局部时函数斜率控制。线性正压和可变幻像型闭合在本研究所取校准构型下局部不稳定，而可变Chaplygin气体存在有限线性径向稳定窗口。非面积修正改变了局部第一定律平衡和壳熵记账。光学分析显示，尽管虫洞和黑洞几何共享相同的外部临界曲线，穿过喉的传播仍会产生额外的内部分支图像。

Conclusion: 高分子修正与精质场环境共同重组了薄壳虫洞的物质内容、径向响应、热力学记账和光学外观。非面积径向函数的存在引入了新的动力学和稳定性判据，并在光学观测中产生了可区分的内部图像特征。

Abstract: Thin-shell wormholes built from effective black hole geometries are sensitive not only to the lapse function but also to the choice of areal radius. We construct a reflection-symmetric thin-shell wormhole from the positive-lapse sector of a polymer black hole surrounded by Kiselev-type quintessence and keep the nonareal angular function throughout the junction, stability, thermodynamic, and optical analyses. The Israel junction conditions give a negative surface energy density for every static throat on the positive branch, while the tangential null and intrinsic strong energy combinations are controlled by the local lapse slope. The radial dynamics is written as an effective-potential problem in which the nonareal sector produces a momentum-flux term and modifies the local stability criterion for surface equations of state with explicit radius dependence. For the sampled calibrated configurations, the linear barotropic and variable phantomlike closures remain locally unstable, whereas the variable Chaplygin gas admits finite linear radial stability windows. The same geometric correction also modifies the local first-law balance and shell entropy bookkeeping, while the optical analysis shows that cross-throat propagation generates additional inner image branches despite the wormhole and black hole geometries sharing the same exterior critical curve. These results identify how polymer corrections and a quintessence environment jointly reorganize the matter content, radial response, thermodynamic bookkeeping, and optical appearance of the resulting thin-shell wormhole.

</details>

Main category: gr-qc

Abstract: In general relativity, the motion of a test mass around a rotating black hole is described by Kerr geodesics. Owing to the symmetries of the Kerr spacetime, these geodesics possess four constants of motion, rendering the associated Hamiltonian system integrable. This integrability underlies much of the analytical framework used to model asymmetric-mass-ratio inspirals, key sources for future gravitational-wave detectors. Real compact bodies, however, are not test masses: their internal structure couples to the background curvature. In this work, we show that a non-spinning body endowed with a tidally induced quadrupole admits no deformation of the geodesic Carter constant that remains conserved, for generic tidal couplings and generic Kerr spin. Consequently, the leading-order tidal dynamics is generically non-integrable. The proof is analytic and relies on two key ingredients: a covariant Hamiltonian formulation of tidal dynamics on the same phase space as the geodesic problem, valid in arbitrary background spacetimes, and a novel relation between curvature tidal scalars and the geodesic Carter constant derived from the algebraic and Killing symmetries of Kerr spacetime. We complement this result with numerical diagnostics of the tidally perturbed dynamics, including Poincaré sections, Lyapunov exponents, and escape-time maps. These reveal chaotic structures in phase space, such as stochastic layers, sensitivity to initial conditions, and fractal basin boundaries, consistently with the analytic non-integrability result.

Abstract: The superradiant instability of rotating black holes can generate a significant overdensity of bosonic matter around them, together forming a gravitational atom. This mechanism allows one to probe a large part of the parameter space of scalars, axions and vectors that lies beyond the reach of traditional detection strategies. Modelling the dynamics of these systems in binaries is, however, subtle, due to the competing nature of the different perturbations. In this work, we provide a robust scheme to treat these perturbations, including both the minimal set of internal ones (relativistic corrections and self-gravity) and the external tidal field. Within the worldline effective field theory framework, we then calculate the Love numbers of gravitational atoms, for the first time also for the most interesting case of spinning clouds. Certain spinning states are found to have \textit{negative} (static) Love numbers, with a magnitude parametrically enhanced relative to the scaling for the non-spinning states -- in phenomenologically relevant scenarios by a factor $\mathcal{O}(10^2\text{--}10^3)$. This dynamical picture allows us to identify at which stages the permanent multipoles are the strongest indicators of new light bosons, and at which the induced ones take over, while keeping track of both types of finite-size effects even during the resonance. More broadly, our results demonstrate that gravitational atoms are a useful toy model for studying the theoretical aspects of tidal response in gravitational-wave physics.

The residual spectrum also overlaps an isotropic power-law SGWB in the mean binned power. We use a fixed covariance obtained by summing independent Fourier-mode power variances. For a frequency-independent SGWB with fiducial amplitude \(Ω_0=10^{-11}\), marginalizing over the dimensionless residual-power factor \(β\) increases the \(Ω_0\) uncertainty by \(13.6\%\) when the residual power is distributed uniformly over the Fourier frequencies in each bin. The largest Gaussian prior standard deviation on \(β\) that limits this increase to \(10\%\) is \(0.0073\). More concentrated distributions of the residual power among Fourier frequencies reduce the increase, reflecting unresolved frequency structure. Omitting the fiducial residual with the covariance held fixed shifts the best-fitting \(Ω_0\) by \(119.5\) times the uncertainty obtained with \(β\) fixed. The numerical values are conditional on the catalog-level scalar power model, fixed instrumental noise, and independent mode-power covariance.

Abstract: Universal relations provide a particularly useful way to extract physical information from neutron star observables in the presence of various uncertainties by reducing the dependence on uncertain model parameters and microphysical inputs. In this study, we examine the oscillation frequencies of mirror dark matter admixed neutron stars using a two-fluid description, where the outer and inner fluids give rise to two distinct fundamental frequencies. We confirm that the universal relation between the mass-scaled fundamental frequency of the outer-fluid-led mode and the stellar compactness, established previously for self-interacting dark matter admixed neutron stars, also holds in the mirror dark matter scenario. However, this universal relation becomes less robust when metric perturbations are included, compared with the corresponding results in the Cowling approximation. Conclusion: 强势约束下连续介质系统趋向理想约束的极限行为依赖于初始条件的准备程度；当初始条件未充分准备时，会涌现出非平凡的非线性、非局域修正力。本文为理解从可压缩到不可压缩、从可伸长到不可伸长等理想约束极限的动力学机制提供了统一框架。

Abstract: Consider a material point in finite dimensions moving under the influence of a potential force according to Newton's laws. Suppose the potential energy function is a generalized well, strictly convex transverse to a smooth submanifold $M$ on which it is minimal. If the potential is steep, one expects the particle will oscillate rapidly about this submanifold and, if the initial displacement is not too great, should approximately move along $M$ as if it were ideally constrained (e.g. geodesic). This expectation is true if the initial conditions are very well prepared but may fail otherwise - additional potential forces determined by how the Hessian of the potential varies along $M$ may be present. The origin of this force is that the transversal motion acts as a simple harmonic oscillator with a slowly varying frequency, which approximately conserves action, not energy. In this work, we regard continuum mechanical systems such as the elastic thread or compressible fluid as material points moving in an infinite dimensional space according to Newton's laws for appropriate potential energy functionals. We show how to arrive at ideally constrained systems such as the inextensible thread and incompressible fluid as a limit of a strong potential force, computing also corrections to the naive predictions when the data is not very well prepared. For example, for the thread we find a resistance to bending emerge from a strong resistance to compression/expansion. For the fluid, the effective incompressible dynamics may be driven by a remnant acoustical wavefield. Both of these emergent features are nonlinear and non-local. Finally, we give examples of some limits for which the naive models robustly hold because the additional force is trivial. These include the homogeneous incompressible Euler, anelastic Euler, as well as the lake and great lake equations.

Abstract: We study static quark--antiquark interactions in rotating SU(3) gluodynamics using quenched lattice simulations at imaginary angular velocity. At zero temperature, we extract the static potential from Wilson loops for quark--antiquark pairs aligned with the rotation axis, for transverse pairs with one source on the rotation axis, and for symmetric transverse pairs across the rotation axis. Within the present accuracy, no significant rotation dependence or anisotropy is observed in the zero-temperature potential. At finite temperature, imaginary rotation suppresses the color-averaged free energies obtained from Polyakov-loop correlators in both longitudinal and transverse geometries. Axial-diagonal comparisons are used to identify a bulk region where open-boundary artifacts are reduced. In this region, the large-distance longitudinal free-energy shift is well described by $ΔF_z(R_{xy})=A R_{xy}^2+B$. The transverse channels exhibit the same qualitative suppression, while their distance dependence additionally reflects the radial arrangement of the static sources and is compatible with a radial single-source free-energy shift in the bulk region. For the finite-temperature observables studied above $T_c$, the response weakens as the temperature is increased. These results provide lattice evidence for a position- and geometry-dependent response of bare static-source free energies to imaginary rotation in a gluonic medium.

Using this framework, we map a coarse ground state phase diagram in the plane of independent electric and magnetic couplings $(g^2, λ)$ and show that a hysteresis analysis can identify the existence of phase transitions. Restoring the physical relation $λ=4/g^2$, we characterize how increasing the system size and changing the electric coupling $g^2$ move the state away from the reference Néel state, for lattice sizes $L=4,6,8$. More broadly, the method offers a sign-problem-free variational framework for continuous non-Abelian gauge groups with dynamical matter that should extend to other matter content and higher-dimensional lattices.

Abstract: Parastatistics is an exotic type of exchange statistics beyond fermions and bosons. Paraparticles transform in higher dimensional representations of the exchange symmetry group, analogous to non-Abelian anyons, yet consistently defined in any dimension. Although paraparticles have long been proposed, they were widely believed to be physically equivalent to fermions or bosons. Nevertheless, a recent paper proposed a different theory, called $R$-parastatistics, and demonstrated that nontrivial $R$-paraparticles can emerge as quasiparticles in condensed matter systems, and are observably distinct from both fermions and bosons. Central to this paper is a general theory of local observables extending the basic family introduced before. First, we define local observables that distinguish particle types. Second, we formulate local observables at special point defects that probe the internal indices of $R$-paraparticles, crucial for observing $R$-parastatistics and for the proposed applications in quantum information. Third, we introduce local observables that create or annihilate particle-antiparticle pairs, important for building a relativistic quantum field theory for $R$-paraparticles. We further introduce generalized hidden symmetries that act on internal indices of $R$-paraparticles while preserving the local observable algebra, providing a basis for proving local indistinguishability and for connecting to a categorical description of $R$-paraparticles. This work sets a solid theoretical foundation for understanding the fundamental physical properties of $R$-paraparticles and pave the way for finding them in nature.

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的量子因果理论缺乏一个系统性框架来统一描述量子信息过程中的因果结构、时序、维度和引力约束。本文旨在从关系性量子因果过程出发，通过严格可控的模型，逐步建立从量子信息到时空几何的条件收敛路径。

Method: 方法涵盖一系列精确与可控模型：新鲜环境幺正碰撞电路（fresh-environment unitary collision circuits）产生退相位-交换动力学（dephasing-exchange kinetics）；吸收态模型展示非阿贝尔量子记忆（non-Abelian quantum memory）与布尔记录（Boolean records）之间的尖锐相变；反转协变缺陷动力学（reversal-covariant defect dynamics）在限制图族上生成局部有限偏序。在此之上，基于认证序（certified order）、正加性记录测度、紧致性和可辨识性等条件，证明子序列收敛到洛伦兹度量-测度空间。

Conclusion: 该框架（RQCP-QG）被呈现为一个定理索引的体系，分别标定已证机制、条件性组合与开放性假设。尽管获得了若干精确或可控的结果，但尚未推导出能够同时生成邻接、时间、体积归一化、维度、号差、非线性爱因斯坦约束和量子物质的单一背景无关微观定律。

Abstract: Relational quantum causal processes formulate finite operational contexts as normal positive functionals on local completely positive maps. Response differences generate an influence algebra, and its central projections define jointly readable Boolean events. We develop this starting point through a sequence of exact and controlled models. Fresh-environment unitary collision circuits produce dephasing-exchange kinetics with an exact charge-center fixed algebra, a uniform finite-step limit at fixed response order, and graph-controlled metastable Markov dynamics. An absorbing-state model exhibits a sharp transition between non-Abelian quantum memory and Boolean records. A reversal-covariant defect dynamics generates a locally finite partial order on a restricted graph family without assuming a Lyapunov time. Conditional on a certified order, a positive additive record measure, compactness, and identifiability, we prove subsequential convergence to a Lorentzian metric-measure space, finite reconstruction bounds, and uniqueness of admissible smooth limits. Complementary finite regulators provide controlled tests of modular-to-boost response, null tomography, same-update variational identities, induced quadratic gravity, and compatible common-refinement limits. These results are exact or controlled within their stated models, but they do not yet constitute a single background-independent microscopic law that jointly generates adjacency, time, volume normalization, dimension, signature, nonlinear Einstein constraints, and quantum matter. Abstract: We investigate microscopic mechanisms underlying decoherence-induced transitions between topologically ordered states. Abstract: Structural asymmetry combined with optical nonlinearity often leads to nonreciprocal light transport.   <summary>Details</summary>

Method: 采用双态生成器扩展动态模态分解（two-state generator extended dynamic mode decomposition, gEDMD），利用弱值满足精确导数关系$dA_w/dt=i\langle[H,A]\rangle_w$的特性，将生成器提取方法（配有精确导数基线）直接应用于前后选择系综（pre-/post-selected ensemble）。通过对窗口拟合摩擦系数施加关于中点的反演对合（reflection involution），将其分解为反对称部分$γ_A$（承载边界条件物理）和对称部分$γ_S$（来自差分格式），两者均可通过$(γ_{fwd}\pm γ_{bwd})/2$从相同数据获得。在晶格干涉仪中仅对端口进行条件化，即可无监督地提取量子柴郡猫结构。

Result: 时间箭头具有两层结构：相干模（coherent-mode）箭头在中点处反转，而涨落层（fluctuation-level）箭头不反转，且$γ_S$在所有规模和类别中均主导$γ_A$——在涨落层$γ_S$比模层大34倍；使用精确导数时两层均反转。量子柴郡猫结构中，粒子和偏振遵循独立连续性方程，偏振携带臂中的局域场仅旋转该臂相位（恰好两倍场强），在生成器中表现为刚性虚位移（rigid imaginary shift），而粒子的弱密度（weak density）在机器精度内保持不变。在$2^8$到$2^{20}$维度上验证：$|γ_A|/γ_S=0.09$至$0.27$（跨越五种类别）；自平均效应（self-averaging）使其对共享边界调制的类别不敏感，消除了$N$量子比特体系中$2^{-N/2}$重叠阻碍。

Conclusion: 前后选择的时间对称条件可以无监督地提取量子系统深层的结构信息：时间箭头具有可分离的两层结构（相干层可反转、涨落层不可反转），其差异性本质上是程度问题而非种类问题；量子柴郡猫结构可以不需人工假设直接从数据中涌现。这些发现表明，免疫性（immunity）归属于推断方法而非系综本身，且自平均效应可有效缓解高维量子系统中的指数尺度障碍。

Abstract: Conditioning on both past and future assigns intermediate-time properties a causal observer does not; these time-symmetric assignments obey exact symmetry theorems and are measurable from trajectories. We use two-state generator extended dynamic mode decomposition (gEDMD): because weak values obey $dA_w/dt=i\langle[H,A]\rangle_w$ exactly, generator extraction, with an exact-derivative baseline, applies unchanged to them. First, a reflection involution on the pre-/post-selected ensemble splits every window-fitted friction uniquely as $γ_{fwd}=γ_A+γ_S$: $γ_A$, antisymmetric about the midpoint, carries the modes' boundary-condition physics; $γ_S$, symmetric, comes from the differencing scheme; both follow from the same data as $(γ_{fwd}\pmγ_{bwd})/2$. At a fixed inference resolution the arrow of time has two layers: the coherent-mode arrow reverses at the midpoint, the fluctuation-level one does not, $γ_S$ dominating $γ_A$ at every size and class. The difference is one of degree: $γ_S$ is 34 times larger there than at the mode layer, and with the exact derivative both layers reverse: immunity belongs to the inference, not the ensemble. Second, in a lattice interferometer conditioned only at its ports, the quantum Cheshire-cat structure emerges unimposed: particle and polarization obey separate continuity equations, and a local field in the polarization-carrying arm rotates that phase alone, at exactly twice the field strength, entering the generator as a rigid imaginary shift, while the particle's weak density stays invariant to machine precision. We verify the sample-level identity and the two layers from $2^8$ to $2^{20}$ dimensions: $|γ_A|/γ_S=0.09$ to $0.27$ across five classes; self-averaging makes it insensitive to class among those sharing a boundary modulation, removing the $2^{-N/2}$ overlap obstruction for $N$ qubits.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 移动域上的对流-扩散方程（advection-diffusion equation）在科学和工程中广泛出现，但传统的贴体网格方法需要不断重新网格化（remeshing），计算复杂且效率低下。开发一种无需重网格、几何处理简单的笛卡尔网格方法具有重要的数值计算意义。

Method: 将移动域问题重新表述为一个盒子上的界面问题（interface problem），在移动界面上引入未知密度（unknown density）来施加Robin边界条件（Robin boundary condition）。体方程采用笛卡尔网格上的单元中心有限差分格式（cell-centered finite-difference scheme）进行离散，界面修正则通过界面附近窄带内的局部问题获得。

Result: 该方法避免了重网格和切割单元（cut cells），与几何多重网格（geometric multigrid）和无矩阵GMRES方法兼容。GMRES迭代次数基本不依赖于网格尺寸，计算成本与体自由度数量成线性关系。一维格式被证明具有时间一阶收敛和空间二阶收敛。一维和二维数值算例验证了方法的精度和效率。

Conclusion: 所提出的笛卡尔网格方法在移动域Robin边界条件问题上同时实现了精度和效率，具有几何处理简单、计算可扩展性好的优势，适用于如主动输运问题（active transport problem）等实际应用场景。

Abstract: We develop a Cartesian grid method for advection--diffusion equations with Robin boundary conditions on moving domains. The moving-domain problem is reformulated as an interface problem on a box, with an unknown density introduced on the moving interface to enforce the Robin condition. The bulk equation is discretized by a cell-centered finite-difference scheme on the Cartesian grid, while interface corrections are obtained from local problems in a narrow band around the interface. The resulting method requires only modest computational geometry, avoids remeshing and cut cells, and is compatible with geometric multigrid and matrix-free GMRES. The GMRES iteration count is essentially independent of the mesh size, and the computational cost scales linearly with the number of bulk degrees of freedom. For the one-dimensional scheme, first-order convergence in time and second-order convergence in space are proved. Numerical examples in one and two dimensions, including manufactured solutions and an active transport problem without an exact solution, demonstrate the accuracy and efficiency of the method.

</details>


### [66] [Numerical approach to the London Equation of superconductivity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27103)
*Nicolás Barnafi,Ignacio Labarca-Figueroa,Carlos Román*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种在全空间ℝ³中求解第二类超导体（type-II superconductor）London方程的通用离散化策略，通过非标准FEM-BEM耦合处理磁势的传输问题，并基于混合有限元方法（mixed formulation）求解源于Helmholtz-Hodge分解的矢量场B₀，进而分析Ginzburg-Landau模型中涡旋成核（vortex nucleation）的等通量问题（isoflux problem）。对于细长椭球几何，计算表明偏离主轴的非轴对称构型可能具有更大的等通量比，暗示解的非唯一性与旋转简并。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有求解第二类超导体London方程的数值方法通常需引入人工截断边界，难以自然处理全空间ℝ³中的无界外域问题。此外，Ginzburg-Landau模型中等通量问题的极大化构型识别，依赖于对磁势Helmholtz-Hodge分解所得矢量场B₀的精确计算，现有方法在这方面的数值处理尚不充分。

Method: 将London方程重新表述为磁势的传输问题（transmission problem），通过非标准FEM-BEM耦合在内部有界区域和外部无界区域同时离散化，避免人工截断。随后计算磁势Helmholtz-Hodge分解得到的矢量场B₀，采用Kikuchi混合公式（mixed formulation）弱施加散度自由约束，并用经典H(curl)-相容有限元离散求解。

Result: 对于恒定磁场下的球体，等通量问题的唯一极大值为与磁场对齐的直径。对于沿主轴方向施加恒定磁场的椭球，细长雪茄形几何中存在偏离主轴的离轴竞争者（off-axis competitors），其等通量比大于主轴方向，这些构型让人联想到U形涡旋。

Conclusion: 由于主轴不再是极大值点，任何离轴极大值构型都因旋转对称性生成一个等价的连续族，意味着等通量问题中解的非唯一性以及旋转方向上的简并性。

Abstract: In this work, we propose a general discretization strategy for solving the London equation for type-II superconductors in the whole space $\mathbb{R}^3$. To compute the magnetic field $H_0$, we reformulate the problem for the magnetic potential as a transmission problem and discretize it through a nonstandard FEM-BEM coupling. This formulation accounts for both the bounded interior domain and the unbounded exterior domain without introducing an artificial truncation.
  We then compute the vector field $B_0$, which arises from the Helmholtz-Hodge decomposition of the magnetic potential in the superconducting sample. This field enters the isoflux problem, which identifies the curves along which vortex nucleation first becomes energetically favorable in the Ginzburg--Landau model of superconductivity. We recast the equations for $B_0$ using the mixed formulation of Kikuchi, in which the divergence-free constraint is imposed weakly, and discretize the resulting problem using a classical $H(\operatorname{curl})$-conforming finite element discretization.
  We validate our discretization strategy through convergence tests and conclude with an application to the isoflux problem. For a ball under a constant applied magnetic field, the unique maximizer is the diameter aligned with the field. For ellipsoids under a constant applied magnetic field aligned with their major axis, our computations provide numerical evidence of a different behavior in sufficiently elongated, cigar-shaped geometries: off-axis competitors reminiscent of U-shaped vortex configurations attain a larger isoflux ratio than the major axis. Since the major axis is therefore not a maximizer, any off-axis maximizer generates, by rotational symmetry, a continuous family of equivalent configurations, implying non-uniqueness and the presence of a degenerate rotational direction in the isoflux problem.

</details>

### [67] [Fluctuation force induced by quenched random polarizations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26747)
*Bing-Sui Lu*

Main category: cond-mat.dis-nn

TL;DR: 本文研究了由冻结在材料中的随机电偶极子（random electric dipoles）引起的零温波动力的行为，分析了不同几何构型下体块与表面偶极无序（quenched dipolar disorder）诱导的波动力的衰减规律，并发现该力可产生排斥作用从而增强“纳米悬浮效应”（nanolevitation effect）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索由不具备热涨落能力的冻结随机电偶极子所诱导的零温涨落力（fluctuation force）的行为，这类体系对应于弛豫铁电体（relaxor ferroelectrics）等材料，旨在理解无序偶极子体系中的涨落-耗散现象及其力学效应。

Method: 采用理论分析方法，针对两种几何构型进行讨论：(1) 两个共面半无限平板（coplanar semi-infinite slabs）构成的层状系统，间距为 ℓ；(2) 真空中中性原子位于半无限平板表面上方 ℓ 处。分别考虑随机偶极无序存在于体块内部（bulk）和表面（surface）的情况。

Result: 体块（表面）偶极无序诱导的涨落力与单位体积（面积）均方淬灭电偶极矩（mean square quenched electric dipole moment）成正比。对于两个半无限单层平板，体块涨落压力按 ℓ⁻³ 衰减，表面涨落压力按 ℓ⁻⁴ 衰减；对于原子-平板体系，体块涨落力按 ℓ⁻⁴ 衰减，表面涨落力按 ℓ⁻⁵ 衰减。此外，平板间的淬灭偶极无序诱导涨落力可表现为排斥性，进而增强满足 Dzyaloshinskii-Lifshitz-Pitaevskii 条件的三层介质体系中的“纳米悬浮效应”。

Conclusion: 淬灭随机偶极无序能够在零温下诱导出具有明确幂律衰减特征的涨落力，且该力在特定条件下具有排斥性，可用于增强纳米尺度悬浮效应，为弛豫铁电体等无序介电体系中的力学行为提供了新的理论视角。

Abstract: We investigate the zero-temperature behavior of the fluctuation force induced by random electric dipoles that are frozen into a material and incapable of fluctuating thermally. Examples of such materials are relaxor ferroelectrics. In terms of the setup and geometry, we focus on a layered system comprising two coplanar semi-infinite slabs separated by a distance $\ell$ as well as a system comprising a neutral atom in the vacuum located at a distance $\ell$ above the surface of a semi-infinite slab. For both systems, we consider the cases where the quenched random dipolar disorder occurs inside the bulk as well as on the surface of the slabs. In all of these cases, we find that the bulk (surface) dipolar disorder-induced fluctuation force grows with the mean square quenched electric dipole moment per unit volume (area). The bulk (surface) dipolar disorder-induced fluctuation pressure between two semi-infinite single-layered slabs decays with $\ell^{-3}$ ($\ell^{-4}$), whereas the bulk (surface) dipolar disorder-induced fluctuation force on an atom in the vacuum near a slab containing the disorder decays with $\ell^{-4}$ ($\ell^{-5}$). We also find that the quenched dipolar disorder-induced fluctuation force between two coplanar slabs can be repulsive and serve to enhance the ``nanolevitation effect" in a three-layered dielectric system that obeys the Dzyaloshinskii-Lifshitz-Pitaevskii condition.

</details>

*Harukuni Ikeda,Francesco Zamponi*

Main category: cond-mat.dis-nn

TL;DR: 本文通过推广复制液体理论（replica liquid theory）中关于分子包含所有复制的假设，允许每个分子仅包含部分复制，从而刻画粒子级别的复制错配（replica mismatch），并将其应用于高维硬球和谐振子球系统，发现复制错配会改变玻璃态转变的动力学与热力学行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的复制液体理论假设不同复制中的粒子围绕共同质心振动，从而形成包含每个复制各一个粒子的分子。本文旨在放宽这一假设，允许分子仅包含部分复制，从而描述粒子跳跃等非振动运动，并研究复制错配对玻璃化转变（glass transition）描述的影响。

Method: 本文推广了复制液体理论，提出广义化公式，允许每个分子包含复制的子集，以描述粒子级别的复制错配。随后将该理论应用于高维硬球（hard spheres）和谐振子球（harmonic spheres）系统，其中平均场（mean-field）描述预期是精确的。

Result: 对于硬球系统，复制错配使玻璃态亚稳态失稳，并将动力学转变（dynamical transition）推向显著更高的堆积分数（packing fraction），而热力学玻璃转变（thermodynamic glass transition）的前导阶保持不变。对于高温谐振子球系统，即使在热力学理想玻璃转变点，玻璃态中仍存在有限的复制错配分数，从而改变了传统复制变分法预测的转变点。

Conclusion: 高维硬球的转变密度与 Campos、Jenssen、Michelen 和 Sahasrabudhe 通过离散化贪婪随机顺序吸附（greedy Random Sequential Absorption）得到的随机球堆积严格下界在前导阶一致，暗示该转变具有算法解释：巨正则动力学在高维空间中比正则动力学更高效。

Abstract: The replicated liquid theory provides a microscopic mean-field description of the glass transition by combining the density functional theory of liquids with the replica method originally developed for spin glasses. In the conventional replica liquid theory, a glassy state is described by assuming that particles in different replicas undergo vibrational motion around common centers of mass, thereby forming molecules that contain one particle from every replica. Here we revisit this assumption by allowing each molecule to contain only a subset of replicas. This generalized formulation describes particle-level replica mismatches, which may be associated with non-vibrational motions such as particle hopping. We apply the theory to high-dimensional hard and harmonic spheres, where the mean-field description is expected to become exact. For hard spheres, replica mismatches destabilize the glassy metastable state and shift the dynamical transition to a significantly higher packing fraction, while leaving the leading thermodynamic glass transition unchanged. The resulting transition density agrees, at leading order in high dimensions, with the recent rigorous lower bound for random sphere packings obtained by Campos, Jenssen, Michelen, and Sahasrabudhe by using a discretized version of greedy Random Sequential Absorption, suggesting an algorithmic interpretation of the transition: grandcanonical dynamics is more efficient in high dimensional spaces than canonical one. For harmonic spheres at finite temperature, the glassy state contains a finite replica-mismatch fraction even at the thermodynamic ideal-glass transition, thereby shifting the transition point from that predicted by the conventional replica ansatz.

</details>


### [69] [On the robustness of noisy solutions in non-convex neural networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27000)
*Enrico M. Malatesta,Alessandra Passalacqua,Riccardo Zecchina*

Main category: cond-mat.dis-nn

TL;DR: 本文将二元感知器（binary perceptron）中描述零温下解空间几何结构的“重叠间隙性质（overlap gap property, OGP）”推广到有限温度情形。研究表明，零温下主导平衡测度的冻结一步复制对称破缺（frozen one-step replica-symmetry-breaking）解在任意有限温度下仍然存在，且有限温度下的损失函数松弛（relaxation）可以解除冻结。此外，有限温度OGP表明，稠密、算法可达的区域在超过零温OGP阈值后仍存在，且这些区域仍具有良好的泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非凸神经网络模型的优化强烈依赖于解空间的几何结构：稀疏、孤立、点状簇在算法上通常不可达，而宽广平坦的区域虽相对稀少却可以被高效找到。此前该图景仅在零温下通过二元感知器的重叠间隙性质（OGP）得到形式化描述，限制了零训练误差构型在临界约束密度之上的算法访问。本文旨在将这一描述扩展到允许并统计惩罚正训练误差的有限温度情形。

Method: 首先证明零温下主导平衡测度的冻结一步复制对称破缺解在任意有限温度下仍然存在；其次，基于单样本吉布斯权重（Gibbs weight）在决策边界附近的平滑性，推导出有限温度下损失函数松弛解除冻结的一般判据；然后将OGP构造推广到有限温度，证明有限能量构型中稠密、算法可达的区域在超过零温OGP阈值后仍然存在，直至一个随允许训练误差增长的阈值；最后，在教师-学生（teacher-student）设置中，利用有限能量消息传递算法（message-passing algorithm）进行数值验证。

Result: 有限温度下冻结一步复制对称破缺解仍然存在，但损失函数的有限温度松弛可在满足平滑性判据时解除冻结。有限温度OGP表明，稠密且算法可达的有限能量区域存在阈值随允许训练误差增加而上升。在教师-学生场景中，这些宽广的有限能量区域仍保持良好泛化能力。数值实验显示，热噪声（thermal noise）能在约束密度处于“恢复教师”和“寻找零温解”均计算困难（computationally hard）的区域中实现有效泛化。

Conclusion: 有限温度下解空间的几何结构更为丰富：冻结区域可被热松弛解除，稠密可达区域延拓至更高的约束密度。热噪声的引入使得在原本计算困难的区域内仍能实现有效泛化，这为理解非凸神经网络优化的温度效应提供了理论基础。

Abstract: Optimization in non-convex neural network models is strongly influenced by the geometry of the solution space: sparse, isolated, point-like clusters are typically algorithmically inaccessible, whereas wide and flat regions can be found efficiently despite being relatively rare. At zero temperature this picture has been formalized in binary perceptrons through the overlap gap property (OGP), which limits algorithmic access to configurations with zero training error above a critical constraint density $α_{\rm OGP}$. Here we extend this description to finite temperature, where a positive training error is allowed and statistically penalized. We first show that the frozen one-step replica-symmetry-breaking solution, dominating the zero temperature equilibrium measure, survives at any finite temperature. We furthermore derive a general criterion, based on the smoothness of the single-pattern Gibbs weight near the decision boundary, that determines when a finite-temperature relaxation of the loss removes freezing. We then extend the OGP construction to finite temperature and show that dense, algorithmically accessible regions of finite-energy configurations persist beyond $α_{\rm OGP}$, up to a threshold $α_{\rm OGP}(ε)$ that grows with the allowed training error $ε$. Finally, in the teacher-student setting, we show that these wide, finite-energy regions still retain good generalization. Using a finite energy message-passing algorithm, we demonstrate numerically that thermal noise enables effective generalization in the regime of constraint densities where both recovering the teacher and finding a zero temperature solution are computationally hard.

</details>

### [70] [Dirac Fermion Scattering and Pseudospin Polarization in Structurally Asymmetric Graphene Wormholes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26162)
*Arián Gorza,Facundo Arreyes,Juan Sebastián Ardenghi*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本文解析研究了通过结构不对称的catenoid虫洞连接的两片石墨烯片层中无质量狄拉克费米子的量子输运，推导了散射基函数并构建了转移矩阵，揭示了曲率、自旋联络与结构不对称性对透射概率、赝自旋极化及负传输相位时间的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索弯曲时空（curved spacetime）中狄拉克费米子的量子输运行为，特别是结构不对称的虫洞几何（wormhole geometry）如何影响无质量狄拉克费米子的透射与赝自旋（pseudospin）动力学。

Method: 在(2+1)维弯曲时空中，通过解析推导得到平坦片层中的整数阶汉克尔函数（Hankel functions of integer order）和弯曲喉部的高斯超几何函数（Gauss hypergeometric functions）作为散射基函数；采用分段数值匹配构建转移矩阵（transfer matrix），并验证其幺正性至数值精度。

Result: 透射概率随能量单调上升至1；全局透射在结构不对称反转下呈现镜像简并，但局域可观测量依赖入射方向；流形的自旋联络（spin connection）作为厄米耦合在喉部诱导A/B子晶格不平衡；结构不对称诱导局域赝自旋不平衡；曲率半径增大通过更大的几何相位增强P_z极化，而突变的入射角度则抑制该极化；势垒下模式呈现负的传输相位时间（negative transmission phase time），与Hartman型波包重塑兼容。

Conclusion: 结构不对称的虫洞几何为调控石墨烯中狄拉克费米子的量子输运与赝自旋极化提供了新的自由度，负传输相位时间表明势垒下存在非经典波包动力学行为。

Abstract: We study the quantum transport of massless Dirac fermions through two asymptotically flat graphene sheets connected by a structurally asymmetric catenoid wormhole in $(2+1)$-dimensional curved spacetime. Analytic scattering basis functions are derived: Hankel functions of integer order (in the half-flux sector) in the flat sheets and Gauss hypergeometric functions in the curved throat. We construct a transfer matrix via piecewise numerical matching, verifying unitarity up to numerical precision. The transmission probability rises monotonically to unity at high energies. Global transmission exhibits mirror degeneracy under inversion of structural asymmetry, but local observables depend on incidence direction. The manifold's spin connection acts as a Hermitian coupling inducing an $A/B$ sublattice imbalance at the throat. Structural asymmetry induces a local pseudospin imbalance. A larger curvature radius enhances $P_z$ polarization via a larger geometric phase; abrupt incidence suppresses it. Sub-barrier modes exhibit a negative transmission phase time, compatible with Hartman-type wave-packet reshaping.

</details>


### [71] [Near-zero effective magnetization enabling ultra-low threshold currents in spin Hall micro-oscillators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26299)
*A. Koujok,H. Kurebayashi,K. Yamamoto,B. Heinz,V. K. Kushwaha,X. Hou,A. Hamadeh,T. Seki,P. Pirro*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本文通过工程化近零有效磁化强度（$M_\mathrm{eff}$），将自旋霍尔振荡器（Spin Hall oscillators）的磁化振荡阈值电流密度降低了两个数量级以上，使得在微米尺度（"Spin Hall Micro-Oscillators", SHMOs）上也能实现低电流激发，并通过微聚焦布里渊光散射光谱和微磁模拟系统验证了这一策略的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 降低激发磁化动力学所需的电流是推动低功耗磁振子器件（magnonic devices）和基于振荡器的计算（oscillator-based computing）的核心挑战。传统自旋霍尔振荡器（Spin Hall Nano Oscillators, SHNOs）需依赖大电流密度来补偿本征磁阻尼，通常只能在纳米尺度下工作，限制了器件的灵活性和功耗表现。

Method: 本文采用工程化近零有效磁化强度（$M_\mathrm{eff}$）的策略，制备了基于W/CoFeB/MgO/Ta的自旋霍尔微振荡器（SHMOs）。使用微聚焦布里渊光散射光谱（micro-focused Brillouin light scattering spectroscopy）定量测量阈值电流密度，并结合系统性的微磁模拟（micromagnetic simulations）分析宏自旋近似（macrospin approximation）的失效机制及$M_\mathrm{eff}$对磁化动力学的影响。

Result: 实验测得SHMOs的阈值电流密度低至$J_{\mathrm{th}} = (0.292 \pm 0.025) \times 10^{10}$ A/m$^2$，相较近期报道的SHNOs降低了两个数量级以上。微磁模拟进一步揭示，近零$M_\mathrm{eff}$设计是降低阈值的关键，同时宏自旋近似在该体系下不再适用。

Conclusion: 研究表明，工程化近零$M_\mathrm{eff}$是实现超低功耗自旋霍尔振荡器（ultra-low-power spin Hall oscillators）和节能磁化控制（energy-efficient magnetization control）的有效策略，为开发微米尺度、低电流激发的自旋霍尔器件提供了新路径。

Abstract: Reducing the electrical current required to excite magnetization dynamics is a central challenge, e.g. for energy-efficient magnonic devices or oscillator-based computing. Spin Hall oscillators typically rely on large current densities to compensate intrinsic magnetic damping, so these systems are usually studied on the nanoscale (Spin Hall Nano Oscillators, SHNOs) to work with moderate currents and a favorable heat dissipation geometry. Here, we demonstrate that engineering a near-zero effective magnetization ($M_\mathrm{eff}$) enables a drastic reduction of the magnetization oscillation threshold current density for Spin Hall oscillators. This makes it possible to excite even comparably large systems with micrometer lateral sizes, so-called "Spin Hall Micro-Oscillators" (SHMOs). Using micro-focused Brillouin light scattering spectroscopy, we quantify the threshold current density in SHMOs based on W/CoFeB/MgO/Ta with near-zero $M_\mathrm{eff}$. We observe threshold current densities as low as $J_{\mathrm{th}}$ = (0.292 $\pm$ 0.025) $\times 10^{10}$ A/m$^{2}$, representing a reduction of more than two orders of magnitude compared with most recent reported SHNOs. Using systematic micromagnetic simulations, we investigate the breaking down of the macrospin approximation and underline the high influence of $M_\mathrm{eff}$ on magnetization dynamics under applied spin currents. Our results establish $M_\mathrm{eff}$ engineering as a powerful strategy for realizing ultra-low-power spin Hall oscillators and energy-efficient magnetization control.

</details>


### [72] [Giant Bulk-Rashba Splitting in Polar Topological Insulator BiSbTeS$_2$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26311)
*Ritam Chakraborty*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本文通过第一性原理计算证明，有序结构BiSbTeSe₂因失去中心反演对称性而允许体Rashba自旋劈裂（bulk-Rashba spin splitting），同时保持拓扑绝缘体（topological insulator）的体能带反转和线性表面态。导带本征线性Rashba系数高达约2.66 eV·Å，接近基准极性Rashba半导体BiTeI，使其成为最强体Rashba拓扑绝缘体系统之一。该工作表明亚晶格有序化（sublattice ordering）可实现巨大体自旋-动量锁定与受保护拓扑表面态的共存。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在Bi₂Se₃或Bi₂Te₃等四二硫族拓扑绝缘体中，五层堆垛保持中心反演对称性，因而体Rashba自旋劈裂被禁止。寻找兼具体Rashba效应与拓扑绝缘体特性的材料体系，为在同一材料中研究体Rashba与拓扑表面对自旋和电荷输运的贡献提供平台。

Method: 采用考虑自旋-轨道耦合（spin-orbit coupling）的第一性原理密度泛函计算（first-principles density functional calculations），分析有序BiSbTeSe₂（Se-Bi-Se-Sb-Te序列）的电子结构。将导带和价带双重态拟合到对称性约束的两带 k·p 哈密顿量（two-band k·p Hamiltonians），提取本征线性Rashba系数。

Result: 有序BiSbTeSe₂在Γ点处的点群对称性从D₃d降至C₃v，保留了拓扑绝缘体的体能带反转和线性色散表面态。能带在Γ点附近呈现明显的线性k依赖自旋劈裂。导带本征线性Rashba系数α_CB≈2.66 eV·Å，价带α_VB≈0.35 eV·Å；导带值将BiSbTeSe₂列为迄今报道的最强体Rashba拓扑绝缘体系统之一，接近基准极性Rashba半导体BiTeI的耦合强度。

Conclusion: 亚晶格有序化为实现巨大的体自旋-动量锁定（bulk spin-momentum locking）与受保护拓扑表面态（protected topological surface states）共存提供了可行路径，使BiSbTeSe₂成为一个可在同一材料内同时研究体Rashba和拓扑表面对自旋与电荷输运贡献的平台。

Abstract: Bulk-Rashba spin splitting is forbidden in tetradymite topological insulators like Bi$_2$Se$_3$ or Bi$_2$Te$_3$, since their quintuple-layer stacking preserves inversion symmetry. We show that BiSbTeSe$_2$ escapes this restriction: in the Se-Bi-Se-Sb-Te sequence, the structure loses its inversion center, reducing the point group symmetry at $Γ$ from $D_{3d}$ to $C_{3v}$. First-principles density functional calculations with spin-orbit coupling show that this ordered structure retains bulk band inversion and a linearly dispersive surface state of a strong topological insulator. Additionally, its bulk bands acquire a pronounced linear-in-$k$ spin splitting away from $Γ$. Fitting the conduction- and valence-band doublets to symmetry-constrained two-band $k\cdot p$ Hamiltonians, we extract intrinsic linear Rashba coefficients of $α_{\mathrm{CB}}\approx2.66~\mathrm{eV\,\textÅ}$ and $α_{\mathrm{VB}}\approx0.35~\mathrm{eV\,\textÅ}$. The conduction-band value places ordered BiSbTeSe$_2$ among the strongest bulk-Rashba topological-insulator systems reported to date and approaches the coupling found in the benchmark polar Rashba semiconductor BiTeI.Sublattice ordering thus provides a route to giant bulk spin--momentum locking that coexists with protected topological surface states, offering a platform in which bulk Rashba and topological surface contributions to spin and charge transport can be investigated within the same material.

</details>


### [73] [On the static dielectric constant of thin dielectrics in extremely scaled silicon nanosheet transistors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26328)
*Massimo V. Fischetti,Dallin O. Nielsen,Edward Chen*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本文通过综述文献和简单模型论证，小尺寸半导体和绝缘体纳米结构（如薄/窄结构）的静态介电常数（static dielectric constant）强烈依赖于其所处环境；其中光学声子（optical phonon）受限对态密度（density of states）的降低对介电常数影响可忽略，且在实际结构（如双栅硅纳米片）中使用体材料（bulk）介电常数是合理的。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 澄清小尺寸半导体和绝缘体纳米结构中静态介电常数是否依赖于环境这一关键问题，并回应文献中关于光学声子受限效应影响介电常数的争议性主张。

Method: 采用文献综述方法回顾电子响应部分；针对离子响应，在文献综述之外引入一个简单模型（simple model）来考虑薄膜中光学声子的受限效应，并评估其对态密度和介电常数的影响。

Result: 光学声子受限导致的态密度减少对介电常数的影响可忽略不计，这与部分文献中的论断相反。在双栅硅纳米片（double-gated Si nanosheets）等实际结构中，通道和栅绝缘体均采用体材料介电常数是合理的。

Conclusion: 小尺寸半导体和绝缘体纳米结构的静态介电常数确实对环境敏感，但光学声子受限效应并非关键因素；在实际纳米器件中沿用体材料介电常数具有合理性。

Abstract: We argue that the static dielectric constant of small (thin and/or narrow) semiconductor and insulator nanostructures depends strongly on the their environment. We do so by considering the electronic response simply reviewing, briefly but critically, the existing literature. Regarding the ionic response, in addition to reviewing the literature, we use a simple model to account for the confinement of optical phonons in thin films and show that the reduction of their density of states has a negligible effect on the dielectric constant, in contrast to some claims found in the literature. In general, we argue that in realistic structures, such as double-gated Si nanosheets, the use of the bulk dielectric constants for both the channel and the gate insulators, is justified.

</details>

Method: 通过理论分析，研究在界面Dzyaloshinskii-Moriya相互作用（iDMI）存在条件下，Zhang-Li扭矩（一种电流诱导的自旋转移扭矩（STT））在器件边界非均匀磁化区域的作用，并比较其翻转行为与SOT的异同。

Abstract: The spin-orbit torque (SOT) is widely recognized as the primary mechanism for current-driven magnetization switching in nonmagnetic/ferromagnetic bilayers. In this work, we show that, in the presence of interfacial Dzyaloshinskii-Moriya interaction (iDMI), the Zhang-Li torque, a current-induced spin-transfer torque (STT) acting on nonuniform magnetization at the device boundary, can trigger switching behaviors strikingly similar to those of SOT, including field-assisted deterministic reversal and switching polarity control. These results suggest that the Zhang-Li torque may act as a significant complementary mechanism to SOT. This work provides a broader perspective on the switching physics in heavy metal/ferromagnet (HM/FM) structures and offers practical guidance for the design of SOT-MRAM devices.

</details>


### [75] [Asymmetric Floquet-Engineered Mode Coupling in Hybrid Magnonics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26453)
*Amin Pishehvar,Jayakrishnan M. P. Nair,Zixin Yan,Yu Jiang,Benedetta Flebus,Xufeng Zhang*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本文提出在混合磁振子系统（hybrid magnonic system）中采用双音Floquet调制（dual-tone Floquet modulation），通过两路共轭驱动的相对相位θ连续调控Floquet工程化相互作用的非对称性，实现了此前线性及单音调制方案中无法获得的非对称模式耦合（asymmetric mode coupling），并在强耦合腔磁振子器件中展示了可逆切换的单边Autler-Townes分裂（single-sided Autler-Townes splitting）作为光谱学证据。

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在混合磁振子系统中，线性磁振子-光子杂化（magnon-photon hybridization）固有的对称性和互易性（reciprocity）阻碍了非对称模式耦合的实现；单音Floquet调制虽可调控耦合强度，但生成的相互作用仍然是对称的，无法打破互易性。因此需要引入新的自由度以实现可编程的非对称耦合。

Method: 引入双音Floquet调制（dual-tone Floquet modulation），利用两路共轭驱动之间的相对相位θ作为连续可调的控制参数，在混合磁振子系统中工程化非对称的相互作用（asymmetric interaction）。实验上在强耦合腔磁振子器件（strongly coupled cavity magnonic device）中实现该方案。

Result: 通过连续调节相对相位θ，可在两个混合模（hybrid modes）之间可逆地切换单边Autler-Townes分裂（single-sided Autler-Townes splitting），该现象直接证明了相位可编程的非对称模式耦合（phase-programmable asymmetric coupling）的存在。

Conclusion: 该研究为实现混合磁振子系统中可控的非互易（nonreciprocal）和拓扑（topological）功能开辟了新路径，对先进量子与经典信号处理具有广泛意义。

Abstract: In hybrid magnonic systems, linear magnon--photon hybridization inherently produces symmetric, reciprocal interactions, precluding asymmetric mode coupling. Floquet driving can tailor mode coupling strengths but, with single-tone modulation, inevitably generates a symmetric interaction that preserves this reciprocity. Here we introduce dual-tone Floquet modulation to unlock a new degree of freedom in hybrid magnonic systems, where the relative phase $θ$ of two commensurate drives continuously controls the asymmetry of the Floquet-engineered interaction, enabling asymmetric mode coupling absent in existing hybrid magnonic systems. We demonstrate this in a strongly coupled cavity magnonic device, where tuning $θ$ reversibly switches single-sided Autler--Townes splitting between the two hybrid modes---a direct spectroscopic signature of phase-programmable asymmetric coupling. This approach opens a new path toward controllable nonreciprocal and topological functionalities in hybrid magnonic systems, with broad implications for advanced quantum and classical signal processing.

</details>


### [76] [Same-spin Andreev reflections in the quantum Hall regime: the role of loss](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26487)

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 超导体与拓扑材料的界面有望实现奇异量子态与激发，手性安德烈夫边缘态（chiral Andreev edge states, CAES）是其中的重要实例。本研究旨在探索CAES的自旋特性，揭示自旋极化手性态中安德烈夫反射（Andreev reflection）的微观机制。

Method: 通过量子霍尔（quantum Hall, QH）边缘通道的选择性自旋过滤（selective spin filtering）来研究CAES的自旋性质；利用随机矩阵理论（random matrix theory）对反射概率的指数分布进行理论解释。

Result: 发现安德烈夫过程中存在自旋翻转（spin-flip）：电子从超导体反射为同一自旋通道的空穴；反射概率的分布呈指数形式；在自旋极化ν=1情形下也观测到了安德烈夫反射，该过程由粒子损失（particle loss）所促成。

Conclusion: 研究揭示了自旋极化手性态中安德烈夫反射的底层机制，并强调在混合材料中构建拓扑超导体（topological superconductors）时必须考虑非厄米性（non-Hermiticity）的重要作用。

Abstract: The interfaces of superconductors and topological materials hold promise for realizing exotic states and excitations. An important example is provided by the chiral Andreev edge states (CAES), which are formed at interfaces between quantum Hall (QH) states and superconductors (SC). CAES combine electron and hole amplitudes which are hybridized via Andreev reflections. This study explores the spin properties of the CAES through selective spin filtering of the QH edge channels. We find robust evidence of spin-flips accompanying the Andreev processes: electrons can be reflected from the superconductor as holes in the same spin channel. We demonstrate that the distribution of the reflection probabilities is exponential and then use random matrix theory to account for this observation. Finally, we observe Andreev reflections in the spin-polarized ν = 1 case, which is enabled by particle loss. Our findings shed light on the mechanism underlying Andreev reflections of spin-polarized chiral states. They also demonstrate the importance of considering non-Hermiticity when constructing topological superconductors in hybrid materials.

</details>


### [77] [Entanglement signatures of topological phase transitions in a dirty Weyl semimetal](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26660)

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 外尔半金属是范例性的无带隙拓扑相（gapless topological phase），其节点结构在弱无序下稳定，但强无序或动量空间内的成对湮灭可破坏拓扑；本文旨在厘清这两种拓扑相变过程中纠缠谱与纠缠熵的演化行为。

Method: 分析含无序外尔半金属的约化关联矩阵（reduced correlation matrix）的特征值分布；利用Rényi熵（Rényi entropy）标度行为刻画无序驱动相变；对比节点湮灭与无序诱导金属化两种转变路径。

Result: 在干净极限下，由ξ=1/2本征值构成的特征分布（反映费米弧结构）在无序作用下展宽为有限宽度分布；节点湮灭导致该分布光谱权重逐渐丧失，而金属化转变则使其融入背景；弱无序下Rényi熵的标度在接近临界无序强度时逐渐失效。

Conclusion: 纠缠谱和纠缠熵能够有效区分外尔半金属的两种拓扑相变路径：节点湮灭过程呈现光谱权重的渐进消失，无序驱动金属化则伴随纠缠标度行为的崩解。

Abstract: Three-dimensional Weyl semimetals (WSMs) constitute paradigmatic gapless topological phases whose nodal structure is stable against weak perturbations, including disorder. Two distinct ways to destroy the topology of the Weyl nodes are through pairwise annihilation in momentum space or by sufficiently strong disorder that eliminates the quasiparticle pole, driving the system into a non-Fermi-liquid diffusive-metallic state. In this work, we study the evolution of entanglement spectrum and entanglement entropy of a dirty WSM as it undergoes these two topological transitions. In the clean limit, the WSM topology is reflected by a locus of $ξ=1/2$ eigenvalues of the reduced correlation matrix mirroring the structure of Fermi arcs. We show that disorder broadens this feature into a finite-width distribution, which disappears either through a gradual loss of spectral weight upon node annihilation or by melting into the background at the onset of metallicity. When tuning across the disorder-driven transition, the scaling of the Rényi entropies observed for weak disorder gradually breaks down as the system approaches the critical disorder strength.

</details>


### [78] [Scanning Gate Microscopy Modulation of Supercurrent in Graphene Josephson Junctions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26689)
*Ivan Villani,Samuele Fracassi,Niccolò Traverso Ziani,Maura Sassetti,Matteo Carrega,Vaidotas Miseikis,Camilla Coletti,Fabio Beltram,Kenji Watanabe,Takashi Taniguchi,Sergio Pezzini,Stefan Heun*

TL;DR: 本文利用扫描门显微镜（Scanning Gate Microscope, SGM）对六方氮化硼（hBN）封装的石墨烯约瑟夫森结（graphene Josephson junctions）进行超电流输运研究，通过实验测量与数值模拟的一致性，展示了在超高空间分辨率下对栅极可调超导现象进行局部成像与操控的可能性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 石墨烯约瑟夫森结因其高载流子迁移率（high carrier mobility）、弹道输运（ballistic transport）以及大范围栅极可调临界电流等优势，在量子技术中具有重要应用前景。研究超电流（supercurrent flow）的空间分布对于揭示输运机制和推进器件工程至关重要。

Method: 采用扫描门显微镜（Scanning Gate Microscope, SGM）对由铌（Niobium）引线接触的hBN封装石墨烯约瑟夫森结中的超电流输运进行探测。研究了超电流调制随施加的针尖电压偏置（tip voltage bias）和针尖-样品间距（tip-to-sample distance）的变化，并对针尖诱导的调制进行了完整表征。

Result: 实验获得的超电流调制结果与数值模拟在定量上保持一致。

Conclusion: 该方法为在超高空间分辨率下对栅极可调超导现象进行局部映射和操控开辟了道路。

Abstract: Graphene Josephson junctions represent an excellent platform for quantum technologies, thanks to the combination of high carrier mobility, ballistic transport, and large gate-tunable critical currents, preserved even under quantizing magnetic fields. Investigating the spatial distribution of supercurrent flow could be crucial for elucidating transport mechanisms and advancing the engineering of these devices. In this work, we employ a Scanning Gate Microscope to investigate supercurrent transport in hBN-encapsulated graphene Josephson junctions contacted by Niobium leads. We study the supercurrent modulation as a function of the applied tip voltage bias and tip-to-sample distance, and provide a complete characterization of the tip-induced modulation. Our experimental results are quantitatively consistent with numerical simulations and pave the way towards local mapping and manipulation of gate-tunable superconducting phenomena with unprecedented spatial resolution.

</details>


### [79] [Symmetry-Selective Strain Control of Anisotropic Magnetic Response in a Silicon FinFET Double Quantum Dot](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26938)
*Yuze Lu,Xiaoyan Liu,Fei Liu*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本文通过结合六带 k·p 模型的 Poisson–Schrödinger 计算与组态相互作用（configuration interaction），理论研究了硅 FinFET 双量子点中局域 g 张量受应变的影响。研究发现，应变的张量分量（如 ε_yy, ε_zz 和 ε_yz）及其空间对称性分别以不同方式调控磁响应的强度和方向，其中剪切分量 ε_yz 在对称性破缺时可显著旋转主轴方向，为控制空穴自旋量子比特的磁响应提供了理论依据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 三维量子点结构（如硅 FinFET）在制备和冷却过程中自然产生应变。在双量子点中，两个量子点可能经历不同的局域应变，从而获得不同的磁响应。为理解这种点间应变差异如何影响耦合空穴自旋，作者开展了本项理论研究。

Method: 研究基于六带 k·p 模型（six-band k·p model）的三维 Poisson–Schrödinger 计算，并结合组态相互作用（configuration interaction）方法，对硅 FinFET 双量子点中的局域 g 张量（local g tensor）进行理论分析。

Result: 应变效应取决于其张量分量和空间对称性。对角分量 ε_yy 和 ε_zz 主要改变主 g 值，对最大响应轴方向影响较小；剪切分量 ε_yz 可改变局域磁响应的指向。当应变剖面保持横向镜面对称时，剪切引起的旋转被强烈抑制；打破该对称性则产生显著的非对角响应并旋转主轴。这些趋势在仅考虑 Zeeman 耦合的计算中同样出现，表明价带 Zeeman 耦合足以产生这些效应，完整哈密顿量则决定其定量表达。

Conclusion: 应变的张量分量与空间对称性可用于控制耦合空穴自旋量子比特（coupled hole-spin qubits）中磁响应的大小和方向。

Abstract: Strain naturally develops in three-dimensional quantum-dot structures such as silicon FinFETs during fabrication and cooling. Such strain becomes especially important in a double quantum dot, because the two dots can experience different local strain and therefore acquire different magnetic responses. To understand how this dot-to-dot strain difference affects coupled hole spins, we theoretically study the local \(g\) tensors of a silicon FinFET double quantum dot by combining a three-dimensional Poisson--Schrödinger calculation based on a six-band \(k\!\cdot\!p\) model with configuration interaction. We find that the effect of strain depends on both its tensor component and its spatial symmetry. For the diagonal components \(ε_{yy}\) and \(ε_{zz}\), strain mainly changes the principal \(g\) values, with only a small opening of the maximum-response axes. In contrast, the shear component \(ε_{yz}\) can also change the orientation of the local magnetic response. When the strain profile preserves the transverse mirror symmetry, the shear-induced rotation is strongly suppressed. Breaking this local constraint permits a pronounced off-diagonal response and rotates the principal magnetic axes. The same component- and symmetry-selected trends appear in a Zeeman-only calculation, showing that the valence-band Zeeman coupling is sufficient to generate them, while the full Hamiltonian determines their quantitative expression. Together, these results show how the tensor component and spatial symmetry of strain can be used to control both the magnitude and orientation of the magnetic response in coupled hole-spin qubits.

</details>


### [80] [Majorana current induced by charge current in Kitaev magnet/graphene bilayers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26943)
*Takehito Yokoyama*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究如何通过电荷电流（charge current）实现对外部电中性的马约拉纳费米子（Majorana fermion）的电子学控制（electronic control）。

Method: 基于Kondo型马约拉纳-电子耦合（Kondo type Majorana-electron coupling）进行微扰计算，推导马约拉纳电流密度（Majorana current density）的解析表达式。

Result: 得到了由电荷电流诱导的马约拉纳电流的解析表达式，表明电荷电流可以驱动马约拉纳费米子的运动；同时讨论了马约拉纳热电流（Majorana heat current）并给出了量级估计。

Conclusion: 电荷电流可在Kitaev磁体与石墨烯复合体系中有效产生并调控马约拉纳电流与马约拉纳热电流，为电中性马约拉纳费米子的电子学操控提供了理论依据。

Abstract: We investigate Majorana current induced by charge current in Kitaev magnets coupled with graphene through the mechanism by which Majorana fermions in the Kitaev magnet are dragged by electrons in graphene. We calculate Majorana current density based on the perturbation with respect to a Kondo type Majorana-electron coupling and obtain an analytical expression of the Majorana current induced by charge current, indicative of electronic control of charge-neutral Majorana fermions. </details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 半导体量子比特（semiconductor qubits）在继承宿主晶格优点的同时，不可避免地与结构无序和界面耦合，尤其是SiGe平台生长过程中引入的晶格位错、非均匀应变和交叉影线图案，会导致器件间的波动、量子比特失效及更高的操作开销。因此，有必要在纳米尺度上揭示这些扩展缺陷的结构特征及其对量子比特能谱的影响。

Method: 采用X射线纳米结构映射（X-ray nano-structural mapping）技术，对Intel Si/SiGe芯片进行表征，实现了30纳米横向分辨率和200纳米功能深度分辨率的测量，并进一步在约1微米尺度（即量子点（quantum dot）器件的典型尺寸）上进行关联分析。

Result: 发现生长过程中引入的扩展晶格缺陷会通过异质结构（heterostructure）传播，造成永久的晶格面畸变和应变。观察到交叉影线的精细结构，并发现衬底偏角（substrate miscut）和生长条件与最终的交叉影线图案相关。进一步计算了这些缺陷对量子比特能谱的影响。

Conclusion: SiGe平台中扩展晶格缺陷的纳米级结构特征可被定量映射并与量子点器件尺度关联，这些缺陷对量子比特能谱的影响可通过计算加以评估，提示生长工程和衬底控制对提升半导体量子比特一致性的重要性。

Abstract: Semiconductor qubits, promising for quantum computation, inherit properties from their host lattice. Quantum dot spins, occupying the local lowest energy states in the conduction band, necessarily couple to structural disorder and interfaces. While silicon-based systems promise low noise alongside industrially compatible manufacturing, the growth of SiGe---a leading platform---unavoidably introduces lattice dislocations, inhomogeneous strain, and crosshatch patterns, expected to cause fluctuations between devices, qubit failure, and subsequently higher operational overhead. Through X-ray nano-structural mapping of an Intel Si/SiGe chip, we reveal, with 30$~$nm lateral and 200$~$nm functional depth resolution, how extended lattice defects introduced during growth propagate through the heterostructure, creating permanently distorted lattice planes and strain. We correlate these at the $\approx1~μ$m scale of a quantum dot device and calculate the impact on qubit energy spectra. We observe crosshatch fine structure and find that substrate miscut and growth correlate with the final crosshatch pattern.

</details>


### [82] [Two-magnon scattering in the framework of the Lippmann-Schwinger equation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26974)
*Jorge Marquez Chavez,Ondřej Wojewoda,Yixuan Song,Geoffrey S. D. Beach,Caroline A. Ross*

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 控制磁损耗（magnetic losses）需要识别外在线宽展宽（extrinsic linewidth broadening）的微观起源。现有方法难以直接建立缺陷结构与散射机制之间的定量联系，因此需要发展一个能够从微观有效场扰动出发分析双磁子散射的理论框架。

Result: 在YIG/GGG(111)和YIG/GGG(110)薄膜中，有效场扰动在特定的晶体学方向上呈现各向异性分布，这种分布特征与晶体对称性直接相关，进而决定了双磁子散射的强度与方向依赖性。

Conclusion: 该利普曼-施温格框架成功将晶体学结构（crystallography）、样品尺度的有效场与双磁子散射过程联系起来，为理解磁损耗的外在线宽展宽提供了一种微观物理图像和定量分析工具。

Abstract: Controlling magnetic losses requires identifying the microscopic origin of extrinsic linewidth broadening. We introduce a Lippmann--Schwinger framework for two-magnon scattering from weak defect potentials, showing that scattering is set by the overlap between the perturbation of the effective field and degenerate spin-wave states. Applied to iron garnet films, YIG/GGG(111) and YIG/GGG(110), we identify crystallographic directions along which these perturbation are elongated. This approach provides a link between the crystallography, effective field on the sample scale and two-magnon scattering.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 铁磁体中的自旋依赖输运是高密度自旋电子存储器（spintronic memories）发展的基础，而强自旋-轨道耦合铁磁体中自旋依赖输运表现出显著各向异性，理解其背后的物理机制对于开发新型自旋电子器件至关重要。

Result: 研究发现自旋极化和磁自旋霍尔电导率均表现出显著的各向异性；自旋-轨道耦合被确定为该大各向异性的主要驱动力。此外，拉伸应变（tensile strain）能够逐步增强自旋霍尔电导率的振荡幅度（oscillatory amplitude）。

These findings establish strong spin-orbit-coupled ferromagnets as a platform for anisotropic spin-current generation and field-free spintronic devices that exploit intrinsic material anisotropy for improved performance and energy efficiency.
