### [84] [A Degenerate Singlet-Triplet Qubit with All-Electrical Orthogonal Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27067)
*Phuong X. Nguyen,Konstantinos Tsoukalas,Jann H. Ungerer,Julian Santen,Valentin John,Stefan D. Oosterhout,Lucas Stehouwer,Stefano Bosco,Giordano Scappucci,Menno Veldhorst,Amir Yacoby*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本文在锗双量子点中实现了全电压正交控制的简并单重态-三重态（DST）量子比特，通过利用电可调的各向异性g因子同时调零塞曼能差和交换相互作用，实现了保真度达99.53%的正交Z轴和X轴旋转，并展示了磁场取向可调的退相干增强工作点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统单重态-三重态（singlet-triplet）量子比特中，塞曼能差 $\Delta E_\mathrm{Z}$ 由固定磁场梯度或g因子不均匀性决定，无法动态调节，导致量子比特的旋转轴不能正交控制，且在空闲时产生不必要的态旋转。该工作旨在实现两个旋转轴完全正交的全电学控制。

Method: 利用锗（germanium）双量子点中两个空穴（hole）自旋的电可调各向异性g因子，找到同时使塞曼能差 $\Delta E_\mathrm{Z}$ 和交换相互作用 $J$ 为零的参数区域，使 $S$ 和 $T_0$ 态在空闲点简并。仅通过基带电压脉冲独立调控 $J$ 和 $\Delta E_\mathrm{Z}$，实现完全正交的 $Z$ 轴与 $X$ 轴旋转。

Result: 随机基准测试（randomized benchmarking）测得平均单量子比特门保真度为99.53%，门操作时间约100纳秒。简并点在宽范围磁场取向下可电学调谐，从而实现增强相干时间的工作区间。

Conclusion: 该全电学正交控制方案消除了固定塞曼能差带来的限制，为在共享全局磁场下实现多量子比特扩展提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Singlet-triplet qubits offer an attractive encoding for semiconductor quantum computing, combining ancilla-free readout, reduced sensitivity to common-mode noise, and baseband voltage control. However, the Zeeman energy difference $ΔE_\mathrm{Z}$ is typically fixed by local magnetic field gradients or $g$-factor inhomogeneities, leaving the exchange interaction $J$ as the only dynamically tunable parameter. This always-on $ΔE_\mathrm{Z}$ precludes orthogonal control of the qubit's rotation axes and introduces unwanted state rotations during idling. Here we demonstrate all-electrical orthogonal control of a degenerate singlet-triplet (DST) qubit formed by two hole spins in a germanium double quantum dot. Exploiting the electrically tunable anisotropic $g$-factors of the two spins, we identify a regime where both $ΔE_\mathrm{Z}$ and $J$ vanish, making the $S$ and $T_0$ states degenerate at the idle point. By applying only baseband voltage pulses, we independently control both $J$ and $ΔE_\mathrm{Z}$, enabling fully orthogonal $Z$- and $X$-axis rotations. Randomized benchmarking yields an average physical single-qubit gate fidelity of 99.53\% for a gate duration of approximately 100 ns. Finally, we electrically tune the degenerate point across a wide range of magnetic field orientations, enabling operation in a regime of enhanced coherence time and offering a route towards multi-qubit scaling under a shared global magnetic field.

</details>


### [85] [Angular Momentum Transfer in Magnetic Weyl Semimetal Spheres](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27086)
*Adriano Biondo,Francesco M. D. Pellegrino*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 该论文在轴子电动力学（axion electrodynamics）框架下研究了拓扑材料（topological materials）中的电磁角动量问题，计算了磁外尔半金属（magnetic Weyl semimetal）球体在点电荷存在下的电磁角动量，并推导了准静态移动点电荷时电磁角动量向机械角动量转换的解析表达式，表明外尔半金属中的效应相比拓扑绝缘体（topological insulator）有所增强。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究拓扑材料中电磁角动量的行为，特别是其在轴子电动力学框架下的表现形式；分析电磁角动量能否通过准静态过程转化为机械角动量，以及这种转化在磁外尔半金属中是否比拓扑绝缘体更显著。

Method: 基于轴子电动力学理论，对一个磁外尔半金属球体在点电荷作用下的电磁角动量进行计算；通过准静态移动点电荷的方式，分析电磁场中储存的角动量转化为球体机械角动量的过程；推导出最终角速度和角位移的解析表达式。

Result: 获得了磁外尔半金属球体在点电荷存在下电磁角动量的计算结果；推导出了准静态过程中球体获得的角速度与角位移的解析表达式；发现该效应在外尔半金属中相比拓扑绝缘体有所增强。

Conclusion: 磁外尔半金属能够将电磁角动量有效地转换为机械角动量，且转换效果优于拓扑绝缘体，这一结果为拓扑材料在角动量操控和力学响应方面的应用提供了理论依据。

Abstract: In this work, we investigate the problem of electromagnetic angular momentum within the framework of topological materials, described by axion electrodynamics. Specifically, we calculate the electromagnetic angular momentum for a spherical sample of magnetic Weyl semimetal in the presence of a point charge. We then analyze how, by moving the point charge quasi-statically, the angular momentum stored in the electromagnetic field can be converted into mechanical angular momentum of the sphere. Finally, we derive analytical expressions for the resulting angular velocity and angular displacement, demonstrating that the effect is enhanced in the Weyl semimetal compared with a topological insulator.

</details>


### [86] [Inhomogeneous saturation of excitons in monolayer transition-metal dichalcogenides](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27161)
*Blake T. Hipsley,Adam Alfrey,Steven T. Cundiff*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 该论文发现过渡金属二硫族化物（TMD）单层中激子共振的表观非均匀展宽依赖于激发强度，并证明该现象源于激子共振能量不同导致的非均匀饱和效应，这对以激子线宽衡量样品质量具有重要意义。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 二维相干光谱（2DCS）能够分离非均匀展宽（inhomogeneous broadening）与均匀展宽（homogeneous broadening），但激子共振线宽对激发强度的依赖性尚不明确，而这对于正确解释样品质量至关重要。

Method: 利用二维相干光谱测量过渡金属二硫族化物（transition-metal dichalcogenide）单层中激子共振的对角线宽（diagonal linewidth）和交叉对角线宽（cross-diagonal linewidth），通过分析激发通量（fluence）与对角线宽的关系，揭示非均匀饱和（inhomogeneous saturation）机制。

Result: 表观非均匀展宽随激发强度变化；对角线宽的通量依赖性源于有效饱和通量（saturation fluence）随激子共振能量的变化。

Conclusion: 激子线宽中的非均匀展宽依赖于激发强度，这一发现对于正确使用线宽作为样品质量指标至关重要。

Abstract: We observe that the apparent inhomogeneous broadening, as measured by two-dimensional coherent spectroscopy (2DCS), of the exciton resonance in transition-metal dichalcogenide monolayers depends on the excitation strength. A key strength of 2DCS is the ability to separate inhomogeneous broadening, which primarily contributes to the diagonal linewidth, from homogeneous broadening, which dominates the cross-diagonal linewidth. We show that the fluence dependence of the diagonal linewidth arises from the effective saturation fluence varying with the exciton's resonance energy, i.e., inhomogeneous saturation. These results are critical for interpreting the exciton linewidths, which are often used as a measure of sample quality.

</details>


### [87] [Magnetic Breakdown and Anomalous Quantum Oscillation in Rhombohedral Tetralayer Graphene](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27207)
*Jing-Yu Zhao,Yang-Zhi Chou,Sankar Das Sarma*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本文研究电子掺杂菱面体四层石墨烯中范霍夫奇点附近的磁击穿效应，发现异常舒布尼科夫-德哈斯振荡（包括朗道扇环状结构和频谱中的异常高频峰），这些现象源于三个费米口袋之间的磁击穿重构，并在强VHS区域（手性超导出现的区域）依然存在，表明磁击穿诱导的异常量子振荡可作为与VHS相关费米面几何的通用探测工具。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在菱面体四层石墨烯中近期报道了手性超导现象，而该体系中范霍夫奇点附近的磁击穿效应尚未被系统研究。

Method: 基于非相互作用能带结构和久保公式（Kubo formula），计算并分析朗道扇和频率谱中的量子振荡特征。

Result: 发现朗道扇中出现环状结构，频率谱中出现异常高频峰；这些异常特征在强VHS区域（半经典图像失效且手性超导出现的区域）依然保持定性不变。

Conclusion: 磁击穿诱导的异常量子振荡可作为与范霍夫奇点相关的费米面几何的通用探测手段，并可能解释近期菱面体四层石墨烯中的量子振荡实验。

Abstract: We investigate magnetic breakdown near Van Hove singularities (VHSs) in the electron-doped rhombohedral tetralayer graphene, where chiral superconductivity has recently been reported. Using the noninteracting band structure and Kubo formula, we identify anomalous Shubnikov-de Haas effects: Ring-like structures in the Landau fan and anomalous high-frequency peaks in the frequency spectra. These anomalous quantum oscillations can be understood by the reconstruction from magnetic breakdown among three nearby Fermi pockets separated by VHSs. Remarkably, these qualitative anomalous features persist into a stronger-VHS regime, where the semiclassical picture breaks down, and chiral superconductivity emerges. The temperature and (weak) disorder dependence of the oscillations are also investigated. Our results establish that the magnetic-breakdown-induced anomalous quantum oscillation provides a general distinctive probe for the underlying Fermi-surface geometry associated with VHSs and may explain the recent quantum oscillation experiment in rhombohedral tetralayer graphene [arXiv:2606.05356].

</details>

### [88] [Boundary-Driven Anisotropic Coarsening in Conserved Phase Separation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26920)
*Emilio N. M. Cirillo,Nicklas Jävergård,Adrian Muntean,Stela Andrea Muntean*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本研究通过三元混合物的表面蒸发模型，证明了仅凭边界驱动（boundary forcing）即可打破具有守恒动力学（conserved dynamics）体系中相分离（phase separation）的各向同性标度对称性，导致平行和垂直于边界方向出现不同的各向异性粗化（anisotropic coarsening）生长规律。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统观点认为，在守恒动力学体系中，相分离的普适标度（universal scaling）是各向同性的。本研究旨在检验边界条件本身是否足以打破这一动力学标度对称性，从而影响非平衡相分离的粗化行为。

Method: 采用一种具有两个守恒组分和一个被动组分的三元混合物（ternary mixture）体系，并引入表面蒸发（surface evaporation）过程，以此作为研究边界驱动效应的简化模型。通过分析平行和垂直于边界方向的生长行为差异，并与标准Model B标度行为进行对比，揭示各向异性的来源。

Result: 蒸发导致质量持续损失并形成宏观浓度梯度（macroscopic concentration gradients），进而驱动各向异性粗化：平行与垂直于边界方向表现出不同的有效生长指数。然而，体相（bulk regions）仍保持标准的Model B标度行为，表明所观察到的各向异性主要源于边界通量（boundary fluxes），而非内在动力学机制的变化。

Conclusion: 边界条件在打破标度对称性方面具有重要作用，这为调控非平衡相分离过程中的粗化行为提供了新的可能途径。

Abstract: Universal scaling in phase separation is typically assumed to be isotropic in systems with conserved dynamics. Here we show that boundary forcing alone can break this dynamical scaling symmetry, leading to anisotropic coarsening, with different effective global growth laws observed parallel and perpendicular to the boundary. We consider a ternary mixture with two conserved components and a passive species undergoing surface evaporation, which provides a simple setting to investigate this effect. In this case, evaporation leads to a progressive mass loss and to the formation of macroscopic concentration gradients, which, in turn, drive anisotropic coarsening, with different effective growth laws observed parallel and perpendicular to the boundary. At the same time, bulk regions appear to retain the standard Model B scaling, suggesting that the observed anisotropy is mainly induced by boundary fluxes rather than by changes in the intrinsic dynamics. Our results indicate that boundary conditions can play an important role in breaking scaling symmetry and may offer a way to influence coarsening behavior in nonequilibrium phase separation.

</details>


### [89] [Neural variational framework for random Young-diagram limit shapes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27061)
*Qian Chen,Bo-Xuan Ge*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文提出一种保持结构的神经变分框架（structure-preserving neural variational framework），用于随机Young图系综（random Young-diagram ensembles），并在多种系综（Plancherel、均匀、最小差、固定q的q-Plancherel）上验证，进而在无解析鞍面假设的情形下研究四次形变钩长效应的系综（quartically deformed hook-length ensemble），数值结果支持形变依赖的宏观鞍面族（macroscopic saddle family）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法对随机Young图系综的分析大多依赖已知解析渐近行为，缺乏一种能够自动适配系综结构与标度、无需先验解析鞍面假设的通用数值框架。

Method: 构建一个保持结构的神经变分框架，其表示（representation）适配每个系综的结构与标度；训练时仅使用系综定义，渐近轮廓仅用于训练后验证；对四次形变系综，将大n神经轮廓与基于精确作用搜索的有限尺寸最大后验（MAP）轮廓及基于角转移Metropolis-Hastings采样的均值轮廓进行比较。

Result: 在所验证的系综上，方法有效；对于四次形变系综，增大形变（deformation）会抑制前几行（leading rows）并拓宽支撑（support）；神经轮廓、离散轮廓与采样均值轮廓之间的一致性达到百分位水平。

Conclusion: 这些结果提供了数值证据，表明存在一个依赖于形变参数的宏观鞍面族（deformation-dependent macroscopic saddle family）。

Abstract: We develop a structure-preserving neural variational framework for random Young-diagram ensembles, with representations adapted to the structure and scaling of each measure. The method is validated on the Plancherel, uniform, minimal-difference, and fixed-\(q\) \(q\)-Plancherel ensembles, using known asymptotic profiles only for post-training comparison. We then study a quartically deformed hook-length ensemble without assuming an analytical saddle shape. Large-\(n\) neural profiles are compared with finite-size MAP profiles obtained from exact-action searches and with mean profiles obtained from corner-transfer Metropolis--Hastings sampling. Increasing the deformation suppresses the leading rows and broadens the support, while the neural, discrete, and sampled mean profiles agree at the percent level. These results provide numerical evidence for a deformation-dependent macroscopic saddle family.

</details>


### [90] [Non-Hermitian Random Matrix Theory of Jamming in Active Disordered Media](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26406)
*Hisao Hayakawa*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文基于非厄米随机矩阵理论（non-Hermitian random matrix theory），为活性堵塞体系（active jammed systems）建立了力学理论框架。通过将线性化动力学矩阵（linearized dynamical matrix）视为对描述被动接触网络的Wishart系综（Wishart ensemble）的非厄米微扰，推导了低频解的态自洽方程，并展示了活性非互易性（active non-reciprocity）如何规整化堵塞转变（jamming transition）处的软模发散（soft-mode divergence），提出了力学柔度（mechanical compliance）的标度律（scaling law）以及活性堵塞点附近的标度形式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 活性堵塞体系（如生物组织、活性颗粒材料）的力学行为无法用传统的厄米平衡态理论（Hermitian equilibrium theory）描述，亟需一种能够处理非互易相互作用（non-reciprocal interactions）与堵塞转变耦合的理论框架。

Method: 从具有非互易相互作用的活性粒子微观模型出发，将线性化动力学矩阵构建为被动接触网络对应的Wishart系综上的非厄米微扰。运用Girko厄米化方法（Girko's Hermitization）结合自洽玻恩近似（self-consistent Born approximation），基于完整的Marchenko–Pastur分布（Marchenko–Pastur distribution）推导了低频解态的态自洽方程。

Result: 活性非互易性能够规整化堵塞转变处原本发散的软模，从而导出力学柔度的标度律。进一步建立了微扰主导区与活性主导区之间的交叉行为（crossover），并提出了活性堵塞点附近的相应标度形式。

Conclusion: 该框架从微观非互易相互作用出发，通过非厄米随机矩阵理论统一描述了活性堵塞体系的力学响应，揭示了活性非互易性如何改变堵塞转变的临界行为。

Abstract: We develop a theoretical framework for the mechanics of active jammed systems based on non-Hermitian random matrix theory. Starting from a microscopic model of active particles with non-reciprocal interactions, we formulate the linearized dynamical matrix as a non-Hermitian perturbation of a Wishart ensemble describing the passive contact network. Using Girko's Hermitization together with the self-consistent Born approximation, we derive a self-consistent equation for the low-frequency resolvent based on the full Marchenko--Pastur distribution. We show that active non-reciprocity regularizes the soft-mode divergence at the jamming transition, leading to a scaling law for the mechanical compliance. We further establish a crossover between perturbative and activity-dominated regimes and propose a corresponding scaling form near the active jamming point.

</details>


### [91] [Interface evolution in the two-dimensional quantum Ising model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26662)
*Georgios Kampanis,Marianna Sorba,Gesualdo Delfino*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文研究了二维量子伊辛模型（quantum Ising model）在自发对称性破缺（spontaneously broken symmetry） regime 中界面的幺正时间演化（unitary time evolution）。通过利用体理论（bulk theory）的渐近准粒子态（asymptotic quasiparticle states）基，在粗糙相（rough phase）中解析确定了序参量（order parameter）在大时间下的行为，并揭示了远离临界点时该相如何崩溃的机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解二维量子伊辛模型中，由初始条件诱导的界面在自发对称性破缺下的晚期时间动力学，特别是探索该动力学对初始条件的依赖关系。

Method: 通过设置一个在两个简并基态（degenerate ground states）之间插值的初始条件来诱导界面，并利用体理论的渐近准粒子态基在大时间下解析求解序参量。

Result: 在粗糙相中，序参量在大时间下被解析确定；且随着远离临界点（criticality）距离的增加，粗糙相崩溃的机制从理论中自然呈现。

Conclusion: 该研究揭示了二维量子伊辛模型中界面动力学的关键特征，特别是初始条件对晚期动力学的影响以及粗糙相稳定性的临界行为。

Abstract: We consider the unitary time evolution of an interface in the regime of spontaneously broken symmetry of the two-dimensional quantum Ising model. The interface is induced by an initial condition interpolating between the two degenerate ground states in one of the spatial dimensions. The interpolation is left generic in order to investigate the dependence of the late time dynamics on the initial condition. Exploiting the basis of asymptotic quasiparticle states of the bulk theory, the order parameter is analytically determined at large times in the rough phase. The mechanism allowing the breakdown of this phase as the distance from criticality increases emerges from the theory.

</details>


### [92] [Quantum Brownian transport in a correlated Gaussian force](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26678)
*Yun Jeong Kang,Seungsik Min,Sung Kyu Seo,Kyungsik Kim*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文研究了一个经典系统-浴模型（system-bath model），其中系统粒子与谐振子浴（harmonic oscillator bath）线性耦合。在白噪声和相关高斯噪声（correlated Gaussian noise）作用下，推导了联合概率密度表达式并计算了系统-浴粒子的均方值。发现浴粒子的均方位移和均方速度在相关高斯噪声下呈现t^5和t^3的超扩散增长（superspreading growths），这种反常输运现象源于主方程（master equation）的混合导数结构。结果表明，在Caldeira-Leggett框架中去除耗散会导致根本不同的、以非扩散性量子扩散（non-diffusive quantum diffusion）为特征的输运机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索经典系统-浴模型在噪声驱动下的反常扩散行为，特别是在相关高斯噪声条件下系统-浴粒子的动力学性质。

Method: 采用经典系统-浴模型，其中系统粒子与谐振子浴线性耦合；对白噪声和相关高斯噪声两种情形，推导联合概率密度（joint probability density）表达式，并计算系统-浴粒子的均方值（mean squared values）。

Result: 白噪声下系统-浴粒子的均方值呈现与正常扩散不同的反常时间依赖关系。相关高斯噪声下，浴粒子的均方位移（mean squared displacement）和均方速度（mean squared velocity）在t≪τ时分别呈现t^5和t^3的超扩散增长。当τ=0时，量子粒子在随机噪声下的均方速度正比于t，而浴粒子在相关高斯噪声下的均方速度正比于t^2。

Conclusion: 反常输运现象来源于主方程（master equation）的混合导数结构（mixed derivative structure），该结构在相对坐标的扩散动力学中与输运坐标耦合。在Caldeira-Leggett框架中去除耗散会导致根本不同的输运机制，其特征为非扩散性量子扩散（non-diffusive quantum diffusion）。

Abstract: We study a classical system-bath model in which a system particle is linearly coupled to a bath of harmonic oscillators. In a system subject to white and correlated Gaussian noises, we derive an expression for the joint probability density, and the mean squared values of the system-bath particles are calculated. For white noise, the mean squared values of the system-bath particles exhibit an anomalous time dependence, which is different from that of normal diffusion. In particular, for a correlated Gaussian noise, the mean squared displacement and mean squared velocity of the bath particle show superspreading growths of $t^5$ and $t^3$ in $t{\ll}τ$, respectively. When $τ=0$, the mean squared velocity of a quantum particle under random noise is proportional to $t$, while the mean squared velocity of a bath particle in the presence of correlated Gaussian noise increases in proportion to $t^2$. This anomalous transport phenomenon results from the mixed derivative structure of the master equation, which couples with the transport coordinates in the diffusion dynamics of the relative coordinates. This result shows that the removal of dissipation in the Caldeira-Leggett framework leads to fundamentally different transport mechanisms characterized by non-diffusive quantum diffusion.

</details>


### [93] [The Role of Odd Diffusivity in Multipoint Statistics of State-Dependent Observables](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26824)
*Jong-Min Park*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文证明，在时间反演和宇称对称性破缺的系统中，奇数扩散率（odd diffusivity）的反对称部分对任意多时点状态可观测量（state-dependent observables）的联合统计量无贡献。Lorentz通量不影响任何多点状态可观测量，奇数扩散系统中的反常关联完全源于奇数迁移率（odd mobility），这使得相关统计量可用传统朗之万动力学（Langevin dynamics）计算，且广义格林-久保关系（Green–Kubo relations）等普适关系无需修改仍成立。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 厘清奇数扩散率中反对称部分（即Lorentz通量）对状态可观测量统计行为的具体作用，区分奇数迁移率与奇数扩散率在涨落-耗散关系（fluctuation–dissipation relations）中的独立角色，解决两者常被混为一谈的问题。

Method: 基于理论分析与推导，证明任意多时点状态可观测量联合统计量对扩散张量反对称部分的不依赖性；通过平均回弛豫（mean back relaxation）、扩散系数、非线性涨落-耗散关系（nonlinear fluctuation–dissipation relation）及广义格林-久保关系在多种系统中的验证加以支持。

Result: 奇数扩散系统中，状态可观测量的反常关联仅由奇数迁移率引起，Lorentz通量对多点统计无贡献；因此可用仅保留反对称迁移率张量而无反对称扩散部分的传统朗之万动力学计算相关统计量。广义格林-久保关系和非线性涨落-耗散关系等普适关系在奇数扩散系统中无需修改仍然成立。

Conclusion: 奇数扩散率的反对称部分不参与状态可观测量的多时点统计，这一发现简化了奇数扩散系统的理论处理，并确保了与之相关的多种普适关系的广泛适用性。

Abstract: Odd diffusivity is a transverse transport coefficient that appears when time-reversal and parity symmetries are broken. Its most distinctive signature is a probability flux perpendicular to a density gradient, generated by the antisymmetric part of the diffusion tensor. Here we show that for an arbitrary number of state-dependent observables measured at arbitrary times, their joint statistics are independent of the antisymmetric part of the diffusion tensor. Thus, the Lorentz flux does not contribute to any multipoint state-observable measurements. The result clarifies the separate roles of two effects that are often linked together by fluctuation--dissipation relations, i.e., odd mobility and odd diffusivity. This observation demonstrates that the anomalous correlations in odd-diffusive systems originate solely from odd mobility. It also allows the statistics of state-dependent observables in odd-diffusive systems to be computed using conventional Langevin dynamics without the antisymmetric diffusion part while preserving the antisymmetric mobility tensor. It implies that universal relations associated with state-dependent observables alone, such as nonlinear fluctuation--dissipation relations and generalized Green--Kubo relations, remain valid in odd-diffusive systems without modification. We verify our findings through mean back relaxation, the diffusion coefficient, the nonlinear fluctuation--dissipation relation, and a generalized Green--Kubo relation in diverse systems.

</details>


### [94] [Temporal Interference from Topological Transitions in Monitored Quantum Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27045)
*Qingyuan Wang,Ruoyu Yin,Eli Barkai*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文研究了通过频闪监测（stroboscopic monitoring）检测量子系统中时间干涉模式（temporal interference patterns）的方法，重点分析了拓扑不变量——缠绕数（winding number）发生 $w \to w-2$ 跃迁时出现的两个暗态（dark states），以及伴随的极慢振荡衰减现象，并与 $w \to w-1$ 跃迁的单调指数衰减进行了对比。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 此前研究表明，平均首次检测复现时间（mean detected recurrence time）由缠绕数 $w$ 这一拓扑不变量整数量化给出。当测量周期与系统时间尺度发生共振时，缠绕数会突变。本文旨在深入分析 $w \to w-2$ 这一特定跃迁的物理机制及其可观测特征。

Method: 论文采用频闪监测方案，将测量的反作用（back action）与酉动力学（unitary dynamics）统一处理。通过分析希尔伯特空间（Hilbert space）中两个暗态的涌现及其对应模式对干涉图案的贡献，推导系统对称性如何导致振荡行为，并给出最优观测条件。

Result: 在 $w \to w-2$ 跃迁附近，首次检测振幅呈现极慢的衰减，并叠加了振荡模式；这不同于 $w \to w-1$ 跃迁附近单调的指数衰减行为。振荡来源于系统的对称性。

Conclusion: 明确了两种不同拓扑跃迁（$w \to w-2$ 与 $w \to w-1$）在首次检测动力学上的本质区别，并给出了实验观测此类振荡现象所需的条件。

Abstract: Temporal interference patterns can be detected with stroboscopic monitoring that treats the back action of measurements and the unitary dynamics. Previous work established that the mean detected recurrence time is integer-quantized and given by a topological invariant, a winding number $w$. When measurement periods are at resonance with the system's timescales, the winding number can abruptly change. We focus on a generic quantum system and the transition $w\to w-2$, signified by the creation of two dark states in Hilbert space, whose corresponding modes are responsible for the interference pattern. Close to the transition an extremely slow decay of the amplitude of first detection is found, superimposed by oscillations, in contrast to the monotonically exponential decay close to the case $w\to w-1$. We show how these oscillations are obtained from the symmetry of the system and find the conditions for optimal observations of the phenomenon.

</details>

### [95] [Gaps in unconventional superconductors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26251)
*Andreas Kreisel*

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

TL;DR: 本文综述了非常规超导体（unconventional superconductors）中能隙（energy gap）的概念基础，解释了其与常规超导体的差异、能隙的来源以及在可实验测量量中的表现形式，旨在为理解当前开放研究问题和矛盾性文献提供基础。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非常规超导体通常表现出非均匀的能隙（non-uniform gapping），并产生备受关注的物理效应，但近期文献中常出现矛盾性结论，因此有必要梳理相关概念基础。

Method: 本文采用综述（review）的形式，系统概述了区分非常规与常规超导体的核心概念，包括能隙的来源及其在可实验测量量中的表现。

Result: 能隙是超导体的定义性特征之一，源于金属系统进入超导态后准粒子激发（quasiparticle excitations）的根本性变化；非常规超导体的非均匀能隙导致了丰富的物理效应。

Conclusion: 该综述为理解非常规超导体的基础概念提供了框架，有助于研究者把握当前开放研究问题并解读近期文献中相互矛盾的结论。

Abstract: The energy gap is one of the defining properties of a superconductor and appears because the quasiparticle excitations radically change once a metallic system enters the superconducting state. Unconventional superconductors typically exhibit a non-uniform gapping and therefore show physical effects that are subject to intense research. In this review, we provide an overview of concepts needed to understand how unconventional superconductors are different from conventional ones, where the gapping comes from and how it expresses itself in experimentally accessible quantities. This should give the basis to understand current open research questions and navigate the recent literature that tend to contain contradictory conclusions.

</details>


### [96] [DC Josephson transport in a three-terminal Yu-Shiba-Rusinov system](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26568)
*Subrata Chakraborty*

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

TL;DR: 本文研究了基于Yu-Shiba-Rusinov（YSR）态的三端约瑟夫森结中的非互易超流输运，证明了通过场调控和对称性破缺可实现原子尺度的非互易超导输运。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扫描隧道显微镜（scanning tunneling microscopy, STM）已证实YSR态是研究磁-超导相互作用和相位相干量子输运的多功能平台，但尚未探索其在多端约瑟夫森结中实现非互易超流输运的可能性。

Method: 构建了一个包含三个BCS超导体终端（`A'、`B'和`C'）的三端约瑟夫森结，每个终端耦合一个承载一对YSR态的磁杂质；将终端A和C作为主输运电极，终端B作为相位控制节点，研究场可调的非互易超流。

Result: 在存在控制终端B且至少一个与主电极耦合的杂质具有破缺的粒子-空穴对称性（broken particle-hole symmetry）时，观察到场可调的非互易超流（nonreciprocal supercurrent）；若无控制终端，超流保持互易（reciprocal）。

Conclusion: 多端YSR结为在原子尺度上工程化非互易超导输运和对称性破缺现象提供了有前景的平台。

Abstract: Scanning tunneling microscopy (STM) of magnetic adatoms on superconducting surfaces has established Yu-Shiba-Rusinov (YSR) states as a versatile platform for studying magnetic-superconducting interactions and phase-coherent quantum transport. Here, we investigate nonreciprocal supercurrent transport in a three-terminal Josephson junction comprising three BCS superconductors (`A', `B' and `C'), each coupled to a magnetic impurity hosting a pair of YSR states. Treating terminals `A' and `C' as primary transport electrodes and terminal `B' as a phase-control node, we demonstrate field-tunable nonreciprocal supercurrent between the primary electrodes. This effect requires both: the control terminal and broken particle-hole symmetry at least in one impurity attached to a primary electrode. Without the control terminal we show that the supercurrent remains reciprocal. Our results establish multi-terminal YSR junctions as a promising platform for engineering nonreciprocal superconducting transport and symmetry-breaking phenomena at the atomic scale.

</details>


### [97] [Electronically Inactive Intercalated La$_2$NiO$_4$ Layer in Superconducting La$_5$Ni$_3$O$_{11}$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26676)
*Tianyang Xie,Yuxin Wang,Zhan Wang,Kun Jiang,Jiangping Hu*

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

TL;DR: 本文通过密度泛函理论、紧束缚模型和旋转不变奴隶玻色子计算，发现La₅Ni₃O₁₁中的插层La₂NiO₄层处于能隙绝缘态而非顺磁金属态，低能电子结构主要由La₃Ni₂O₇块体主导，插层在电子学上不活跃，从而建立了一个简化的低能描述框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: La₅Ni₃O₁₁作为超导Ruddlesden–Popper镍酸盐家族中的新成员，包含插层的La₂NiO₄层，该层是否参与低能电子结构尚未明确，需要探究其电子角色。

Method: 结合密度泛函理论（density functional theory, DFT）、基于Wannier函数的紧束缚模型（tight-binding modeling）以及旋转不变奴隶玻色子方法（rotationally invariant slave-boson, RISB）进行计算分析。

Result: 实际电子参数使La₂NiO₄层处于能隙绝缘态，而非顺磁金属态；实际的层间杂化无法在费米能级（Fermi level）处产生可观测的La₂NiO₄衍生的谱权重。

Conclusion: La₅Ni₃O₁₁的低能电子结构主要由La₃Ni₂O₇块体决定，插层La₂NiO₄层在电子学上不活跃，这为理解插层Ruddlesden–Popper镍酸盐中的超导性提供了统一框架。

Abstract: The recent discovery of superconductivity in La$_5$Ni$_3$O$_{11}$ extends the family of superconducting Ruddlesden--Popper nickelates beyond La$_3$Ni$_2$O$_7$. Unlike conventional members of a single Ruddlesden--Popper series, La$_5$Ni$_3$O$_{11}$ contains an intercalated La$_2$NiO$_4$ layer between La$_3$Ni$_2$O$_7$ blocks, raising the question of whether this additional layer participates in the low-energy electronic structure. Here, we combine density functional theory, Wannier-based tight-binding modeling, and rotationally invariant slave-boson calculations to investigate the electronic role of the intercalated layer. We find that realistic electronic parameters place the La$_2$NiO$_4$ layer in gapped insulating regimes rather than a paramagnetic metallic state. Furthermore, realistic interlayer hybridization fails to generate any appreciable La$_2$NiO$_4$-derived spectral weight at the Fermi level. Our results demonstrate that the low-energy electronic structure of La$_5$Ni$_3$O$_{11}$ is governed primarily by the La$_3$Ni$_2$O$_7$ block, with the intercalated La$_2$NiO$_4$ layer remaining electronically inactive. This establishes a minimal low-energy description of La$_5$Ni$_3$O$_{11}$ and provides a unified framework for understanding superconductivity in intercalated Ruddlesden--Popper nickelates.

</details>


### [98] [Density-wave phases, anisotropic transport, and Planckian dissipation in single crystals of the superconductor La3Ni2O7](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26990)
*Zhehong Liu,Masamichi Nakajima,Markus Kriener,Shunsuke Kitou,Xiaowei Lyu,Chieko Terakura,Kosuke Karube,Ka Man Yip,Sorin Lazar,Nobuto Nakanishi,Keiko Shimada,Akiko Kikkawa,Yukako Fujishiro,Xiuzhen Yu,Taka-hisa Arima,Yoshinori Tokura,Yasujiro Taguchi*

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

TL;DR: 本文通过测量高质量La₃Ni₂O₇单晶的面内和面外电阻率，建立了高精度的压力-温度相图，发现压力诱导的结构转变增强了电阻率及其各向异性，在密度波相被完全抑制的边界附近出现了零电阻超导电性，且超导转变温度以上的电阻率在宽温区内呈温度线性依赖，散射率落在普朗克极限（Planckian limit）范围内，表明超导电性源自密度波关联和普朗克耗散主导的奇异金属态（strange-metal state）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 双层镍酸盐（bilayer nickelates）中的压力诱导超导电性为研究电荷/自旋序与电输运在非常规超导（unconventional superconductivity）中的交织作用提供了平台，但由于缺乏大尺寸单晶的精确测量，电阻率绝对值、各向异性及载流子散射率等关键定量信息仍不充分。

Method: 通过测量高质量La₃Ni₂O₇单晶的面内（in-plane）和面外（out-of-plane）电阻率，建立了高精度的压力-温度相图（pressure-temperature phase diagram），并分析了与密度波形成相关的两个不同异常及其压力依赖性的对比。

Result: 压力诱导的结构转变同时增强了两个方向的电阻率及其各向异性（anisotropy）；零电阻超导电性出现在密度波相被完全抑制的边界附近；超导转变温度以上，电阻率在宽温区内呈现温度线性依赖（temperature-linear dependence），且散射率落在普朗克极限范围内。

Conclusion: 压力通过修改密度波序（density-wave orders）显著改变各向异性电荷输运，最终产生一个具有强散射的奇异金属态，超导电性从中发展；密度波关联和普朗克耗散（Planckian dissipation）被确立为La₃Ni₂O₇的显著特征。

Abstract: Pressure-induced superconductivity in bilayer nickelates provides a platform for investigating intertwined roles of charge/spin orders and electric transport in unconventional superconductivity. However, important quantitative information on the transport, such as the absolute value of the resistivity, the anisotropy, and the scattering rate of carriers, remains insufficient due to the lack of accurate measurements using large single crystals. Here we establish a high-precision pressure-temperature phase diagram of high-quality La3Ni2O7 single crystals, by measuring the in-plane and out-of-plane resistivities. We resolve two distinct anomalies associated with density-wave formation with contrasting pressure dependences. The pressure-induced structural transition enhances not only the resistivity values for both directions, but also its anisotropy at low temperatures, demonstrating a pronounced effect of density-wave order on the charge dynamics. Superconductivity with zero-resistance emerges near the boundary where the density-wave phases are fully suppressed, and above Tc, the resistivity exhibits a temperature-linear dependence over a wide temperature range while the scattering rate falls within a regime of the Planckian limit. Our results show that pressure dramatically changes the anisotropic charge transport via modifying density-wave orders, and eventually produces a pronounced strange-metal state with strong scatterings, from which superconductivity develops. This establishes robust density-wave correlations and Planckian dissipation as remarkable features of La3Ni2O7.

</details>


### [99] [Dual Enhancement of Superconductivity in FeSe/SrTiO3 via Orbital and Correlation Synergy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27104)
*Guihao Jia,Jingming Yan,Yucong Peng,Shendong Su,Pei Ouyang,Xiaopeng Hu,Qi-Kun Xue,Wei Li*

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

TL;DR: 本文通过单层FeSe/SrTiO3体系，展示了针尖诱导拉伸应变可控地将dz2轨道带移至费米能级，驱动超导电性的两阶段增强，并揭示了轨道选择性配对机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在铁基超导体中，dz2轨道带通常远离费米能级（Fermi level），被认为不参与库珀配对（Cooper pairing）。本研究旨在探索通过应变调控活化该轨道带，从而增强超导电性的可能性。

Method: 以单层FeSe/SrTiO3为模型系统，利用扫描隧道显微镜（STM）针尖施加可控的拉伸应变（tensile strain），结合谱学测量追踪dz2轨道带的位置移动及超导能隙（superconducting gap）的变化。

Result: 应变首先通过面内晶格膨胀增强电子关联（electronic correlation），实现第一阶段超导增强；随后dz2带与dxy带杂化（hybridization），重构配对活性d轨道带，引发第二阶段更强的能隙增强。超导能隙从17.8 meV增大至23.6 meV，且费米波矢（Fermi wave vector）不变，排除载流子掺杂效应。

Conclusion: 本工作建立了通过应变激活电子关联与能带工程（band engineering）调控超导态的路径，揭示了此前未被认识的轨道选择性配对机制（orbital-selective pairing mechanism），对关联多带超导体（correlated multiband superconductors）具有广泛意义。

Abstract: In iron-based superconductors, the dz2 orbital band typically resides far below the Fermi level and has not been considered to participate in Cooper pairing. Here, using monolayer FeSe/SrTiO3 as a model system, we demonstrate that tip-induced tensile strain controllably shifts the dz2 band toward the Fermi level, driving a two-stage enhancement of superconductivity. In-plane lattice expansion first enhances electronic correlation, amplifying superconductivity in the initial stage. As strain further increases, the upward-shifted dz2 band hybridizes with the dxy band, reconstructing the pairing-active d-orbital bands and inducing a secondary, stronger gap enhancement. Collectively, these two stages enlarge the superconducting gap from 17.8 to 23.6 meV. Throughout this process, invariant Fermi wave vectors confirm that the enhancement originates from band renormalization and reconstruction rather than carrier doping. Our work establishes a route to tailor superconducting states via strain-activated electronic correlations and band engineering, and reveals a previously unrecognized orbital-selective pairing mechanism with broad implications for correlated multiband superconductors.

</details>


### [100] [Momentum Structure of Superconductivity and Sublattice Effects from Quasiparticle Interference in CsV$_3$Sb$_5$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27148)
*Aaron G. Greenberg,Xinze Yang,Junze Deng,Pranab Kumar Nag,Kirsty Scott,Yi Jiang,Haoyu Hu,Chandra Shekhar,Dong Chen,Claudia Felser,Santiago Blanco-Canosa,Päivi Törmä,B. Andrei Bernevig,Eduardo H. da Silva Neto*

TL;DR: 本文利用亚开尔文扫描隧道显微镜对kagome材料CsV₃Sb₅进行高分辨率能谱成像，通过准粒子干涉（QPI）分析揭示各向同性超导能隙，排除了部分配对密度波（PDW）调制的存在，并展示了费米面上子晶格纹理的谱学敏感性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: kagome晶格中布洛赫波函数（Bloch wavefunctions）的子晶格纹理所编码的量子干涉被广泛认为是关联态（correlated states）——包括手征电荷序、非常规超导及其在配对密度波（pair-density-wave, PDW）态中的可能交织——的关键机制。然而其低能电子结构的实验图像仍不够清晰。

Method: 采用亚开尔文（sub-Kelvin）扫描隧道显微镜（scanning tunneling microscopy, STM），对kagome材料CsV₃Sb₅进行高能量分辨率和密集能量采样的超导能谱映射。结合第一性原理（ab initio）和对称性计算，对谱学数据进行准粒子干涉（quasiparticle interference, QPI）分析。

Result: QPI分析揭示，在源自V原子M_z-偶（M_z⁺）d轨道的费米面上存在各向同性的超导能隙（isotropic superconducting gap），从而限制了可能的能隙对称性，并将能隙各向异性约束在剩余的V原子M_z-奇（M_z⁻）和Sb p_z能带上。CDW峰选dI/dV谱与空间平均态密度紧密吻合，未在亚能隙能量范围内出现显著增强，因此不支持在灵敏度范围内存在额外的PDW调制。此外，特定QPI散射矢量的选择性缺失表明谱学对费米面上的子晶格特征具有敏感性。

Conclusion: 这些结果为CsV₃Sb₅中与kagome超导电性相关的低能电子结构提供了更清晰的实验图像，约束了超导能隙对称性，并排除了显著PDW调制的存在。

Abstract: Quantum interference encoded in the sublattice texture of kagome Bloch wavefunctions has been widely invoked as a route to correlated states, including chiral charge order, unconventional superconductivity, and their possible intertwining in pair-density-wave (PDW) states. Using sub-Kelvin scanning tunneling microscopy, we conducted spectroscopic mapping of the kagome material CsV$_3$Sb$_5$ with high energy resolution and dense energy sampling through the superconducting gap. Quasiparticle interference (QPI) analysis, aided by ab initio and symmetry calculations, reveals an isotropic superconducting gap on the Fermi surfaces derived from V $M_z$-even ($M_z^+$) $d$ orbitals, thereby constraining possible gap symmetries and limiting any gap anisotropy to the remaining V $M_z$-odd ($M_z^-$) and Sb $p_z$ bands. Meanwhile, the CDW-peak-selected d$I$/d$V$ spectra closely track the spatially averaged density of states and show no distinct enhancement restricted to subgap energies, which do not support an additional PDW modulation within our sensitivity. Finally, the selective absence of specific QPI scattering vectors points to a spectroscopic sensitivity to sublattice character on the Fermi surface. Together, these results provide a clearer experimental picture of the low-energy electronic structure relevant to kagome superconductivity in CsV$_3$Sb$_5$.

</details>

### [101] [Disentangling intermittent flow structure contributions to anomalous scaling and multifractality in turbulence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26896)
*Ritwik Mukherjee,Siddhartha Mukherjee*

Main category: physics.flu-dyn

TL;DR: 本文通过阈值涡量滤波与Biot-Savart反演方法，分离湍流间歇性结构对能量谱、结构函数标度及多分形特性的影响，证明该方法可选择性地移除间歇性效应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 湍流间歇性（turbulence intermittency）引发Kolmogorov自相似理论的失效，导致反常标度（anomalous scaling）与多分形（multifractality）行为，但其流结构如何定量影响不同测量指标尚不明确。

Method: 采用阈值化涡量滤波与Biot-Savart反演生成滤波速度场，通过逐步滤除极端涡量贡献，分离间歇性结构对湍流测量的影响。

Result: 滤除间歇性后，能量谱标度保持而瓶颈区平坦化，结构函数标度趋向Kolmogorov值；横向指数更快恢复，涡旋拉伸与应变自放大效应被量化；间歇性滤除减小了粗糙度奇异指数（roughness singularity exponent）范围，而残余场呈现更强的多分形性。

Conclusion: Biot-Savart方法能选择性地移除湍流间歇性效应及其标度影响，为理解间歇性结构对湍流统计物理的作用提供量化工具。

Abstract: Intermittency in turbulence manifests as intense vortices and sharp peaks of dissipation. Causing the breakdown of Kolmogorov's simple self-similar theory, it leads to anomalous scaling, multifractality and so far remains beyond the scope of a complete theoretical description. How intermittent flow structures influence these different measurements is not known quantitatively. With a simple filtering procedure-thresholding vorticity and inverting the Biot-Savart law to generate filtered velocity fields-we show the effects of intermittent flow structures can be disentangled. As extreme vorticity contributions to the velocity field are filtered out, the energy spectrum scaling persists, while the bottleneck is flattened, and structure function scalings tend towards their Kolmogorov values. The approach is more rapid for transverse exponents, revealing the selective importance of intensely swirling flow regions. Similarly, the extent of multifractality reduces as intermittency is filtered, shrinking the range of roughness singularity exponents. The residual fields are curiously more multifractal, but their structure begins to break away from an underlying turbulence skeleton. The effects on vortex stretching and strain self-amplification are quantified. Our work shows that a Biot-Savart approach can selectively remove the effects of intermittency from turbulence, and hence from its scalings.

</details>


<div id='physics.plasm-ph'></div>

### [102] [Divertor topology and vacuum vessel design for stellarators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27127)
*Andrew Giuliani,Raffael Wendlinger,Misha Padidar,Robert Davies,Shibabrat Naik,Calvin Lowe,Georg Harrer*

Main category: physics.plasm-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了用于在真空场中设计边缘磁结构的仿星器优化算法，联合优化模块化线圈与真空容器，首次实现了仿星器中的精确雪花偏滤器，并生成了多种准轴对称仿星器设计方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 仿星器（stellarator）的边缘磁结构设计对偏滤器（divertor）和真空容器（vacuum vessel）的耦合至关重要，但现有方法缺乏鲁棒且高效的联合优化框架。

Method: 提出了鲁棒计算任意类型（椭圆、双曲、抛物）周期轨道不动点的数值方法；引入参数化真空容器族以高效计算点与容器间的距离及其导数；利用有符号距离函数（signed distance function）约束线圈与容器间隙，允许线圈放置在容器上或之外；联合优化模块化线圈和真空容器。

Result: 实现了多种排热磁拓扑结构，包括标准X点偏滤器、单零及双零位形；首次在仿星器中实现了精确的雪花偏滤器（snowflake divertor）；生成了多个具有兼容真空容器和不同偏滤器架构的准轴对称仿星器设计。

Conclusion: 所提出的框架为下一代STAR Lite原型提供了候选设计方案，展示了仿星器偏滤器设计的多样性和可行性。

Abstract: We present stellarator optimization algorithms for designing the edge magnetic structure in vacuum fields, together with the vacuum vessel. First, we introduce a numerical method that robustly computes periodic-orbit fixed points of any type (elliptic, hyperbolic, or parabolic), which could form the basis of a divertor. To couple divertor and vacuum vessel design, we introduce parametric families of vacuum vessels for which point-to-vessel distances, and their derivatives, can be computed efficiently. The resulting algorithms use signed distance functions to enforce coil-vessel clearance while allowing coils to be placed on or off the vessel. Using these methods, we jointly optimize modular coils and the vacuum vessel to realize a wide range of magnetic topologies for diverting exhaust, including standard X-point divertors, and single- and double-null configurations. For the first time, we show that precise snowflake divertors can be achieved in stellarators. Using this framework, we generate a number of quasi-axisymmetric stellarator designs with compatible vacuum vessels and diverse divertor architectures, which we consider to be candidates for a next-generation STAR Lite prototype.

</details>

### [103] [Dynamical phase retrieval for Schr{ö}dinger evolution on finite graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26705)
*Philippe Jaming,Azita Mayeli*

Main category: math.CA

TL;DR: 本文研究了有限连通图上薛定谔演化（Schrödinger evolution）的动力学相位恢复（dynamical phase retrieval）问题，给出了无相位数据唯一确定初始态（模去全局相位）的判据，证明了该判据在几乎所有势能下成立，并讨论了唯一性失效的障碍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在相位恢复问题中，仅能获取波函数的模长（phaseless data）而非完整复数信息，这在量子力学、光学等领域有重要意义。本文旨在解决有限连通图上薛定谔演化中，由无相位数据恢复初始态（initial state）的唯一性问题，并给出普适性的充分条件。

Method: 利用图薛定谔算子（graph Schrödinger operator）H_Q = Δ_G + Q 的谱分解，提出基于谱的 B₂ 条件（B₂ condition）、平方特征向量矩阵（squared-eigenvector matrix）的可逆性以及特征向量支撑集的重叠条件（overlap condition）作为唯一性判据。通过构造显式势能和测度论论证，证明该判据在有限连通图上既是可实现也是具普遍性的（generic）。

Result: 在上述三个假设下，无相位薛定谔演化数据（模长信息）能唯一确定每个初始态，仅差一个全局相位（global phase）。每个有限连通图都存在一个显式的实对角势能使得该判据成立；此外，对于勒贝格几乎处处（Lebesgue-almost every）的实势能 Q 和任意 T>0，动力学相位恢复均成立。同时给出了若干唯一性失效的障碍（obstructions）。

Conclusion: 本文建立了有限连通图上动力学相位恢复的完整理论框架，给出了既充分又具普适性的唯一性判据，并阐明了判据的成立条件与失效场景。

Abstract: We study dynamical phase retrieval for Schr\''odinger evolutions on finite connected graphs. Let \[ H\_Q=Δ\_G+Q \] be a graph Schr\''odinger operator with a real diagonal potential. We investigate when phaseless data obtained from the associated Schr\''odinger evolution \[ |e^{-itH\_Q}u\_0(j)|, \qquad 0\leq t\leq T,\ j\in V, \] determines the initial state $u\_0\in\C^V$ up to a global phase. We give a uniqueness criterion in terms of the eigenvalues and eigenvectors of $H\_Q$. The assumptions are a $B\_2$ condition on the spectrum, meaning that the sums $λ\_j+λ\_k$ determine the unordered pair $\{j,k\}$, invertibility of the squared-eigenvector matrix $\bigl(φ\_k(j)^2\bigr)\_{j,k}$ and an overlap condition on the supports of pairs of eigenvectors. Under these hypotheses, the phaseless Schr\''odinger data determine every initial state uniquely, modulo global phase. We then show that the criterion is both realized and generic. Every finite connected graph admits an explicit real diagonal potential for which the criterion holds. Moreover, for every finite connected graph, dynamical phase retrieval holds for Lebesgue-almost every real potential $Q\in\R^V$ and every $T>0$. We also give several obstructions to uniqueness.

</details>

### [104] [Topological control of local electroneutrality in confined electrolytes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26282)
*Marcelo Lozada-Cassou*

Main category: cond-mat.soft

TL;DR: 本文基于泊松-玻尔兹曼理论（Poisson-Boltzmann theory），证明拓扑结构决定了受限电解质（confined electrolytes）中局部电中性（local electroneutrality）的有限尺寸偏离，并揭示了一种普适的拓扑层级：球腔偏离最强，柱腔次之，平板狭缝最弱。该结果将拓扑确立为受限诱导电荷重分布的组织原则，并报告了空心圆柱和球状纳米颗粒中存在内部受限电荷反转（ICCR）这一反直觉现象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解受限电解质中局部电中性违反（violations of local electroneutrality）的物理起源，特别是有限尺寸效应（finite-size effects）与空间拓扑（topology）之间的内在联系。

Method: 基于泊松-玻尔兹曼理论（Poisson-Boltzmann theory）进行理论分析，引入电中性偏离比（electroneutrality deviation ratio）来量化与三种拓扑类别（平板狭缝、圆柱腔、球腔）相关的全局静电约束。

Result: 有限尺寸偏离遵循稳健的拓扑层级：球腔中最强，圆柱腔中次之，平板狭缝中最弱。在带电空心圆柱和球状纳米颗粒中发现了内部受限电荷反转（ICCR）。

Conclusion: 拓扑是受限诱导电荷重分布的组织原则，改变拓扑可在不改变局部泊松-玻尔兹曼方程（local Poisson-Boltzmann equations）的情况下改变全局静电约束。该结果建立了超越泊松-玻尔兹曼描述评估拓扑效应的基准。

Abstract: Topology governs finite-size violations of local electroneutrality in confined electrolytes. Within Poisson-Boltzmann theory, we show that this topological control gives rise to a universal hierarchy of deviations in spherical, cylindrical, and planar confinement. We quantify this effect through an electroneutrality deviation ratio that captures the global electrostatic constraints associated with compactness and boundary multiplicity in the three topological classes corresponding to slit, cylindrical, and spherical cavities. Although local electroneutrality is asymptotically restored in the limit of infinite cavity size, finite-size deviations follow a robust topological hierarchy, being strongest in spherical cavities, weaker in cylindrical confinement, and weakest in planar slits. These results prove that topology is the organizing principle underlying confinement-induced charge redistribution and that violations of local electroneutrality constitute a general electrostatic constraint governing overcharging, charge reversal, and long-range charge correlations. More fundamentally, they demonstrate that changing the topology modifies the global electrostatic constraints without altering the local Poisson-Boltzmann equations. As a counterintuitive manifestation of nonlinear confinement in a point-ion model, we report the existence of inside confinement charge reversal (ICCR) in charged hollow cylindrical and spherical nanoparticles. Since physically consistent, more general electrolyte theories must recover the Poisson-Boltzmann description in the appropriate limiting case, the present results establish a benchmark against which topological effects should be assessed beyond the Poisson-Boltzmann description.

</details>


### [105] [Scale-dependent universality class crossover in magnetic skyrmion polymers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26289)
*R. L. Silva,R. C. Silva,R. L. Stamps*

Main category: cond-mat.soft

TL;DR: 该论文研究偶极磁性斯格明子（dipolar magnetic skyrmions）链的统计力学（statistical mechanics），发现其违反生物聚合物（如肌动蛋白actin、DNA、微管microtubules）中普遍存在的谐波标度（harmonic scaling）行为。通过第一性原理计算得到双指数（bi-exponential）形式的相互作用势，多尺度模拟揭示出温度依赖的幂律行为及磁场的无关性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 偶极磁性斯格明子可组装成具有交替螺旋性（alternating helicity）的链状结构，其统计力学行为与已知生物聚合物（如肌动蛋白、DNA、微管）的通用谐波标度（harmonic scaling）存在显著偏离，这一反常现象亟需理论解释。

Method: 通过第一性原理（first principles）计算斯格明子间的相互作用势，得到双指数（bi-exponential）形式；结合多尺度模拟（multiscale simulations）分析不同键数极限下的温度依赖行为。

Result: 相互作用势呈现双指数形式，包含两个特征衰减长度（characteristic decay lengths），分别对应排斥与吸引的微观机制。在单键（单键）的蠕虫链极限（worm-chain limit）下，温度依赖的幂律指数为1；在三键极限下，指数为1/2。该幂律行为对磁场强度（magnetic field strength）不敏感，且与相互作用势的具体形式（如Morse势或双Yukawa势）无关。

Conclusion: 斯格明子链的异常统计力学行为源于径向相互作用的竞争（competing radial interactions）导致的四次横向约束（quartic transverse confinement），而非相互作用势的精确形式；这一发现揭示了磁性斯格明子聚合物与生物聚合物标度行为的本质差异。

Abstract: Dipolar magnetic skyrmions can assemble into chains with alternating helicity that act as one-dimensional polymers, yet their statistical mechanics violates the universal harmonic scaling observed in actin, DNA, and microtubules. From first principles, we compute the inter-skyrmion pair potential and find a bi-exponential form of competing interactions with two characteristic decay lengths that encode the distinct microscopic mechanisms of repulsion and attraction. Multiscale simulations reveal a power-law temperature dependence with exponent $1$ in the worm-chain limit of a single bond, and exponent $1/2$ in the three-bond limit. We find that the power-law behavior is remarkably independent of magnetic field strength, and the crossover is due to competing radial interactions responsible for the bonds, resulting in a quartic transverse confinement. We show that the precise form of the competing interactions (e.g., Morse or double-Yukawa) does not affect the temperature dependence.

</details>


### [106] [Mode-Resolved Light Scattering Recovers Polymer Thermodynamics in Solutions with Trace Large-Mass Scatterers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26576)
*Noriaki Mizumoto,Takashi Yasuda,Xiang Li*

Main category: cond-mat.soft

TL;DR: 本文提出一种通过分子迁移率（molecular mobility）分辨静态散射强度的方法，将聚合物信号与不可避免的大质量散射体（如聚集体、纳米气泡）贡献分离。在聚乙二醇（PEG）水溶液中验证，即使大散射体占总强度90%以上，仍可提取纯净聚合物散射信号，从稀溶液到半稀溶液（288–358 K）恢复普适量渗透状态方程，为热力学测量建立可靠基础。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 光散射（light scattering）可直接探测聚合物构象与热力学性质，但聚合物溶液中不可避免地存在痕量大质量散射体（如聚集体和纳米气泡），其散射强度远高于聚合物本身信号，掩盖了来自聚合物的本征散射信息，阻碍了定量分析。

Method: 通过分子迁移率对静态散射强度进行模式分辨分析（mode-resolved analysis），利用聚合物与大质量散射体在分子运动性上的差异，将二者的散射贡献分离。

Result: 该方法应用于聚乙二醇（polyethylene glycol, PEG）水溶液，即使大质量散射体占总散射强度的90%以上，仍能成功分离出聚合物散射信号。分离后的聚合物散射强度在288 K至358 K的宽温度范围内，从稀溶液到半稀溶液（dilute to semidilute regime）均恢复出普适量渗透状态方程（universal osmotic equation of state）。

Conclusion: 该分辨分析方法为测量相互作用大分子溶液的热力学性质建立了可靠基础，尤其适用于因不可重复的大质量散射体而此前无法进行定量分析的体系。

Abstract: Light scattering provides direct access to polymer conformations and thermodynamics. However, trace large-mass scatterers such as aggregates and nanobubbles form unavoidably in polymer solutions and dominate the scattered intensity, obscuring the intrinsic polymer signal. We demonstrate that resolving the static scattering intensity by molecular mobility cleanly separates the polymer and large-scatterer contributions. Applying this mode-resolved analysis to aqueous poly(ethylene glycol) solutions, we isolate the polymer scattering even when these scatterers account for more than 90 % of the total intensity. The resolved polymer intensity recovers the universal osmotic equation of state from the dilute to the semidilute regime over 288 to 358 K. This approach establishes a reliable basis for measuring the thermodynamics of interacting macromolecules in solutions where irreproducible large-mass scatterers have precluded quantitative analysis.

</details>


### [107] [Squirming motion near corrugated surfaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26875)
*Sagnik Garai,Thomas G. Fai,Christina Kurzthaler*

Main category: cond-mat.soft

TL;DR: 本文通过微扰方法研究了周期性边界下方微观游泳者（squirmers）的动力学行为，发现拉拽者（pullers）会陷入表面波纹谷中，而推动者（pushers）则表现出与表面周期相对应的振荡动力学；此外，游泳者相对于表面波纹的倾斜会导致依赖波长的漂移，从而区分推动者与拉拽者。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 微观游泳者（microswimmers）常在复杂受限环境中运动，其中长程流体动力学相互作用（hydrodynamic interactions）与短程排斥相互作用的耦合可产生有趣的动力学行为。现有研究多关注平面壁附近的游泳行为，而对周期性边界如何改变游泳速度并导致与平面壁定性不同的行为尚缺乏系统理解。

Method: 采用基于双球坐标（bispherical coordinates）和洛伦兹互易定理（Lorentz reciprocal theorem）的微扰方法（perturbative approach），在小振幅极限下表征游泳体（squirmer）与周期性表面之间的相互作用，系统探索了边界波纹波长、游泳体类型、取向及游泳体-表面距离等参数的影响。

Result: 拉拽者（pullers）会陷入表面波纹的谷中，与平面壁附近的滑动行为形成对比；推动者（pushers）的近表面动力学呈现反映表面周期性的振荡行为；游泳者取向相对于表面波纹倾斜时，会产生依赖波长的漂移，从而将推动者与拉拽者分离开来。

Conclusion: 这些发现突显了流体动力学相互作用在塑造微观游泳者于结构化边界附近输运行为中的作用，对生物膜形成（biofilm formation）等微生物学现象及技术应用具有潜在意义。

Abstract: Swimming microorganisms often operate in complex confinement, where an interplay of long-ranged hydrodynamic interactions and a short-ranged repulsive interaction can give rise to interesting dynamical behaviors. Here, we theoretically investigate the trajectories of microswimmers - modeled as squirmers - in the presence of periodic boundaries. The latter modify their swimming velocity, leading to behaviors that differ qualitatively from swimming near planar walls. Using a perturbative approach based on bispherical coordinates and the Lorentz reciprocal theorem, we characterize the interaction between a squirmer and a periodic surface in the limit of small surface amplitude and systematically explore its dependence on the boundary corrugation wavelength, squirmer type, orientation, and swimmer-surface distance. Most importantly, our results reveal that pullers become trapped in the valley of the surface corrugations, in contrast to their sliding motion near planar walls. Furthermore, the near-surface dynamics of pushers display oscillations, reflecting the periodicity of the surface structure. A tilt of the swimmer orientation with respect to the surface corrugations results in a wave-length dependent drift that sorts pushers from pullers. These findings highlight the impact of hydrodynamic interactions in shaping microswimmer transport near structured boundaries with potential implications for microbiological phenomena, such as biofilm formation, and technological applications.

</details>


### [108] [Instability-induced bistable shape-morphing kirigami structures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26941)
*Xiaoyuan Ying,Marcelo A. Dias*

Main category: cond-mat.soft

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种各向异性双稳态剪纸结构（anisotropic bistable kirigami structures）的逆设计框架，通过几何约束（geometric frustration）和失稳诱导变形（instability-induced deployment）实现可编程形状变构，并建立了几何变换与能量景观（energy landscape）之间的半解析力学模型，通过有限元模拟和实验验证了稳定构型与各向异性变形的可编程性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于剪纸（kirigami）的变形系统主要依赖各向同性展开、柔性材料或外部约束来维持展开形状，这限制了几何可编程性、结构完整性以及在刚性材料系统中的适用性。

Method: 采用半解析力学模型（semi-analytical mechanical model）与几何相结合的方法，建立几何变换与能量景观之间的直接联系，实现各向异性双稳态剪纸结构的逆设计。

Result: 失稳诱导的形状变形在剪纸结构中产生了可调的双稳态（tunable bistability）和定向展开（directional deployment），并通过有限元模拟和实验验证了稳定的展开构型和可编程的各向异性变形。

Conclusion: 该框架提供了一种通用的设计策略，可与多种主动驱动系统（active actuation systems）集成，拓展了在软体机器人（soft robotics）、生物医学器件、自适应建筑和航空航天等领域的工程应用。

Abstract: Deployable shape-morphing structures that transform from flat sheets into stable three-dimensional configurations are highly desirable for applications ranging from soft robotics and biomedical devices to adaptive architecture and aerospace systems. Existing kirigami-based morphing systems primarily rely on isotropic deployment, compliant soft materials, or external constraints to maintain deployed shapes, which limits geometric programmability, structural integrity, and applicability in rigid-material systems. Here, we present an inverse design framework for anisotropic bistable kirigami structures that enables programmable shape morphing through controlled geometric frustration and instability-induced deployment. The framework combines a semi-analytical mechanical model with geometry to establish a direct connection between geometric transformation and the underlying energy landscape. We show that instability-induced shape morphing leads to tunable bistability and directional deployment in anisotropic kirigami structures. The results are validated through finite element simulations and experiments, demonstrating stable deployed configurations and programmable anisotropic morphing. The proposed framework further provides a general design strategy that can be integrated with various active actuation systems, enabling broader engineering applications.

</details>


### [109] [Twist-driven helical flattening in nematic elastomer cylinders](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27079)
*Alexia Chatzitheodorou,Christian D. Santangelo*

Main category: cond-mat.soft

TL;DR: 本文针对液晶弹性体（liquid crystal elastomers, LCEs）中复杂几何与复杂指向矢（director）结构的耦合问题，提出了一种非欧几里得板理论（non-Euclidean plate theory），揭示了贯穿厚度扭曲指向矢与中面曲率之间的反常耦合，并预测了圆柱结构在长波螺旋展平模式下的失稳行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究主要关注平坦薄板上的指向矢图案或简单曲面上的简单图案，尚未探索非平凡几何与非平凡指向矢构型的耦合情况。然而许多生物系统同时具有复杂几何与复杂螺旋纤维结构，因此需要理解指向矢与底曲几何（underlying geometry）之间的相互作用。

Method: 采用针对向列弹性体（nematic elastomers）的非欧几里得板理论，引入贯穿厚度方向具有扭曲的指向矢（through-thickness twisted director），构建了一个有效二维模型。

Result: 在固定扭曲角（twist angle）下，模型发现中面曲率（mid-surface curvature）与指向矢扭曲之间存在反常耦合（anomalous coupling）。该耦合项源于取向序（orientational order）与弹性（elasticity）的相互作用，并主导传统的弯曲贡献。以具有贯穿厚度扭曲的圆柱几何为例，发现圆柱对长波长螺旋展平模式（long-wavelength helical flattening mode）不稳定，并确定了失稳的临界参数。

Conclusion: 研究表明，指向矢扭曲与几何曲率之间存在强耦合效应，这一机制可用于理解和设计具有复杂几何与复杂指向矢构型的可编程形变材料。

Abstract: Liquid Crystal elastomers (LCEs) deform anisotropically along a prescribed nematic director field, making them promising candidates for programmable shape change. Existing studies have primarily focused on either director patterns on flat sheets or simple patterns on curved geometries, leaving the case of a non-trivial director on a non-trivial geometry still largely unexplored. Many biological systems, however, have both complex geometries and complex helical fiber architectures. To explore the interplay between director and underlying geometry, we use a non-Euclidean plate theory for nematic elastomers with a through-thickness twisted director to develop an effective 2D model. With a fixed twist angle, our model shows an anomalous coupling between mid-surface curvature and director twist. This emergent term arises from the interplay between orientational order and elasticity, and dominates traditional bending contributions. To illustrate the general theory, we study the stability of cylindrical shapes with through-thickness twist. We find that the cylinder is unstable to a long-wavelength helical flattening mode and determine the critical parameter for the onset of instabilities.

</details>


<div id='hep-ph'></div>

# hep-ph [[Back]](#toc)

### [110] [SET-ANUBIS: a modular pipeline for ANUBIS long-lived particle sensitivity studies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26112)
*Sofie Nordahl Erner,Anna Mullin,Théo Reymermier,Paul Swallow,Oleg Brandt for the ANUBIS Collaboration*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: SET-ANUBIS 是一个开源的 Python 框架，用于 ANUBIS 探测器上长寿命粒子（long-lived particle）信号的端到端模拟、接收效率与灵敏度研究。它采用端口-适配器架构，集成从模型参数解析、分支比与寿命计算、事件生成（Pythia8/MadGraph）到几何建模、选择链筛选的完整流程，并配备可复现性测试套件与科学仪表盘。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长寿命粒子信号研究需要一套端到端（end-to-end）的模拟与灵敏度分析工具链，而现有框架在模型参数暴露、事件生成、几何建模与选择链的集成上缺乏统一的、可复现且可审计的开源方案。ANUBIS 探测器作为 ATLAS 洞穴中拟议的附加探测器，其灵敏度研究亟需一个模块化、可配置且支持直接模型对比的框架。

Method: 采用端口-适配器架构（ports-and-adapters architecture）将物理域逻辑与外部生成器、持久化存储和可视化分离。从 Universal FeynRules Output 模型或用户提供的事例率出发，暴露模型参数与粒子内容，计算衰变宽度、分支比与寿命，通过 Pythia8 或 MadGraph 执行或准备事件生成，导入 HepMC 事件记录，建模 ATLAS 洞穴与 ANUBIS 跟踪站，并应用可配置的几何、运动学与隔离条件序列。

Result: v1.0.0 版本引入确定性可复现性测试套件，包含五个独立场景（R1–R5），覆盖模型访问、分支比插值、Pythia 指令生成、MadGraph 卡片生成及完整选择链。最终场景输出机器可读的 JSON 和独立 HTML 选择追溯报告，记录累计切割流程与事件级通过/未通过转换。可选的科学仪表盘支持事件、几何、运行批次与来源审查，且不改变物理结果。

Conclusion: SET-ANUBIS 提供了一个可审计、可复现且可扩展的开源工具链，用于 ANUBIS 探测器的长寿命粒子灵敏度研究，支持不同长寿命粒子模型的直接对比。框架、文档与参考输入均以 GNU GPL 第三版或更高版本发布。

Abstract: SET-ANUBIS (Simulation, accEptance and sensiTivity studies framework for ANUBIS) is an open-source Python framework for end-to-end studies of long-lived particle signatures in the proposed ANUBIS detector. Starting from a Universal FeynRules Output model or user-supplied rates, it exposes model parameters and particle content, evaluates decay widths, branching ratios and lifetimes, prepares or runs event generation through Pythia8 or MadGraph, ingests HepMC event records, models the ATLAS cavern and ANUBIS tracking stations, and applies a configurable sequence of geometric, kinematic and isolation requirements. The implementation follows a ports-and-adapters architecture so that physics-domain logic remains separate from external generators, persistent storage and visualisation. A scan-aware SQLite catalogue and content-addressed store preserve cards, metadata and compact selection-ready event bundles while avoiding duplicate artefacts.
  Release v1.0.0 introduces a deterministic reproducibility suite with five independent scenarios (R1--R5) covering model access, branching-ratio interpolation, Pythia command generation, MadGraph card generation and the complete selection chain on a compact HepMC2 sample. The final scenario produces machine-readable JSON and a standalone HTML selection trace that records cumulative cutflows and event-level pass/fail transitions. Optional scientific dashboards provide event, geometry, campaign and provenance inspection without changing the physics results. The framework, documentation and reference inputs are distributed under the GNU GPL version 3 or later and are intended to support auditable ANUBIS sensitivity studies and direct comparison of alternative long-lived particle models.

</details>


### [111] [Automated NRQCD and NRQED simulations of quarkonium and leptonium production with P-wave states and physical-mass effects](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26739)
*Luca Maxia,Hua-Sheng Shao,Lukas Simon*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文介绍了 MadSONS 模块，该模块将 MadGraph5_aMC@NLO 框架扩展至任意含非相对论性束缚态（non-relativistic bound states）的树级产生过程的全自动模拟，重点实现了 P 波（P-wave）夸克偶素（quarkonia）与轻子偶素（leptonia）的产生，并通过 J/ψ+ψ(2S) 联合产生过程展示了其对 LHCb 数据的改进描述。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在先期工作（arXiv:2510.26773）中仅实现了 S 波（S-wave）态的色与自旋投影算符，本工作旨在将其推广至 P 波态，实现对含轨道角动量（orbital angular momentum）的非相对论性束缚态产生过程的自动化模拟。

Method: 采用对偶数（dual numbers）数值计算轨道角动量投影所需的导数，并引入动量重排（momentum-reshuffling）过程以计入束缚态质量效应对相空间（phase space）的影响。

Result: 构建的框架可兼容主流多用途蒙特卡洛（Monte Carlo）事件生成器，适用于多种对撞机环境；在 J/ψ+ψ(2S) 产生过程的应用中，获得了对 LHCb 数据的改进描述。

Conclusion: MadSONS 模块完成了对任意含束缚态树级产生过程的全自动模拟支持，为非相对论性 QCD（NRQCD）与 QED 中的重夸克偶素和轻子偶素研究提供了通用工具。

Abstract: We present the $\texttt{MadSONS}$ module, which extends the $\texttt{MadGraph5_aMC@NLO}$ framework by providing a fully automated solution for the simulation of arbitrary tree-level production processes involving non-relativistic bound states. More specifically, we focus on the production of quarkonia and leptonia in non-relativistic QCD and QED, respectively. This work constitutes the next step in the programme initiated in arXiv:2510.26773, where the colour and spin projectors required for S-wave states were first incorporated into $\texttt{MadGraph5_aMC@NLO}$. In the present development, we implement the remaining projectors associated with orbital and total angular momentum, thereby enabling the event generation of processes involving P-wave states. The derivatives required for the orbital-angular-momentum projection are evaluated using dual numbers. In addition, we implement a momentum-reshuffling procedure that accounts for physical bound-state mass effects in the phase space. The resulting framework applies to a wide range of collider environments while remaining compatible with standard multi-purpose Monte Carlo event generators for parton showering and hadronisation. As a first phenomenological application, we revisit $J/ψ+ ψ(2{\rm S})$ production at the LHC and obtain an improved description of the LHCb data.

</details>


### [112] [Constraining $α$-cluster compactness in $^{16}\rm O$ and $^{20}\rm Ne$ at TeV energies using azimuthal anisotropy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26758)
*Aswathy Menon Kavumpadikkal Radhakrishnan,Suraj Prasad,Neelkamal Mallick,Raghunath Sahoo,Gergely Gábor Barnaföldi*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本研究通过IP-Glasma+MUSIC+iSS+UrQMD混合框架模拟超相对论性轻离子（¹⁶O和²⁰Ne）对撞，发现末态椭圆流（elliptic flow）系数v₂对原子核的α团簇（α-clustered）几何构型高度敏感，尤其体现在OO碰撞中不同构型导致的中心度依赖性和v₂峰值位置的差异。通过系统变化团簇大小和间距，可确定与LHC实验数据最佳匹配的核结构参数，证明TeV能量下轻离子碰撞中的流观测量可用于约束轻核的核结构参数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 超相对论性轻离子碰撞中的各向异性流（anisotropic flow）对碰撞核的初始几何形态敏感。本研究旨在检验椭圆流测量能否约束¹⁶O和²⁰Ne中提出的α团簇核密度分布参数，从而利用流观测量反推轻核的内部结构。

Method: 采用混合框架IP-Glasma+MUSIC+iSS+UrQMD，在质心能量√s_NN=5.36 TeV下模拟OO和Ne–Ne碰撞。分别采用Woods–Saxon分布和α团簇构型（团簇紧密度可变）描述核密度分布，并在ALICE、CMS和ATLAS探测器运动学接受度内计算椭圆流系数v₂{2,|Δη|>1}。

Result: 末态椭圆流对原子核几何形态高度敏感，尤其在OO碰撞中，不同α团簇构型导致v₂的中心度依赖性和峰值位置发生显著变化。通过系统变化¹⁶O和²⁰Ne的团簇大小与团簇间距，可确定与LHC Run 3实验数据最佳吻合的团簇参数范围。

Conclusion: TeV能量轻离子碰撞中的流观测量可用于优化轻核的核结构参数，表明高能重离子碰撞实验能作为约束轻核内部团簇结构的有效工具。

Abstract: Anisotropic flow in ultra-relativistic light-ion collisions is sensitive to the initial geometry of the colliding nuclei. We investigate whether elliptic flow measurements can constrain the parameters of the proposed $α$-clustered nuclear density distributions of $^{16}$O and $^{20}$Ne at LHC energies. Using the hybrid framework IP-Glasma+MUSIC+iSS+UrQMD, we simulate OO and Ne--Ne collisions at $\sqrt{s_{\mathrm{NN}}}=5.36$ TeV for the Woods--Saxon and $α$-clustered configurations with varying cluster compactness. The elliptic flow coefficient $v_2\{2,|Δη|>1\}$ is calculated in the kinematic acceptances of ALICE, CMS, and ATLAS detectors and is compared with the Run~3 OO and Ne--Ne experimental measurements. It is observed that the final-state elliptic flow is significantly sensitive to the nuclear geometry, especially in OO collisions, where different configurations lead to distinct centrality dependencies and peak positions of $v_{2}$. By performing a systematic variation of the cluster size and inter-cluster separation in $^{16}$O and $^{20}$Ne nuclei, this work attempts to identify the cluster parameter range that provides the best agreement with the experimental data. These results show that the flow observables in TeV-energy light-ion collisions can be used to optimize the nuclear structure parameters of light nuclei.

</details>


### [113] [Anomalies in Hadronic $B \to VV$ Decays](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27202)
*Bhubanjyoti Bhattacharya,Marianne Bouchard,Alexandre Jean,David London,Ipsita Ray*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文在味SU(3)对称性假设下对无粲B→VV衰变（B∈{B⁰, B⁺, B_s⁰}, V∈{ρ, K*, φ, ω}）进行了最新数据的整体拟合分析，发现与标准模型在SU(3)_F极限下的预测存在显著偏差：在仅考虑ρ和K*末态时偏差达5.2σ，加入φ和ω后偏差增至>7σ。该结果具有群论上的严格性，且约30%的SU(3)_F破缺效应似乎不足以解释这一矛盾。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近期在无粲B→PP衰变（P∈{π, K, η, η'}）的SU(3)_F拟合中发现了与标准模型在SU(3)_F极限（SM_{SU(3)_F}）之间4.1σ的偏差。本文将此分析方法推广到无粲B→VV衰变，旨在检验在矢量介子末态中是否同样存在显著的SU(3)_F对称性破坏迹象。

Method: 在味SU(3)对称性（flavour SU(3) symmetry [SU(3)_F]）假设下，对无粲B→VV衰变进行整体拟合。依次引入不同的对称性假设与末态粒子集合：首先仅考虑同位旋对称性（isospin symmetry）分析B→ρK*衰变；然后分别在仅含ρ和K*末态、以及包含ρ, K*, φ, ω全部末态的情形下进行SU(3)_F拟合。理论输入较为有限，结果在群论（group theory）意义上具有严格性，并在SU(3)_F极限下几乎精确成立。

Result: 仅考虑同位旋对称性的B→ρK*拟合结果可接受。在SU(3)_F框架下、末态仅含ρ和K*时，与SM_{SU(3)_F}的偏差为5.2σ。当末态扩展至包含ρ, K*, φ, ω时，偏差增大至>7σ。估计约30%量级的SU(3)_F破缺效应似乎不足以解释这一矛盾，但需要进一步验证。

Conclusion: 无粲B→VV衰变在SU(3)_F极限下与标准模型预测存在显著偏离，且偏离程度（>7σ）远大于此前在B→PP衰变中发现的4.1σ偏差。该结果具有群论上的严格性，对新物理（new physics）或更大规模的SU(3)_F破缺效应提出了检验需求。

Abstract: Recently, a fit of charmless $B\to PP$ decays ($B \in \{B^0, B^+, B_s^0\}$, $P \in \{ π, K, η, η' \}$) to the latest data was performed under the assumption of flavour SU(3) symmetry [SU(3)$_F$]. It was found that there is a $4.1σ$ disagreement with the SU(3)$_F$ limit of the Standard Model [$\rm SM_{SU(3)_F}$]. In this paper, we extend this analysis to charmless $B \to VV$ decays ($V \in \{ρ, K^*, φ, ω\}$). The fit examining $B \to ρK^*$ decays, assuming only isospin symmetry, is found to be acceptable. When we fit to $B \to VV$ decays with $V \in \{ ρ, K^* \}$ within SU(3)$_F$, we find a $5.2σ$ discrepancy with SM$_{SU(3)_F}$. Finally, when $B \to VV$ decays with $V \in \{ ρ, K^*, φ, ω\}$ are considered, the discrepancy grows to $>7σ$. The theoretical input in this analysis is modest, so our results are quite rigorous, group theoretically, and hold almost exactly in the SU(3)$_F$ limit. Although it seems unlikely that the introduction of $\sim 30$% SU(3)$_F$-breaking effects can account for this discrepancy, this must be verified.

</details>


### [114] [An Equilibrating Parton Shower for Jet Quenching and Medium Response](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26143)
*Ismail Soudi,Adam Takacs*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文基于QCD有效动能理论（EKT），展示了在高能极限下EKT如何还原为标准喷注淬灭（jet-quenching）的能量损失与角度展宽演化，同时在热尺度附近提供一致描述。由此推导出一个直接源于EKT的部分子簇射（parton shower）模型，它再现了完整的线性化玻尔兹曼方程（linearized Boltzmann equation），将弹性和非弹性碰撞统一处理，从而将硬部分子能量损失与热平衡化置于同等地位。该簇射模型进一步将EKT拓展至非均匀扰动，连接了喷注演化与介质流体力学响应，包括尾流（wakes）、马赫锥（Mach cones）及多粒子关联。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在弱耦合框架下，夸克-胶子等离子体（QGP）的热化行为与喷注状扰动在介质中的演化可由同一套QCD有效动能理论（EKT）描述。现有研究在高能区域已有成熟模型，但需将高能喷注淬灭描述与热尺度附近的动力学过程统一起来，以建立更完整的理论框架。

Method: 首先证明在高能极限下，EKT可约化为标准的喷注淬灭能量损失与角度展宽演化方程。随后在此基础上，直接从EKT推导出一个部分子簇射模型，该模型再现了完整的线性化玻尔兹曼方程，将弹性（elastic）与非弹性（inelastic）碰撞统一处理，同时涵盖硬部分子能量损失和热平衡化过程。

Result: 所得部分子簇射模型成功将EKT拓展至非均匀扰动情形，能够描述喷注演化与介质流体力学响应之间的耦合，包括尾流、马赫锥及多粒子关联等复杂现象。

Conclusion: 该工作建立了一个从QCD有效动能理论到喷注淬灭与热化的统一理论框架，所推导的部分子簇射模型为研究喷注-介质相互作用提供了更全面的理论工具，弥合了高能极限与热尺度之间的描述差距。

Abstract: In the weak-coupling approach, thermalization of the quark-gluon plasma and the evolution of jet-like perturbations in the medium can be described by the same underlying QCD effective kinetic theory (EKT). In this work, we show how the EKT reduces to the standard energy-loss and angular-broadening evolution of jet-quenching in the high-energy regime, while providing a consistent description near the thermal scales. Building on this, we derive a parton shower directly from EKT that reproduces the full linearized Boltzmann equation, combining elastic and inelastic collisions to capture both hard-parton energy loss and thermal equilibration on an equal footing. This shower lets us extend EKT to inhomogeneous perturbations, connecting jet evolution to the medium's hydrodynamic response, including wakes, Mach cones, and multi-particle correlations.

</details>


### [115] [Particle Production via Rippled Bubble Walls](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26569)
*Ryusuke Jinno,Shota Nakagawa,Yuichiro Nakai,Yaoduo Wang*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了一阶相变（first-order phase transition）中超相对论性厚壁气泡（thick bubble wall）的非热粒子产生机制，发现气泡壁内部的多重波纹结构（ripple）可通过共振动量交换显著增强重粒子的产生，在高波纹数下该新贡献可主导产生率并远超传统通道。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有框架仅考虑了简单气泡壁结构对粒子产生的影响，而真实相变中气泡壁可能具有复杂的内部调制。本研究旨在揭示厚壁气泡的多重波纹结构如何影响非热粒子产生，特别是针对远超相变能量尺度的重粒子。

Method: 基于气泡膨胀粒子产生（bubble-expansion particle production）框架，通过建模具有振荡厚壁轮廓（oscillatory thick-wall profile）的气泡壁，推导了粒子在壁背景中的分裂过程跃迁概率（transition probability），并识别出与壁微结构动量转移相关的新贡献项。

Result: 在传统产生通道之外，识别出一种由与波纹的共振动量交换（resonant momentum exchange）驱动的增强产生模式。当波纹数量足够大时，该新机制可主导粒子产生率，并显著提升质量远大于相变标度（phase-transition scale）的粒子丰度。

Conclusion: 膨胀气泡壁的内部结构在粒子产生中可扮演重要角色，在评估强一阶相变的宇宙学效应时应予以考虑。

Abstract: We investigate non-thermal particle production during first-order phase transitions in the presence of ultra-relativistic thick bubble walls with non-trivial internal structure. Extending the framework of bubble-expansion particle production, we consider bubble walls containing multiple ripples and study how such spatial modulations affect the production of heavy particles coupled to the order parameter field. By modeling an oscillatory thick-wall profile, we derive the transition probability for particle splitting processes in the wall background, and identify a new contribution associated with momentum transfer from the wall microstructure. In addition to the conventional channel, we find an enhanced production mode arising from resonant momentum exchange with the ripples. For sufficiently large numbers of ripples, the new contribution can dominate the production rate and significantly increase the abundance of particles much heavier than the phase-transition scale. Our results demonstrate that the internal structure of expanding bubble walls can play an important role in particle production and should be taken into account when assessing the cosmological implications of strongly first-order phase transitions.

</details>


### [116] [A Geometric CPVSM Framework: Dynamical Quark Mass Spurt with Explicit CP Violation in the Standard Mode](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26681)
*Chilong Lin*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文在标准模型（Standard Model, SM）框架内提出一种自上而下的几何方法，利用无量纲二维比值向量对CKM矩阵进行笛卡尔式参数化，将9个实自由度的复质量平方矩阵精确约化为5个基准参数和2个不变比值坐标，证明了交换子为零条件是四重简并的根源，并指出非交换量子修正的必要性；该几何框架还可统一推广至PMNS轻子矩阵，建立味起源的几何描述。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有CKM矩阵参数化方法（如Wolfenstein参数化）缺乏直观的物理映射，难以清晰分离上型和下型夸克对电弱混合（electroweak mixing）及CP破坏（CP violation）的贡献，且味结构（flavor structure）的几何起源尚不明确。

Method: 构建无量纲2D比值向量$(x,y)$和$(x',y')$，建立类似笛卡尔坐标的CKM矩阵参数化；通过代数约化将复质量平方矩阵$\mathbf{M}^2$的9个实自由度降为5个基线参数，再进一步得到幺正对角化矩阵$U$中的2个不变比值坐标$(x,y)$；推导CKM矩阵的精确解析表达式及Jarlskog不变量（Jarlskog invariant）$J$的闭合形式；分析交换子条件$[\mathbf{M}^2_R, \mathbf{M}^2_I] = 0$与四重简并的关系，并考察临界边界几何下（$xy \to 0$）的夸克质量突变（quark mass spurt）行为。

Result: 获得$|J_0| \approx 3.08 \times 10^{-5}$的稳健数值收敛结果；证明$[\mathbf{M}^2_R, \mathbf{M}^2_I] = 0$是四重简并$|p| = |p'| = |p^*| = |p'^*|$的根本来源，并引发非微扰的Wolfenstein尺度混合，表明非交换量子修正（$[\mathbf{M}^2_R, \mathbf{M}^2_I] \neq 0$）的必要性；在$xy \to 0$的临界几何边界附近，系统发生夸克质量突变，重塑质量层级（mass hierarchy）的同时几何上保持非共线CP破坏，其CP破坏幅度足以解释宇宙重子不对称性（Baryon Asymmetry of the Universe, BAU）。

Conclusion: 通过将上述矢量失配（vector mismatch）范式推广到包含狄拉克中微子（Dirac neutrinos, $\nu_R$）的PMNS轻子矩阵，可利用角度矢量偏移（angular vector offsets）自然地容纳大轻子混合角，从而在夸克和轻子两个扇区建立统一的重整化相位不变量（rephasing-invariant）几何味起源框架。

Abstract: We present a top-down geometric framework within the Standard Model (SM), utilizing dimensionless 2D ratio vectors $(x,y)$ and $(x',y')$ to establish a Cartesian-like parameterization of the Cabibbo-Kobayashi-Maskawa (CKM) matrix. This approach provides an intuitive physical mapping that explicitly disentangles up- and down-type quark sector contributions to electroweak mixing and CP violation. Crucially, we prove an exact algebraic dimensional reduction from the 9 real degrees of freedom of the complex mass-squared matrix $\mathbf{M}^2$ down to 5 baseline parameters, and ultimately to 2 invariant ratio coordinates ($x,y$) in the unitary diagonalization matrix $U$. This baseline yields exact analytical expressions for the CKM matrix and a closed-form Jarlskog invariant $J$, achieving robust numerical convergence to $|J_0| \approx 3.08 \times 10^{-5}$. We demonstrate that the exact commutativity condition $[\mathbf{M}^2_R, \mathbf{M}^2_I] = 0$ is the fundamental origin of an intrinsic fourfold magnitude degeneracy governed by $|p| = |p'| = |p^*| = |p'^*|$, inducing non-perturbative Wolfenstein scale mixing that signals the necessity of non-commuting quantum corrections ($[\mathbf{M}^2_R, \mathbf{M}^2_I] \neq 0$). Remarkably, near critical boundary geometries ($xy \to 0$), the system exhibits a \textit{quark mass spurt} that reshapes the mass hierarchy while geometrically preserving non-collinear CP violation ($xy' - x'y \neq 0$), yielding magnitudes sufficient to account for the cosmological Baryon Asymmetry of the Universe (BAU). Extending this vector mismatch paradigm to the PMNS lepton matrix with Dirac neutrinos ($ν_R$), we naturally accommodate large leptonic mixing angles through angular vector offsets, establishing a unified, rephasing-invariant geometric framework for flavor origins across both quark and lepton sectors.

</details>


### [117] [Outcomes of Grand Unified Symmetry Breaking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27158)
*Harish Hemming,Tanmay Vachaspati,Anja Wachowitz*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文通过数值模拟研究了大统一模型（Grand Unified Models）中对称性破缺的结果，发现偏置畴壁（biased domain walls）与磁单极子（magnetic monopoles）的形成及其相互作用，包括磁单极子在壁上的吸收、畴壁湮灭时产生单极子、带磁荷壁的坍缩（可能产生带磁黑洞）以及偏置畴壁产生的随机引力波背景（stochastic gravitational wave background），为通过宇宙学观测探测大统一时代（grand unification epoch）提供了新途径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索大统一模型（Grand Unified Models）中对称性破缺（symmetry breaking）的动力学结果，特别是畴壁（domain walls）与磁单极子（magnetic monopoles）的形成及其相互作用过程。

Method: 采用数值模拟（numerical simulation）方法研究对称性破缺的演化结果。

Result: 发现了偏置畴壁（biased domain walls）与磁单极子（magnetic monopoles）的形成；观察到壁与单极子的多种相互作用，包括单极子在壁上的吸收、畴壁湮灭时单极子的产生、带磁荷壁的坍缩（在考虑引力后可能产生带磁黑洞），以及偏置畴壁产生的随机引力波背景（stochastic gravitational wave background）。

Conclusion: 这些发现为通过宇宙学可观测信号（cosmological observables）探测大统一时代（grand unification epoch）开辟了新途径。

Abstract: We numerically study the outcome of symmetry breaking motivated by Grand Unified models. Our results show the formation of biased domain walls for a range of parameters together with magnetic monopoles. The interactions of walls and monopoles leads to novel processes such as the absorption of monopoles on walls, production of monopoles when domain walls annihilate, collapse of walls with magnetic charge that can, with gravity taken into account, produce magnetically charged black holes, and production of a stochastic gravitational wave background from biased domain walls. Our findings open new avenues for probing the grand unification epoch by cosmological observables.

</details>
