### [118] [Isolation of spin-valley locked nodal-line fermions in $d$-wave $\mathrm{AV_2X_2O}$ altermagnets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26150)
*Pritesh Srivastava,Rahul Verma,Bahadur Singh*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文通过第一性原理计算和紧束缚模型，在d波交变磁体AV₂X₂O（A = Rb, Cs, K; X = Te, Se, S）中预言了C配对自旋-谷锁定节线费米子，并建立了此类拓扑态的设计原理与材料调控机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索d波交变磁体（d-wave altermagnet）中由晶体对称性稳定的新型拓扑电子态，特别是实现自旋-谷锁定（spin-valley locking）的节线费米子（nodal-line fermions），并建立一般性设计原则。

Method: 结合第一性原理计算（first-principles calculations）与最小紧束缚模型（minimal tight-binding model）分析AV₂X₂O材料体系；利用对称性分析（包括面外镜面对称性M_z）验证节线的拓扑保护；引入层工程（layer engineering）和电子关联（electronic correlations）作为材料调控手段。

Result: 低能电子结构在C₄ₓ配对谷（C₄z-paired valleys）附近同时存在自旋简并（spin-degenerate）和自旋极化（spin-polarized）两类节线（nodal lines）；自旋极化节线由面外镜面对称性M_z保护，且对自旋-轨道耦合（spin-orbit coupling）保持鲁棒；最小模型揭示了这些态的微观起源及隔离设计原理。

Conclusion: 确立了AV₂X₂O材料家族作为实现d波交变磁体中拓扑自旋-谷锁定的通用平台，为相关拓扑电子态的实验探索提供了理论指导。

Abstract: Crystalline symmetries stabilize topological states with distinct electronic properties, while altermagnets exhibit momentum-dependent spin splitting without net magnetization. Here, we combine first-principles calculations with a minimal tight-binding model to realize $C$-paired spin-valley-locked nodal-line fermions in the $d$-wave altermagnet $\mathrm{AV_2X_2O}$ (A = Rb, Cs, or K; X = Te, Se, or S). The low-energy electronic structure hosts coexisting spin-degenerate and spin-polarized nodal lines around $C_{4z}$-paired valleys near the Fermi level. The spin-polarized nodal lines are protected by the out-of-plane mirror symmetry $\mathcal{M}_z$ and remain robust against spin-orbit coupling. The minimal model reveals their microscopic origin and establishes a general design principle for their isolation. Layer engineering and electronic correlations serve as material-specific knobs for realizing these isolated spin-valley-locked nodal lines near the Fermi level. Our results establish the $\mathrm{AV_2X_2O}$ family as a versatile platform for exploring topological spin-valley locking in $d$-wave altermagnets.

</details>


### [119] [Influence of BaTiO_3 nanoparticles on the anisotropy of the dielectric properties of nematic liquid crystal 5CB](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26263)
*Juliya M. Gudenko,Oleksandr S. Pylypchuk,Victor V. Vainberg,Denis O. Stetsenko,Igor A. Gvozdovskyy,Serhii E. Ivanchenko,Eugene A. Eliseev,Vladimir N. Poroshin,Anna N. Morozovska*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本研究实验分析了向列型液晶5CB与铁电BaTiO₃纳米颗粒（平均尺寸24 nm，浓度0–10 wt.%）组成的悬浮液，发现纳米颗粒显著影响介电常数、各向异性及介电损耗；杂质离子在纳米颗粒壳层中积累导致离子-电子屏蔽效应，进而改变介电性质和导电机理，并引起中间相-各向同性相转变温度随浓度呈现非单调变化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索铁电纳米颗粒（BaTiO₃）的掺入如何影响液晶悬浮液（nematic liquid crystal suspension）的介电响应（dielectric response）和电导率（electric conductivity）机制，填补对纳米颗粒浓度与介电性质关系的理解空白。

Method: 实验制备不同浓度（0–10 wt.%）BaTiO₃纳米颗粒（平均尺寸24 nm）分散于向列型液晶5CB中的悬浮液，通过测量介电常数各向异性、介电损耗、电容和电阻等参数，分析温度依赖性及浓度依赖性。

Result: 纳米颗粒显著改变介电常数大小和各向异性及介电损耗；较低温度（中间相）和较高温度（各向同性相）下均观察到介电各向异性的显著温度依赖；中间相-各向同性相转变温度随浓度呈非单调变化；纯液晶及低浓度悬浮液中电容和电阻随温度升高而增大；高浓度下纳米颗粒壳层中空间电荷积累促进离子-电子屏蔽（ionic-electronic screening），强烈影响离子输运。

Conclusion: 高浓度BaTiO₃纳米颗粒通过空间电荷积累形成的离子-电子屏蔽效应，改变了悬浮液的介电性质和导电机理，并导致中间相-各向同性相转变温度随浓度呈现非单调关系。

Abstract: This work is devoted to the mechanisms of dielectric response and electric conductivity of suspensions consisting of the nematic liquid crystal 5CB with different concentrations (from 0 to 10 wt.%) of ferroelectric BaTiO_3 nanoparticles with an average size of 24 nm. We revealed that the incorporation of nanoparticles influences significantly the dielectric permittivity magnitude and anisotropy, as well as dielectric losses of the suspension. A pronounced temperature dependence of the anisotropic dielectric permittivity of the suspensions was found at lower temperatures corresponding to the mesophase state; but it is also present at higher temperatures corresponding to the isophase. The dependence of the mesophase-isophase transition temperature on the concentration of BaTiO_3 nanoparticles appeared nonmonotonic. With increasing temperature, both the capacitance and the electrical resistance of the pure liquid crystal increase, as well as it increases in the suspensions with small concentration of BaTiO_3 nanoparticles. Due to space charge accumulation in the shells of nanoparticles, larger concentrations of BaTiO_3 nanoparticles influence strongly the ionic transport by promoting the formation of ionic-electronic screening. This effect modifies the dielectric properties and conduction mechanisms of the suspension, leading to the nonmonotonic dependence of the mesophase - isophase transition temperature versus the nanoparticle concentration.

</details>


### [120] [Dynamic phase-field model for brittle fracture in grounded glaciers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26274)
*Aarosh Dahal,Umar Khayaz,Ravindra Duddu,Aditya Kumar*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文揭示了在固定自重条件下，使用准静态相场断裂框架模拟冰川断裂时会产生非物理的扩散裂纹带和损伤模式，并提出将断裂后传播视为动态不稳定性，采用动态断裂公式（含惯性项）可获得尖锐、局部的贯穿冰层裂纹。此外，本文还提出了一种新的动态相场断裂模型公式，其中弹性、惯性和重力贡献在断裂区域得到一致性退化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 冰川断裂和崩解是冰量损失和海平面上升的关键因素，但预测性建模仍具挑战。准静态线弹性断裂力学（quasi-static linear elastic fracture mechanics）常用于研究接地冰川断裂，但该工作在模拟中发现准静态脆性断裂模拟在裂纹启动后会产生非物理结果，因此需要更准确的建模方法。

Method: 通过相场断裂框架（phase field fracture framework）进行准静态脆性断裂模拟，发现固定自重下裂纹启动后系统过度驱动；转而采用动态断裂公式（dynamic formulations of fracture），引入惯性效应以改善模拟结果。此外，提出了一种新的动态相场断裂模型，对弹性、惯性和重力贡献在断裂区域进行一致性退化。

Result: 准静态模拟在裂纹启动后出现非物理的扩散裂纹带加厚和弥散损伤模式；动态公式则产生尖锐、局部的裂纹，裂纹可贯穿冰层厚度。

Conclusion: 冰川断裂的裂纹后传播本质上是一种动态不稳定性（dynamic instability）而非准静态过程，因此应采用动态断裂公式进行建模。新提出的动态相场断裂模型能够更准确地模拟冰层断裂行为。

Abstract: Fracture and calving of glaciers are key contributors to ice-mass loss and sea-level rise, yet predictive modeling remains challenging. Fracture in grounded glaciers is driven by gravitational forces and is typically studied within the framework of quasi-static linear elastic fracture mechanics. In this work, we show that purely quasistatic brittle fracture simulations within the phase field fracture framework under fixed self-weight can become strongly overdriven after crevasse initiation, producing unphysical thickening of the diffusive crack band and diffuse damage patterns. This pathology arises because gravity drives a growing region ahead of the crack tip beyond the strength surface. To resolve this, we show that the post-nucleation propagation is fundamentally a dynamic instability rather than a quasistatic process and propose the use of dynamic formulations of fracture. We demonstrate that accounting for inertia results in sharp, localized cracks that propagate through the ice thickness. As a second objective, this paper introduces a new dynamic formulation of the phase-field fracture model of Kumar et al. (J. Mech. Phys. Solids 2018) in which elastic, inertial, and gravitational contributions are degraded consistently in fractured regions.

</details>


### [121] [Thermodynamics-Informed Machine Learning for Energy Materials Discovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26296)
*Pol Benítez,Cibrán López,Claudio Cazorla*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文提出将热力学（thermodynamics）与机器学习（machine learning）相结合是材料发现领域的重要且尚未充分探索的前沿方向，指出当前ML模型仅局限于零温静态能量预测，必须转向基于自由能（free energy）的热力学描述，以适应现实工况下的材料设计需求。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有机器学习模型在材料发现中仅能预测零温下的静态晶格能量，忽略了决定材料在有限温度下相稳定性、功能响应和性能的自由能景观（free-energy landscapes），这严重限制了材料在现实工况下的可预测设计。

Method: 本文以Perspective（视角文章）形式展开论述，首先分析基于能量的ML模型的根本缺陷，强调熵（entropy）和非谐性（anharmonicity）在自由能及材料功能中的关键作用；继而综述新兴策略，包括机器学习的原子间势（machine-learned interatomic potentials）与ML-统计力学混合框架（hybrid ML-statistical mechanics frameworks），并识别与数据可用性、可迁移性及热力学一致性相关的关键挑战。

Result: 提出了一条以热力学为核心的ML发展路线图（roadmap），核心方向包括直接自由能学习（direct free-energy learning）、熵感知表示（entropy-aware representations）以及跨温度自适应采样（adaptive sampling across temperature）。

Conclusion: 下一代ML模型必须超越静态能量预测，转向对材料在现实工况下行为的热力学描述；这一范式为能源材料领域提供了变革性机遇。

Abstract: Machine learning (ML) is transforming materials discovery by enabling rapid prediction of properties that previously required computationally expensive first-principles calculations. Yet most current ML models remain fundamentally limited to zero-temperature descriptions, learning static lattice energies while neglecting the thermodynamic effects that govern materials behaviour at finite temperature. Because phase stability, functional response, and performance are governed by free-energy landscapes rather than static energies alone, this limitation represents a major barrier to predictive materials design under realistic operating conditions. In this Perspective, we argue that developing thermodynamics-informed ML constitutes one of the most important and least explored frontiers in materials discovery. We examine the fundamental shortcomings of energy-based models, highlighting the essential roles of entropy and anharmonicity in determining free energies and materials functionality. We review emerging strategies, including machine-learned interatomic potentials and hybrid ML-statistical mechanics frameworks, while identifying key challenges related to data availability, transferability, and thermodynamic consistency. Building on these advances, we outline a roadmap for thermodynamics-informed ML centred on direct free-energy learning, entropy-aware representations, and adaptive sampling across temperature. We highlight the transformative opportunities this paradigm offers for energy materials and argue that the next generation of ML models must move beyond static energy predictions towards a thermodynamic description of materials behaviour under realistic operating conditions.

</details>


### [122] [Physics-informed Machine Learning Prediction of Hubbard Interaction Parameters](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26422)
*Jiyeon Kim,Indukuru Ramesh Reddy,Bongjae Kim,Sooran Kim*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文提出了基于机器学习（machine learning, ML）的集成学习模型，用于预测过渡金属氧化物（transition-metal oxides, TMOs）的Hubbard相互作用参数（包括有效在位库仑相互作用$U_{\rm eff}$、位间相互作用$V$和Hund耦合$J$），这些参数源自约束随机相位近似（constrained random-phase approximation, cRPA），旨在替代经验选取或高计算成本的严格方法。模型结合了集成学习与基于回归的暴力搜索（brute-force search, BFS），并采用物理驱动的特征，取得了高预测精度（$U_{\rm eff}$、$V$、$J$的RMSE分别为0.148 eV、0.062 eV、0.007 eV），并给出了显式解析表达式，揭示了各参数的物理决定因素。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Hubbard相互作用参数的精确确定对于关联材料中超越密度泛函理论（beyond-DFT）的方法（如DFT+$U$、DFT+动态平均场论（DMFT）及DFT+$U$+$V$）至关重要。然而，这些参数常被经验性地选取，限制了其在不同材料间的可移植性。尽管cRPA等先进方法提供了严格的评估路径，但其高昂的计算成本阻碍了大规模材料筛选。

Method: 采用集成学习模型与基于回归的暴力搜索（BFS）相结合的方法。特征构建方面，选取了涵盖电子、结构和原子特性的物理驱动描述符，包括过渡金属（TM）的$d$带宽度（TM-$d$ bandwidth）及TM-$d$/O-$p$带中心分离（band-center separation），以反映局域化（localization）和屏蔽（screening）效应。该模型同时实现了预测准确性与显式解析表达式的获取。

Result: 集成模型对$U_{\rm eff}$、$V$、$J$的预测均方根误差（RMSE）分别为0.148 eV、0.062 eV和0.007 eV。解析形式表明：$U_{\rm eff}$与电子局域化及TM-$d$/O-$p$杂化（hybridization）直接相关；$V$的决定因素为杂化程度和结构紧凑性（structural compactness）；$J$主要由TM离子的元素描述符控制。

Conclusion: 本研究提供了一种高效预测cRPA导出的$U_{\rm eff}$、$V$、$J$的方法，同时为这些Hubbard相互作用背后的物理因素提供了深入见解。

Abstract: Accurate determination of Hubbard interaction parameters is essential for beyond-DFT approaches such as DFT+$U$, DFT+DMFT, and DFT+$U$+$V$ in correlated materials. In practice, however, these parameters are often chosen empirically, limiting their transferability across materials. Advanced computational approaches such as the constrained random-phase approximation (cRPA) provide a rigorous route for evaluating Hubbard interactions, but their computational cost remains a bottleneck for large-scale materials screening. Here, we present machine-learning (ML) models for predicting cRPA-derived Hubbard interaction parameters: effective on-site $U_{\rm eff}$, inter-site $V$, and Hund's coupling $J$ for transition-metal oxides (TMOs). We combine ensemble-learning models with a regression-based brute-force search (BFS) approach to achieve both predictive accuracy and explicit analytical expressions. We construct features that capture electronic, structural, and atomic properties, including the TM-$d$ bandwidth and TM-$d$/O-$p$ band-center separation, as physically motivated descriptors of localization and screening. Our ensemble models achieve RMSEs of 0.148 eV, 0.062 eV, and 0.007 eV for $U_{\rm eff}$, $V$, and $J$, respectively. The derived analytical forms directly relate $U_{\rm eff}$ to electron localization and TM-$d$/O-$p$ hybridization, suggest the importance of hybridization and structural compactness in determining $V$, and indicate that $J$ is governed primarily by elemental descriptors of the TM ion. Together, the present study provides an efficient approach for predicting cRPA-derived $U_{\rm eff}$, $V$, and $J$, while offering physical insight into the factors underlying these Hubbard interactions.

</details>


### [123] [PUDA: An AI-Native Hardware Harness for Self-Driving Laboratories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26464)
*Zekun Ren,Hongzhao Tan,Jiaen Yee,Kedar Hippalgaonkar*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: PUDA（Physical Unified Device Architecture）是一种面向自主实验室（self-driving laboratories）的人工智能原生硬件连接框架，通过无头（headless）命令行运行时环境，使AI代理能够以确定性、可审计的方式观测、决策并执行实验操作，同时将协议、运行记录、样本、测量数据和命令日志组织为带有溯源信息的结构化数据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自主实验室通常采用以人为中心的图形用户界面（graphical user interface, GUI）编排层，缺乏专门为AI代理设计的高效硬件交互接口。PUDA的提出旨在填补这一空白，为AI系统与物理实验工具之间提供一种原生的、直接的交互方式。

Method: PUDA采用无头设计（headless by design），通过可发现（discoverable）的命令行接口暴露设备功能；使用JSON协议经由分布式消息系统（distributed messaging system）进行路由；将命令响应、数据产品和报告保存为结构化记录。PUDA将协议（protocols）、运行（runs）、样本（samples）、测量（measurements）和命令日志（command logs）组织为由运行标识符（run identifiers）和时间戳（timestamps）关联的AI原生数据结构，保留从协议提交到硬件响应再到数据产品的完整溯源（provenance）。PUDA将科学编排与物理操作及数据遥测（data telemetry）分离：代理选择实验，PUDA执行已验证的命令并捕获附带溯源的设备状态、响应和数据。

Result: PUDA并非一个新的优化器、编排器或配方语言（recipe language），而是一个面向代理型自主实验室（agentic SDLs）的实用执行与数据环境。

Conclusion: PUDA提供了一种AI原生的硬件连接框架，使得AI系统能够与物理工具进行交互。其更广泛的物理AI（physical AI）意义在于，它为AI系统操作物理实验设备建立了基础性的执行和数据基础设施。

Abstract: Physical Unified Device Architecture (PUDA) is an AI-native hardware harness for self-driving laboratories (SDLs). Rather than building a human-centered graphical user interface (GUI) orchestration layer, PUDA creates a command-line runtime environment that lets agents observe, orient, decide, and act over experiments while hardware execution remains deterministic, atomic, and auditable. Headless by design, devices appear through discoverable command-line interfaces, JSON protocols are routed through a distributed messaging system, and command responses, data products, and reports are preserved as structured records. PUDA organizes protocols, runs, samples, measurements, and command logs into an AI-native data structure linked by run identifiers and timestamps, preserving provenance from submitted protocol through hardware response to resulting data products. PUDA separates scientific orchestration from physical operation and data telemetry: agents choose experiments, while PUDA executes validated commands and captures provenance-linked state, responses, and data. The contribution is not another optimizer, orchestrator, or recipe language. It is a practical execution and data environment for agentic SDLs; the broader physical AI implication is that PUDA provides an AI-native hardware harness for AI systems to interact with physical tools.

</details>


### [124] [Nanoscale Imaging of Strain-Controlled Altermagnetic Domains in α-MnTe](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26495)
*Alex L. Melendez,Sijie Xu,Liangbo Liang,An-Ping Li,Pengcheng Dai,Hu Miao,Zhaoyu Liu,Huan Zhao*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本研究通过结合压电驱动单轴应变装置与扫描氮空位磁力测量技术，在室温下原位成像了体相α-MnTe在压缩过程中的磁畴演化，发现应变通过畴合并（domain coalescence）调控磁织构，而卸载后畴网络表现出滞后的碎片化（hysteretic fragmentation），揭示了畴连接性与拓扑结构作为应变诱导磁记忆的关键载体。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 交替磁体（altermagnets）兼具补偿磁序与动量依赖的自旋分裂，有望避免传统铁磁体的杂散场问题，从而实现自旋电子学功能；力学应变提供了一种调控其奈尔序（Néel order）的潜在手段，但应变如何微观地重组交替磁织构的路径尚不清晰。

Method: 将压电驱动单轴应变单元与扫描氮空位磁力测量技术（scanning nitrogen-vacancy magnetometry）集成，在室温原位成像体相α-MnTe在压缩过程中的磁畴结构。

Result: 压缩通过畴合并（domain coalescence）重组磁织构，增大最大连通畴的尺寸并降低畴壁密度；卸载后应变形成的畴网络并不沿加载路径回溯，而是碎片化为新的亚稳态构型，在最大畴尺寸与杂散场分布上产生显著滞后（hysteresis）。

Conclusion: 畴连接性与拓扑结构是应变诱导磁记忆（strain-induced magnetic memory）的关键载体；畴合并与滞后碎片化构成了α-MnTe中应变控制的微观路径，为实现应变可编程的交替磁织构与可重构自旋电子学器件奠定了基础。

Abstract: Altermagnets combine compensated magnetic order with momentum-dependent spin splitting, offering a route to spintronic functionality without the stray fields of conventional ferromagnets. Mechanical strain provides a promising means of controlling their Néel order, yet the microscopic pathway by which strain reorganizes an altermagnetic texture remains unresolved. Here, we integrate a piezo-driven uniaxial strain cell with scanning nitrogen-vacancy magnetometry to image the magnetic domains of bulk α-MnTe during in situ compression at room temperature. We find that compression reorganizes the magnetic texture through domain coalescence, increasing the size of the largest connected domain while reducing the domain-wall density. Upon unloading, however, the strain-formed domain network does not retrace the loading pathway. Instead, the large connected regions fragment into a new metastable configuration, producing pronounced hysteresis in the maximum domain size and stray-field distribution. These results identify domain connectivity and topology as key carriers of strain-induced magnetic memory. Our work reveals domain coalescence and hysteretic fragmentation as the microscopic pathway of strain control in α-MnTe and establishes a route toward strain-programmable altermagnetic textures and reconfigurable spintronic devices.

</details>


### [125] [Percolating Multifractal Domains at a Polymorphic Phase Boundary](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26586)
*Yuan-Jinsheng Liu,Xu Tian,Jiahui Zhai,Shi Liu*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本研究通过大规模分子动力学模拟，揭示了无铅铁电固溶体(K,Na)NbO3-(Bi,Na)ZrO3中多晶型相界（polymorphic phase boundary, PPB）本质上是渗流多重分形极性畴（percolating multifractal polar domain）结构，该结构通过分形连通性（fractal connectivity）和多尺度异质性（multiscale heterogeneity）控制介电与压电响应。最大可逆压电响应出现在分形畴体积分数接近三维渗流阈值时，表明近临界极性连通性实现了巨可逆机电耦合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 铁电体中巨压电性通常与相界不稳定性（phase-boundary instabilities）相关，但传统上将多晶型相界（PPB）归因于铁电相的共存，缺乏对其微观机制的深入理解。

Method: 采用大规模分子动力学（molecular dynamics）模拟，对无铅(K,Na)NbO3-(Bi,Na)ZrO3固溶体体系进行研究，定量分析极性网络的全局分形维数（fractal dimension）和多分形谱宽度（multifractal spectrum width）。

Result: PPB中存在渗流多重分形极性畴结构；分形连通性和多尺度异质性可作为PPB的微结构序参量（microstructural order parameters）；最大可逆压电响应出现在分形畴体积分数接近三维渗流阈值（percolation threshold）时。

Conclusion: 渗流多重分形极性畴是PPB增强压电性的微观机制，分形骨架（fractal backbone）保持极性记忆并提供恢复力，周围非分形区域提供极性顺服性以实现大极化旋转和应变，分形连通性可作为高性能压电材料的设计参数。

Abstract: Giant piezoelectricity in ferroelectrics is commonly associated with phase-boundary instabilities, among which the polymorphic phase boundary (PPB) is a prominent example conventionally attributed to the coexistence of ferroelectric phases. Here, using large-scale molecular dynamics simulations of the lead-free (K,Na)NbO3-(Bi,Na)ZrO3 solid solutions, we show that the PPB hosts a percolating multifractal polar domain which governs the dielectric and piezoelectric responses. By quantifying the global fractal dimension and multifractal spectrum width of this polar network, we identify fractal connectivity and multiscale heterogeneity as microstructural order parameters for the PPB. The maximum reversible piezoelectric response occurs when the fractal-domain volume fraction approaches the three-dimensional percolation threshold, suggesting that near-critical polar connectivity enables giant reversible electromechanical coupling. In this mechanism, the fractal backbone preserves polar memory and provides the restoring force required for reversibility, while the surrounding nonfractal regions supply the polar compliance needed for large polarization rotation and strain. These results establish percolating multifractal polar domains as a microscopic mechanism for PPB-enhanced piezoelectricity and suggest fractal connectivity as a design parameter for high-performance piezoelectrics.

</details>


### [126] [Transient Detour and Cooperative Oxygen Exchange in the Polarization Switching of Ferroelectric Hf0.5Zr0.5O2](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26597)
*Ryotaro Sahashi,Po-Yen Chen,Teruyasu Mizoguchi*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本研究通过电场诱导分子动力学（Electric-Field-Induced MD）模拟，结合机器学习力场（MACEField），在无缺陷的氧化铪锆（HZO）理想晶格中动态再现了P-E电滞回线，揭示了极化翻转由3配位氧（O3c）与4配位氧（O4c）的动态互换驱动，而非传统简单位移模型，并发现了伴随阳离子-氧瞬态成键的“绕行”行为以及胞内体积涨落自补偿的“内部自补偿机制”。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: HZO作为下一代非易失性存储器的核心材料，其极化翻转机制的研究长期依赖静态能量势垒分析，导致实际电场驱动下的瞬态成键与协同动力学机制尚未明确。本文旨在从动态视角揭示HZO极化翻转的微观物理起源。

Method: 采用微调后的机器学习力场（MACEField），对无缺陷理想HZO晶格进行电场诱导分子动力学（Electric-Field-Induced MD）模拟，动态追踪氧原子位移轨迹并分析配位数的变化。

Result: 成功动态再现了P-E电滞回线；证明极化翻转由3配位氧（O3c）与4配位氧（O4c）的动态互换驱动，而非传统的S:N/S:T简单位移模型；发现了由瞬态阳离子-氧成键导致的独特“绕行”行为；识别出配位数变化伴随的局域体积涨落在晶胞内相互抵消的“内部自补偿机制”。

Conclusion: HZO在不产生宏观应变的前提下维持稳定极化翻转的能力源于O3c/O4c协同交换路径的完整性，而非单个原子位移的最小化；这一发现为下一代铁电存储器的耐久性与可缩放性设计提供了关键原则。

Abstract: Hafnium zirconium oxide (HZO) has attracted significant attention as a core material for next-generation non-volatile memories due to its excellent ferroelectricity in the ultra-thin film regime and its CMOS process compatibility. However, the exploration of its polarization switching mechanism has predominantly relied on static energy barrier analyses, leaving the transient bond formation and cooperative dynamic mechanisms under actual electric field driving unresolved. In this study, we performed Electric-Field-Induced MD simulations on a defect-free ideal HZO lattice using a fine-tuned machine learning force field (MACEField). As a result, we successfully reproduced the P-E hysteresis loop dynamically and demonstrated that the polarization switching in HZO is driven not by conventional simple displacement models (S:N/S:T models), but by the dynamic mutual exchange of 3-coordinated oxygen (O3c) and 4-coordinated oxygen (O4c). Analysis of the oxygen atom displacement trajectories revealed that this pathway is accompanied by a unique "detour" behavior originating from transient cation-oxygen bond formation. Furthermore, we identified an "internal self-compensation mechanism" in which the local volumetric expansion and contraction accompanying the coordination number changes are effectively offset within the cell. These findings provide, from a dynamic perspective, a microscopic physical origin of for HZO's exceptional ability to sustain stable polarization switching without macroscopic strain, a property that has long distinguished HZO from conventional perovskite ferroelectrics yet lacked atomistic explanation. These findings suggest that preserving the integrity of cooperative O3c/O4c exchange pathways, rather than minimizing individual atomic displacements, is the key design principle for endurance and scalability in next-generation ferroelectric memories.

</details>


### [127] [Computational Study of Water Adsorption and Dissociative Mechanisms Impacting g-C3N4's Optical and Electronic Properties](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26667)
*Amil Aligayev,Ulkar Jabbarli,F. Javier Domınguez-Gutierrez,Ulkar Samadova,Jialin Li,Stefanos Papanikolaou,Qing Huang*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本研究通过从头计算（ab initio）、自洽电荷密度泛函紧束缚（SCC-DFTB）及ReaxFF反应力场分子动力学等多种计算模拟方法，系统研究了水分子吸收对g-C3N4纳米片电子与光学性质的影响，发现H2O解离为HO和O分子后能显著增强纳米片的光吸收和电导率，表明其在光催化水裂解产氢中的潜力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为实现可持续能源解决方案，水裂解（water splitting）是产生清洁氢燃料的关键过程，因此需要寻找高效的光催化材料以提升能量存储、减排及可持续发展目标的实现。

Method: 采用综合计算模拟方法，结合基于密度泛函理论（density functional theory, DFT）的从头计算与自洽电荷密度泛函紧束缚（self-consistent charge density functional tight binding, SCC-DFTB）模拟，对g-C3N4纳米片在吸收OH和H2O分子后的行为进行表征；同时利用ReaxFF框架下的经典分子动力学（classical molecular dynamics）模拟，建模多个H2O分子的释放过程并评估氢析出（H2 evolution）速率。

Result: 发现H2O分解为HO和O分子后，与原始（pristine）纳米片相比，显著增强了g-C3N4纳米片的光吸收（optical absorbance）和电导率（conductance）。

Conclusion: 研究结果表明，g-C3N4纳米片具有作为水裂解应用有效材料的潜力。

Abstract: In the quest for sustainable energy solutions, water splitting emerges as a crucial process for generating clean hydrogen a versatile and renewable fuel essential for energy storage, emissions reduction, and achieving sustainability goals. This study employs a comprehensive computational approach, utilizing atomistic simulations to systematically investigate the effects of water absorption on the electronic and optical properties of g-C3N4 nanosheets. Our methodology integrates ab initio computations grounded in density functional theory (DFT), which allows for a detailed characterization of the nanosheet and serves as a benchmark for self-consistent charge density functional tight binding (SCC-DFTB) simulations. This approach provides valuable insights into the behavior of the nanosheet under the influence of absorbed OH and H2O molecules by considering calculated parameters for photocatalytic efficiency. Additionally, we extend our investigation to classical molecular dynamics simulations within the ReaxFF framework, modeling the emission of multiple H2O molecules and assessing the subsequent rate of H2 evolution. A key finding of our study reveals that the dissociation of H2O into HO and O molecules significantly enhances both the optical absorbance and conductance of the nanosheet compared to its pristine state. These results underscore the potential of g-C3N4 nanosheets as effective materials for water splitting applications.

</details>


### [128] [End-to-End Modeling of a Volatile TiO2 Memristor for Neuromorphic Circuit Simulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26815)
*Lukas Endres,Hannes Töpfer,Michaela Blum,Hauke Honig,Peter Schaaf*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文基于挥发性TiO2忆阻器（memristor）的实验数据，开发并验证了一个SPICE仿真模型，该模型能够在连续和脉冲激励下准确复现器件的电学行为，并成功应用于泄漏积分发放（leaky integrate-and-fire）神经元仿真。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 忆阻器在非易失性存储、神经形态计算、逻辑电路和模拟信号处理等领域具有广阔的应用前景，而开发这类系统需要基于能够准确复现真实器件电学行为的仿真模型。

Method: 首先通过实验测量数据验证TiO2基忆阻器的忆阻行为，并识别合适的模型；随后优化模型参数以匹配实测特性；最后将优化后的模型在SPICE中实现，并通过对比仿真结果与测量数据进行验证。

Result: 仿真结果与实验测量数据之间显示出良好的一致性，表明所开发的模型能够准确复现所研究忆阻器的电学行为。

Conclusion: 该模型适用于电路级仿真，并通过泄漏积分发放神经元的应用实例展示了其实用性。

Abstract: Memristors are promising devices for applications such as non-volatile memory, neuromorphic computing, logic circuits, and analog signal processing. The development of such systems requires accurate simulations based on models that reproduce the electrical behavior of real devices under both continuous and pulsed excitation. This work presents the development of a simulation environment for a volatile TiO2-based memristor. Experimental measurement data are analyzed to verify the memristive behavior of the device and to identify a suitable model. The model parameters are then optimized to match the measured characteristics. The resulting model is implemented in SPICE and validated by comparing simulation results with measurement data. The comparison shows a good agreement between simulation and experiment, demonstrating that the developed model is suitable for reproducing the electrical behavior of the investigated memristor and can be applied in circuit-level simulations, as demonstrated by a leaky integrate-and-fire neuron.

</details>


### [129] [Inferring Magnetic Material Parameters from Statistical Measures in Strongly Fluctuating Magnetization Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26833)
*Kübra Kalkan,Atreya Majumdar,Ross Knapman,Omer Fetai,Franziska Scheibel,Sabrina Disch,Illia Horenko,Karin Everschor-Sitte*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文提出一种仅基于磁化动力学数据推断磁性材料参数（如交换刚度、磁各向异性）的框架，利用潜熵（latent entropy）等统计量对微观磁化动力学进行反演建模，在多晶/多相微结构的高涨落条件下实现参数推断与晶界检测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 交换刚度（exchange stiffness）和磁各向异性（magnetic anisotropy）等磁性材料参数决定了磁系统的行为与功能，但传统基于织构的方法在多晶或多相微结构的高涨落（strongly fluctuating regimes）条件下变得不可靠，亟需一种新的推断方法。

Method: 提出一个纯磁化（magnetization-only）框架，从热驱动磁化动力学（thermally driven magnetization dynamics）中提取时间均值（temporal mean）和潜熵（latent entropy）等统计量，对描述符（descriptors）进行模型拟合，并通过模型反演来推断材料参数。

Result: 该框架能够实现材料参数推断（material-parameter inference）以及非均匀样品中的晶界检测（grain-boundary detection）。在所考察的描述符中，潜熵在参数估计精度上优于时间均值。

Conclusion: 潜熵是从动态磁化数据中推断磁性材料参数的高效描述符，有望应用于高温及强烈涨落条件下的实验参数提取。

Abstract: Magnetic material parameters such as the exchange stiffness and magnetic anisotropy govern the behavior and functionality of magnetic systems, yet their local inference from magnetization data remains challenging, particularly in strongly fluctuating regimes with polycrystalline or multiphase microstructure, where conventional texture-based methods become unreliable. We introduce a magnetization-only framework for inferring material parameters from thermally driven magnetization dynamics. Using micromagnetic simulations, we extract statistical quantities such as temporal mean and latent entropy from the magnetization dynamics, fit models to these descriptors, and invert the models to infer material parameters. We show that this framework enables material-parameter inference as well as grain-boundary detection in a heterogeneous sample. Among the descriptors considered, latent entropy yields more accurate parameter estimates than the temporal mean. Our results establish latent entropy as an efficient descriptor for inferring magnetic material parameters from dynamical magnetization data and point toward its use for experimental parameter extraction at high temperatures and, more broadly, under strongly fluctuating conditions.

</details>


### [130] [Magnetic hopfions at room temperature](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26839)
*Kaixin Zhu,Wenli Gao,Zhan Wang,Shuaishuai Sun,Siyuan Huang,Junxi Tong,Jun Li,Huanfang Tian,Zian Li,Huaixin Yang,Ying Zhang,Olle Eriksson,Filipp N. Rybakov,Nikolai S. Kiselev,Jianqi Li*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: No AI summary available.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: No AI summary available.

Method: No AI summary available.

Result: No AI summary available.

Conclusion: No AI summary available.

Abstract: Hopfions are three-dimensional (3D) topological solitons predicted to exist in diverse magnetic systems, yet their practical utility has been largely restricted to cryogenic environments. Here, we overcome this temperature constraint by demonstrating stable magnetic hopfions in the chiral magnet Co8Zn8Mn4 at and above room temperature. Using a transmission electron microscope equipped for in situ optical excitation, we generate magnetic hopfions with femtosecond laser pulses. Long-term observations further reveal Brownian-like motion at room temperature and thermally activated collapse upon approaching the high-temperature regime. Together with micromagnetic simulations and homotopy group analysis, our experimental observations uncover the hopfion formation mechanism through the fusion of bimeron pairs. These findings establish room-temperature magnetic hopfions and provide a framework for their further studies under technologically relevant conditions.

</details>


### [131] [Crystal forming ability of amorphous refractory metals under nanoindentation: a molecular dynamics study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26881)
*Prashant Dwivedi,Alberto Fraile,Tomas Polcar*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 通过大规模分子动力学（molecular dynamics）纳米压痕模拟，研究了非晶态难熔金属（V、Nb、Mo、Ta、W）在接触加载下的机械诱导结晶（devitrification）行为，发现非晶到体心立方（bcc）的转变通过体相成核、生长和合并发生，结晶能力（CFA）随元素种类和压头速度变化，并揭示了剪切关联位移路径与微观组织演化机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非晶态难熔金属涂层兼具高硬度与化学惰性，但其亚稳态（metastability）在接触加载下易于发生机械诱导结晶，且不同难熔金属中玻璃态向母体体心立方（bcc）晶体重排的难易程度尚不清楚。

Method: 采用熔淬（melt quenching）方法制备非晶态V、Nb、Mo、Ta、W至300 K；通过从头算（ab initio）液态径向分布函数（radial distribution functions）验证各原子间势（interatomic potential）；利用大规模分子动力学（molecular dynamics）纳米压痕（nanoindentation）模拟进行探测。

Result: 压痕通过体相成核、生长与合并驱动局域非晶到bcc转变；结晶能力（CFA）定义为bcc分数-深度S形曲线的最大斜率，按V > Mo > Nb > Ta > W递减，约相差四倍；CFA随压头速度按CFA ∝ v⁻ᵐ（平均指数m=1.08）下降，使CFA·v近乎常数，转变速率几乎与速度无关；转变原子携带过量非仿射位移（non affine displacement）和局域剪切应变（local shear strain），而非静水压力，表明剪切关联位移路径；抗性最强元素W的体相驱动力最大，故抗性源于共价键强度而非热力学驱动力；早期bcc有序度决定转变锐度与深度，半转变机械功决定持久核密度；晶粒种群平衡将成核、生长与合并关联至末端微观组织，其完整性不遵循CFA顺序。

Conclusion: 非晶态难熔金属在接触加载下的结晶受剪切关联位移路径主导，抗结晶能力与共价键强度相关而非热力学驱动力，末端微观组织的完整性独立于结晶能力排序。

Abstract: Amorphous refractory metal coatings combine high hardness with chemical inertness, yet their metastability makes them prone to mechanically induced crystallisation (devitrification) under contact loading, and how readily such a glass re-orders to its parent body centred cubic (bcc) crystal is unknown across the refractory series. We prepared amorphous V, Nb, Mo, Ta and W by melt quenching to 300 K, validated each interatomic potential against ab initio liquid radial distribution functions, and probed them by large scale molecular dynamics nanoindentation. Indentation drives a localised amorphous to bcc transformation by bulk nucleation, growth and coalescence. A crystal forming ability (CFA), the maximum slope of the sigmoidal bcc fraction versus depth curve, spans about a factor of four and decreases as V > Mo > Nb > Ta > W; it falls with indenter velocity as CFA $\propto v^{-m}$ with a mean exponent of 1.08, so CFA$\cdot v$ is nearly constant and the transformation rate is almost velocity independent. Transforming atoms carry excess non affine displacement and local shear strain, not hydrostatic pressure, marking a shear associated displacive pathway; the bulk driving force is largest for the most resistant element, W, so resistance tracks cohesive bond strength rather than the thermodynamic driving force. Early bcc like order sets the sharpness and depth of the transition, whereas the mechanical work to half transformation sets the persistent nucleus density. A grain population balance links nucleation, growth and coalescence to a terminal microstructure whose completeness does not follow the CFA order.

</details>


### [132] [Structure-Property Correlation of Cr/Cu-MnFeCoNi High-Entropy Alloys for Alkaline Water Electrolysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26888)
*Shreyasi Chattopadhyaya,Raphael B. de Oliveira,Deepti Gangwar,Tymofii S Pieshkov,Marcelo L. Pereira Junior,Dhiman Banik,Astrid Campos-Mata,Atin Pramanik,Soumyabrata Roy,Douglas S. Galvão,Chandra Sekhar Tiwary,Krishanu Biswas,Pulickel M Ajayana*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文通过比较CrMnFeCoNi（HEA-Cr）与MnFeCoNiCu（HEA-Cu）两种高熵合金（high-entropy alloy, HEA），发现单一元素替换（Cr替换为Cu）能显著提升碱性析氢反应（HER）和析氧反应（OER）的双功能催化活性。实验与计算分析表明，Cu替换通过调控电子结构优化关键反应中间体的结合能，且OER后HEA-Cu表面形成富Cu壳层结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高熵合金（HEA）因其组分复杂性和可调的表面化学性质，在电催化领域展现出潜力，但单一元素替换如何影响HEA的结构-性能关系尚不明确。本文旨在通过对比两种HEA，揭示元素替换对碱性析氢反应（HER）和析氧反应（OER）催化活性的调控机制。

Method: 实验上，比较了CrMnFeCoNi（HEA-Cr）和MnFeCoNiCu（HEA-Cu）两种高熵合金在相同条件下对碱性HER和OER的催化活性。计算分析通过电子结构调控和反应中间体（H*, O*, OH*, OOH*）结合能的变化，解释实验结果。

Result: HEA-Cu在HER和OER上均优于HEA-Cr，其过电位（538 mV）和Tafel斜率（165 mV dec⁻¹）更低。计算表明Cu替换优化了电子结构，提供了更有利的中间体结合能。OER后HEA-Cu表面出现Cu迁移，形成富Cu外层与多金属核心的壳核结构。

Conclusion: 单一元素替换是调控高熵合金表面化学和电化学动力学的有效策略，为设计高性能、低成本的水电解催化剂提供了新思路。

Abstract: High-entropy alloys (HEAs), with their unique compositional-complexity and tunable surface chemistry, have emerged as promising electrocatalysts for energy conversion. The catalytic activity of HEA often arises from the interplay between the intrinsic activity of the individual elements and the synergistic effects generated at the interfaces. Even a single-element substitution in a multicomponent HEA can substantially alter the surface-chemistry and electrochemical kinetics of the active surface. Here we investigated the structure-property relationship of CrMnFeCoNi (HEA-Cr) and MnFeCoNiCu (HEA-Cu) HEAs by comparing the alkaline hydrogen evolution reaction (HER) and oxygen evolution reaction (OER) activity. Under the same experimental condition, HEA-Cu outperforms HEA-Cr towards both HER and OER. Substituting Cr with Cu significantly enhances the bifunctional activity, where HEA-Cu achieved a lower overpotential (538 mV) and Tafel slope (165 mVdec-1) when compared with HEA-Cr. Computational analysis corroborates these findings, showing that Cu substitution modulates the electronic-structure to provide favorable binding energies for reaction intermediates (H*, O*, OH*, and OOH*). Interestingly, unlike HER, recovered HEA-Cu after OER showed migration of Cu forming a Cu-rich outer layer shell with a multimetallic core. These findings demonstrate the potential of single-element substitution in HEAs as a strategy for designing high-performance, cost-effective catalysts for efficient water electrolysis.

</details>


### [133] [Ferroelectric switchable altermagnetic-like compensated ferrimagnets with charge ordering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26971)
*Xinyu Yang,Shuai Dong*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文预测Fe₃O₅单层材料具有一种混合自旋劈裂机制，结合了类交替磁体（altermagnet）的k路径交替劈裂和亚铁磁体（ferrimagnet）的塞曼劈裂（Zeeman splitting）。该材料借助隐藏的磁电耦合（magnetoelectricity）可实现电场完全调控自旋劈裂，其电导率自旋极化率超过99%，接近半金属（half-metal）但净磁化强度为零。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非常规共线磁体（如交替磁体）兼具铁磁体和反铁磁体的优势，但几乎无净磁化。通过引入铁电性（ferroelectricity）和电荷有序（charge ordering）等额外自由度，可进一步实现功能化。本文旨在设计一种兼具这些特性的新型二维材料。

Method: 通过理论预测和设计原则，提出Fe₃O₅单层结构，并分析其自旋劈裂机制，即类交替磁体k路径交替劈裂与亚铁磁类塞曼劈裂的叠加。利用自旋-电荷耦合（spin-charge coupling）产生的隐藏磁电性，研究电场对自旋劈裂的调控。

Result: Fe₃O₅单层展现出混合自旋劈裂机制；通过电场可实现自旋劈裂的完全切换；其电导率自旋极化率超过99%，与半金属相当但净磁化强度为零。

Conclusion: Fe₃O₅单层作为一类新型非常规共线磁体，结合了多种自旋劈裂机制和电场调控能力，在自旋电子学（spintronics）中具有潜在应用价值。

Abstract: Unconventional collinear magnets with almost zero magnetization but prominent nonrelativistic spin-splitting, such as altermagnets, can inherit the advantages of both ferromagnets and antiferromagnets. By incorporating more degrees of freedom such as ferroelectricity and charge ordering, these unconventional magnets can be even more interesting and functionalized. With this design principle, the Fe$_3$O$_5$ monolayer is predicted to exhibit a hybrid spin-splitting mechanism, with the superposition of the altermagnetic-like $k$-path alternating splitting and ferrimagnet-like Zeeman splitting. Benefiting from the hidden magnetoelectricity based on the spin-charge coupling, such spin-splitting can be fully switched by an electric field. Its conductivity is highly spin-polarized, with a polarization ratio above $99\%$, comparable to half-metals but with zero magnetization.

</details>


### [134] [Theory of phonon-magnon hybridization and angular momentum in CrI$_3$ and CrBr$_3$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26986)
*Maxime Mignolet,Miquel Royo,Massimiliano Stengel,Matthieu J. Verstreate*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文发展了一个约束哈密顿量（constrained Hamiltonian）框架，通过引入混合刚度矩阵（hybrid stiffness matrices）和贝里曲率（Berry curvatures），量化了磁材料中声子（phonons）与磁子（magnons）的杂化程度（在CrI₃中达8%，CrBr₃中达25%），并分析了杂化过程中总角动量守恒及其在声子与磁子子系统间的重新分配。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 磁性材料中角动量可由电子、磁子（magnons）和声子（phonons）等多种载流子介导。磁子能与能量相近的圆极化声子（circularly polarized phonons）在特定对称条件下发生相互作用，当相互作用足够强时，两者频率偏移并混合形成杂化磁弹准粒子（hybrid magneto-elastic quasi-particles）。该研究旨在量化此类杂化程度并揭示角动量守恒机制。

Method: 本文发展了一个约束哈密顿量（constrained Hamiltonian）框架，该框架融合了混合刚度矩阵（hybrid stiffness matrices）和贝里曲率（Berry curvatures）。通过引入总能量分解（decomposition of the total energy）方法（即文献中用于声子原子贡献的范数分解（norm decomposition）的推广），量化声子与磁子之间的杂化程度。

Result: 在CrI₃中声子-磁子杂化程度达到8%，在CrBr₃中达到25%。杂化过程中总角动量保持守恒，但在声子子系统（phononic subsystem）和磁子子系统（magnonic subsystem）之间重新分配。

Conclusion: 该约束哈密顿量框架可有效量化磁材料中声子-磁子的杂化程度，并揭示杂化过程中总角动量守恒但子系统间重新分配的物理机制。

Abstract: In magnetic materials angular momentum can be mediated by different carriers, including electrons, magnons, and phonons. The magnons can interact with circularly polarized phonons which are close in energy, provided specific symmetry conditions are met. If the interaction is strong enough the phonons and magnons shift in frequency and start to mix to form hybrid magneto-elastic quasi-particles. In this paper, we develop a constrained Hamiltonian framework which incorporates hybrid stiffness matrices and Berry curvatures. We quantify the degree of hybridization between phonons and magnons (up to 8% in CrI$_3$ and 25% in CrBr$_3$) using a decomposition of the total energy, which is a generalization of the norm decomposition for atomic contributions to phonons used in the literature. We also explore how the total angular momentum is conserved but shared between the phononic and magnonic subsystems upon hybridization.

</details>


### [135] [Generation of representative powder particle packing in 2D/3D: which tool for which application?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26992)
*Antoine Tainturier,Louis Lemarquis,Victor Szczepan,Marc Bernacki*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文针对粉末冶金、增材制造和颗粒物理模拟中的初始密堆积生成问题，系统比较了四种开源工具（DR、DR-ME、LAMMPS、dp3D）在物理可行性、粒度分布忠实度、相对密度和计算开销方面的表现，发现DEM方法密度最高但速度慢三个数量级，而DR方法在速度与精度间提供了实用的权衡，并提出了面向应用的选型指南。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管密堆积（dense sphere packings）是粉末冶金、增材制造和颗粒物理（granular physics）模拟的初始状态，但堆积生成器的选择很少基于系统的基准测试，缺乏指导性对比。

Method: 对四种开源工具进行基准测试：基于顺序放置的DR（dropping-and-rolling）及其致密化变体DR-ME，以及基于离散元法（discrete element method, DEM）的LAMMPS（重力模式）和dp3D（等静压压缩）。在四种构型（2D/3D对数正态分布、3D双峰分布及3D域尺寸研究）中，以Hellinger距离衡量粒度分布（particle size distribution, PSD）忠实度，以相对密度Phi对比工业MIM级粉末的实测固体加载量（phi_exp=0.62）。

Result: 在3D场景中，DEM代码（LAMMPS与dp3D）达到最密堆积，但运行速度慢三个数量级以上；对于约20000个颗粒，DR方法比dp3D密度低9%，但快1800倍。

Conclusion: 这些理想化模型堆积得出了面向应用的工具选择指南（application-driven tool-selection guidelines）。

Abstract: Although dense sphere packings serve as the initial state for simulations in powder metallurgy, additive manufacturing and granular physics, the choice of a packing generator is rarely guided by a systematic benchmark. A representative packing must be (i)-(ii) physically admissible (non-overlapping particles in gravitational equilibrium); (iii) faithful to the target particle size distribution (PSD); (iv) representative in relative density Phi; and (v) computationally affordable. Four open-source tools have been benchmarked, meeting (i)-(ii) by construction: the sequential DR (dropping-and-rolling) and its densified variant DR-ME, and the discrete element method (DEM) codes LAMMPS (gravity) and dp3D (isostatic compression). Across four configurations (2D/3D lognormal, 3D bimodal, and a 3D domain-size study), they are compared against an industrial MIM-grade powder, with PSD fidelity measured by the bin-width-independent Hellinger distance and Phi against the feedstock solid loading (phi_exp = 0.62, by Archimedes' method). In 3D, the DEM codes reach the densest packings but run more than three orders of magnitude slower: for approximatively 20 000 particles, DR shows a 9% phi shortfall relative to dp3D while running 1800x faster. These idealised model packings yield application-driven tool-selection guidelines.

</details>


### [136] [No band gap, no problem: Defects in InAs using a band-avoiding occupation-constrained density functional theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27095)
*Peter A. Schultz,Arthur H. Edwards,Evan M. Anderson,Anthony C. Knighton,Leopoldo Diaz*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文针对密度泛函理论（DFT）低估实验带隙的“带隙问题”（band gap problem），提出了一种避免带边态的占据约束DFT方法（ba-occ-DFT），通过阻止带边态的错误占据来实现原子缺陷的可靠总能量计算。在窄带隙半导体砷化铟（InAs）上的应用表明，该方法能够规避带隙问题，将带边误差与缺陷能级计算分离，即使在DFT带隙为零的情况下也能进行严格的缺陷能级预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 密度泛函理论（DFT）系统性地低估实验带隙，即著名的“带隙问题”（band gap problem）。由于带隙决定了缺陷能级的能量尺度，这一问题使原子缺陷的电荷跃迁能（charge transition energies）计算变得复杂。在窄带隙半导体的极端情况下，DFT带隙坍缩为零，似乎完全排除了缺陷能级的定量预测可能性。

Method: 本文提出了一种避免带边态的占据约束DFT方法（band-avoiding occupation-constrained DFT, ba-occ-DFT）。该方法通过阻止带边态（band-edge states）的错误占据，实现了原子缺陷的可靠总能量计算。

Result: 在窄带隙半导体砷化铟（indium arsenide, InAs）上的应用表明，ba-occ-DFT能够规避带隙问题，将带边误差（band-edge errors）与缺陷能级计算分离。即使在DFT带隙为零的情况下，该方法仍能实现窄带隙半导体中严格的缺陷能级预测。

Conclusion: ba-occ-DFT方法有效解决了窄带隙半导体中因DFT带隙问题导致的缺陷能级计算困难，为在零DFT带隙条件下进行可靠的缺陷能级预测提供了一条可行路径。

Abstract: Density functional theory (DFT) underestimates the experimental band gap---the infamous band gap problem. As the band gap defines the energy scale of defect levels, this complicates computation of charge transition energies for atomic defects. In the extreme case of narrow-gap semiconductors, the DFT band gap collapses to zero, seemingly precluding quantitative predictions of defect levels. We present a band-avoiding occupation-constrained DFT (ba-occ-DFT) approach that prevents spurious occupation of band-edge states and enables reliable total energy calculations of atomic defects. Application to indium arsenide (InAs) shows that ba-occ-DFT circumvents the band gap problem, separates band-edge errors from defect level calculations, and enables rigorous defect level predictions in a narrow-gap semiconductor despite a zero DFT band gap.

</details>


### [137] [Formation of $\mathrm{L}1_2$-ordered $γ'$-$\mathrm{Ni}_3\mathrm{Al}$ precipitates in ternary Cu-Ni-Al alloys modelled using an ab initio concentration wave theory and atomistic simulations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27108)
*Christopher D. Woodgate,Hubert J. Naguszewski,Samuel L. Deacon,Mathys P. Potel,Ján Minár,David Quigley,Julie B. Staunton*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本研究通过第一性原理电子结构计算与浓度波分析相结合的建模框架，系统研究了Cu-Ni-Al伪二元合金的热力学与相稳定性，揭示了三种与成分相关的相行为区间，并建立了相变与电子结构特征之间的关联，展示了一种高效模拟多组分替代式合金中化学有序化与共格析出的计算工作流。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 析出强化型Cu-Ni-Al合金因共格$L1_2$有序$\gamma'$-Ni₃Al析出相能同时赋予材料高机械强度与良好的输运性质，具有重要技术应用价值。然而，对该伪二元体系的相稳定性和热力学行为的系统理论理解尚不充分，需要建立高效的计算方法加以研究。

Method: 采用结合第一性原理电子结构计算（first-principles electronic structure calculations）与浓度波分析（concentration wave analysis）的计算建模框架，从中提取原子-原子有效对相互作用（atom-atom effective pair interactions），并用于原子级蒙特卡罗模拟（atomistic Monte Carlo simulations）。

Result: 研究发现三种与成分（Cu含量）相关的相行为区间：低Cu含量时Cu溶解于$L1_2$有序Ni₃Al相，仅存在Ni-Al间化学有序化转变；中等成分时高温有序化后伴随低温的Cu与$L1_2$有序Ni₃Al的相分离；高Cu含量时$L1_2$有序Ni₃Al直接从固溶体中析出，无明显的二次转变。这些相变与合金的电子结构特征相关。

Conclusion: 该工作展示了一种能够同时捕捉多组分替代式合金中化学有序化与共格析出的计算高效工作流，对析出强化（precipitation strengthening）等现象的研究具有参考意义。

Abstract: Precipitation-strengthened Cu-Ni-Al alloys are of interest for technological applications because coherent, $\mathrm{L}1_2$-ordered $γ'$-$\mathrm{Ni}_3\mathrm{Al}$ precipitates can confer high mechanical strength while allowing the material to retain many of the good transport properties characteristic of elemental Cu. In this work, we study the thermodynamics and phase stability of the pseudobinary $\textrm{Cu}_x (\textrm{Ni}_{3/4} \textrm{Al}_{1/4})_{1-x}$ system, $0 \leq x \leq 1$. We use a computational modelling framework combining first-principles electronic structure calculations with a concentration wave analysis from which atom-atom effective pair interactions are extracted for use in atomistic Monte Carlo simulations. Our modelling reveals three distinct, composition-dependent regimes of phase behaviour, in qualitative agreement with the experimentally determined phase diagram. At low Cu content, Cu is soluble in the $\mathrm{L}1_2$-ordered $\mathrm{Ni}_3\mathrm{Al}$ phase, with a single identifiable phase transition corresponding to chemical ordering between Ni and Al. At intermediate compositions, this high-temperature ordering is followed at lower temperatures by phase separation of Cu and $\mathrm{L}1_2$-ordered $\mathrm{Ni}_3\mathrm{Al}$. Finally, at high Cu content, $\mathrm{L}1_2$-ordered $\mathrm{Ni}_3\mathrm{Al}$ precipitates directly from the solid solution, with no clearly identifiable secondary transition. We relate these phase transformations to features of the underlying electronic structures of the considered alloys. Overall, this work demonstrates a computationally efficient workflow capturing both chemical ordering and coherent precipitation in multicomponent substitutional alloys, with relevance to the study of phenomena such as precipitation strengthening.

</details>


### [138] [Materials Behavior as Mechanism Ensembles: A Probabilistic Framework for Emergent Behaviors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27163)
*Brad L. Boyce,Mitchell A. Wood,Krishna Garikipati,Andreas E. Robertson,Jeffrey Larson,Ishan Srivastava,Bert Debusschere,Saaketh Desai,Prasad Iyer,Pieterjan Robbe,Mathew Cherukara,Todd Munson,Ming Du,Trupti Mohanty,David J. Gardner,Laurent Capolungo,Benjamin A. Jasperson,Jingye Tan,Rémi Dingreville*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文提出一个概率框架（probabilistic framework），将材料行为建模为多个组成机制（constituent mechanisms）的系综，通过机制激活、状态演化与宏观可观测量的概率连接来理解涌现行为（emergent behavior）。以金属疲劳裂纹扩展为例，该框架将损伤容限（damage tolerance）重新表述为机制竞争的推理问题，并为多尺度模拟、多模态表征和机器学习的整合提供了基础。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统材料科学通常将行为视为从结构到性能的确定性映射（deterministic mapping），但许多重要现象源于跨尺度的多机制条件性激活。以金属疲劳为例，裂纹扩展常被建模为单调不可逆过程，而局部微观结构、加载历史和竞争性单元过程实际上会影响扩展、停滞和自愈之间的平衡，现有模型难以刻画这种复杂性。

Method: 提出一个概率框架，将材料行为视为一个由组成机制构成的系综，机制之间的激活、相互作用和演化共同决定涌现结果。该框架以概率方式连接机制激活、状态演化和宏观可观测量的关系。

Result: 在疲劳裂纹扩展案例中，该框架将损伤容限重新表述为关于机制竞争的推理问题（inference problem over mechanism competition），为多尺度模拟、多模态表征和机器学习的整合提供了理论基础。该逻辑可推广至其他物理和化学系统，表明该框架具有可移植性（portable）。

Conclusion: 本文的更大目标是从事后关联结构与性能，转向事先识别使期望涌现行为成为可能条件，从而推动材料科学范式的转变。

Abstract: Materials behavior is often treated as a deterministic mapping from structure to properties, yet many important phenomena emerge from the conditional activation of multiple mechanisms across scales. This is especially evident in fatigue of metals, where crack growth is typically modeled as monotonic and irreversible process, despite evidence that local microstructure, loading history, and competing unit processes can shift the balance among propagation, arrest, and self-healing. Here we present a probabilistic framework that describes materials behavior as an ensemble of constituent mechanisms whose activation, interaction, and evolution determine emergent outcomes. The framework connects mechanism activation, state evolution, and macroscopic observables in a probabilistic way. In the case of fatigue crack propagation, it reframes damage tolerance as an inference problem over mechanism competition and provides a basis for integrating multiscale simulation, multimodal characterization, and machine learning. The same logic extends to other physical and chemical systems suggesting a portable framework for any system in which emergent behavior reflects mechanism competition under changing conditions. The broader ambition of this perspective review is a shift from correlating structure and performance after the fact to identifying, in advance, the conditions that make desired emergent behavior probable.

</details>

### [139] [Determining Critical Temperature Differences of Low-Temperature-Differential Stirling Engines: Nonlinear Dynamics Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26539)
*Momoko Amemiya,Yuki Izumida*

Main category: nlin.AO

TL;DR: 本研究采用非线性动力学（nonlinear dynamics）方法，推导了低温差斯特林发动机（LTD Stirling engines）临界温差的自洽方程，将其解耦为同宿分岔点（homoclinic bifurcation points），从而揭示了决定临界温差的参数组合，建立了超越经验设计的基础性能优化原则。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 低温差斯特林发动机虽能在日常低温环境下运行，但其停止转动的临界温差尚未被确定，这一问题亟待解决。

Method: 采用非线性动力学方法，推导了确定临界温差的自洽方程（self-consistent equations），将其作为动力学模型中同宿分岔点进行分析。

Result: 自洽方程的解揭示了决定临界温差的参数组合，从而明确了发动机停止转动的条件。

Conclusion: 基于所得参数组合，可建立超越经验设计的基础设计原则，用于改进低温差斯特林发动机的性能。

Abstract: While the low-temperature-differential (LTD) Stirling engines are innovative engines that can operate with low temperature differentials in our daily life, the problem of determining the critical temperature differences below which the engine ceases to rotate remains unexplored. In this study, we solve this problem using a nonlinear dynamics approach. We derive the self-consistent equations that determine the critical temperature differences as homoclinic bifurcation points of a dynamical model of the LTD Stirling engines. The solutions of the self-consistent equations reveal a combination of parameters that determines the critical temperature differences. This enables us to establish the fundamental design principles for improving the performance of the LTD Stirling engines beyond empirical designs.

</details>

### [140] [Phantom-divide crossing and suppressed structure growth in kinetically braided dark energy with momentum exchange](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26447)
*Masroor C. Pookkillath,Shinji Tsujikawa*

Main category: astro-ph.CO

TL;DR: 该论文构建了一个线性稳定的标量场模型，该模型同时实现了暗能量状态方程从w_DE<-1到w_DE>-1的向上穿越，以及冷暗物质（CDM）扇区中引力成团效应的减弱，并通过改良的CLASS计算揭示了相关的宇宙学观测特征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在构建一个同时具有两种非平凡行为的理论一致模型：暗能量状态方程（dark-energy equation of state）穿越幻影分界线（phantom divide，即w_DE=-1），以及冷暗物质（CDM）扇区中引力成团（gravitational clustering）的减弱，从而为未来的似然分析（likelihood analysis）提供理论基础。

Method: 采用指数势（exponential potential）破缺移位对称性（shift symmetry），驱动背景从稳定的幻影相（phantom phase）向非幻影相（nonphantom regime）演化；引入纯动量传递相互作用（pure momentum-transfer interaction），在不改变CDM背景稀释律（dilution law）的前提下增加其动力学惯性（dynamical inertia）。推导背景及线性扰动方程，建立无鬼影（no-ghost）和拉普拉斯稳定性（Laplacian-stability）条件，并在准静态近似（quasi-static approximation）下分析有效引力耦合。使用改良的CLASS程序（包含标量场扰动和完整玻尔兹曼层级）进行数值计算。

Result: 在哈勃半径（Hubble radius）内部的深亚视界尺度上，准静态近似下CDM的有效引力耦合（effective gravitational coupling）可降至牛顿常数（Newton's constant）以下，从而抑制晚期结构增长和小尺度物质功率谱；而重子（baryonic）的引力耦合则因伽利略编织（Galileon braiding）效应保持增强。在辐射-物质相等（radiation–matter equality）附近出现瞬态编织（transient braiding）信号。代表性的稳定解显示，物质功率谱在数值可探测的最低波数处增强，CMB温度功率谱在2≤ℓ≤30的多极（multipole）范围内下降，并出现声学尺度（acoustic scale）和首个温度峰值位置的微小偏移。

Conclusion: 该模型为同时实现暗能量状态方程穿越和CDM引力成团减弱提供了一个线性稳定的理论框架，其数值结果展示了可观测的宇宙学特征，为进一步的完整似然分析（full likelihood analysis）铺平了道路。

Abstract: We construct a linearly stable scalar-field model that realizes both an upward crossing of the dark-energy equation of state, from $w_{\rm DE}<-1$ to $w_{\rm DE}>-1$, and weakened gravitational clustering in the cold dark matter (CDM) sector. An exponential potential breaks shift symmetry and drives the background from a stable phantom phase toward the nonphantom regime, while a pure momentum-transfer interaction increases the dynamical inertia of CDM without altering its background dilution law. We derive the background and linear perturbation equations and establish the no-ghost and Laplacian-stability conditions. For perturbations deep inside the Hubble radius, where the quasi-static approximation applies, the effective gravitational coupling for CDM can fall below Newton's constant, suppressing late-time growth and small-scale matter power, while the baryonic coupling remains enhanced by Galileon braiding. A modified CLASS calculation, including the scalar-field perturbation and the full Boltzmann hierarchies, reveals signatures of transient braiding around radiation--matter equality. For the representative stable solutions studied here, these signatures include an enhancement of matter power toward the lowest wavenumbers probed numerically and a reduction of CMB temperature power over the multipole range $2\leq\ell\leq30$. We also find small shifts in the acoustic scale and the position of the first temperature peak. These results motivate a full likelihood analysis of the model.

</details>


### [141] [Non-Minimally Coupled Chain Inflation at High Scales](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27193)
*Miguel Barroso Varela,Orfeu Bertolami,Katherine Freese,Evangelos Sfakianakis*

Main category: astro-ph.CO

TL;DR: 本论文研究了标量场与引力之间的非最小耦合（non-minimal coupling, ξRφ²）对链式暴胀（chain inflation）模型动力学及观测效应的影响，发现该耦合可显著改变隧穿速率，打破标量倾斜与暴胀能标的刚性关联，使原本仅能在极低能标（~3 GeV）成立的模型提升至约10¹¹ GeV的高能标，并产生可被下一代干涉仪探测的随机引力波背景信号。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准慢滚暴胀（slow-roll inflation）并非唯一实现加速膨胀的机制，链式暴胀通过一系列快速量子隧穿事件提供了一种替代方案。在高能标下，标量场通常被预期与引力存在非最小耦合，但这种耦合在链式暴胀中的动力学与观测后果尚未被系统研究。本文旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 论文在爱因斯坦框架（Einstein frame）下分析了非最小耦合对欧几里得反弹作用量（Euclidean bounce action）的场依赖放大效应，从而修正隧穿率。在此基础上，发展了一套解析框架，将该修正的隧穿动力学与标量谱指数（scalar spectral index）、其跑动（running）、原初曲率功率谱（primordial curvature power spectrum）以及来自气泡碰撞的随机引力波背景（stochastic gravitational wave background）联系起来。

Result: 对于ξ=O(10)的情形，非最小耦合打破了最小耦合纯倾斜余弦模型中决定标量倾斜与暴胀能标之间的刚性关系，使原本暴胀势能标度V_*^{1/4}≲3 GeV的模型提升至V_*^{1/4}~10¹¹ GeV。高能标下的非最小耦合链式暴胀在dHz–kHz频段产生具有峰值的随机引力波信号，可被爱因斯坦望远镜（Einstein Telescope）和宇宙探索者（Cosmic Explorer）探测。此外，模型还预言了独特的谱指数跑动，可由西蒙斯天文台（Simons Observatory）检验。

Conclusion: 非最小耦合使链式暴胀成为可行的多信使宇宙学（multi-messenger cosmology）目标，同时为原初引力波和谱指数跑动提供了可检验的预言。

Abstract: Chain inflation offers an alternative to standard slow-roll dynamics, with accelerated expansion proceeding through a sequence of rapid quantum tunneling events between metastable vacua. At the high energy scales relevant for the early Universe, scalar fields are generically expected to couple non-minimally to gravity via operators like $ξRφ^2$, allowed by symmetry and required as counterterms for interacting theories in curved spacetime. We study the dynamical and observational consequences of this coupling for chain inflation. We find the modifications to the model for arbitrary $ξ$ and focus on interesting phenomenology for $ξ={\cal O}( 10)$. We show that, in the Einstein frame, the non-minimal coupling induces a field-dependent amplification of the Euclidean bounce action, thus modifying the tunneling rate across the chain. We develop an analytic framework connecting this modified tunneling dynamics to the scalar spectral index, its running, the primordial curvature power spectrum, and the stochastic gravitational wave background from bubble collisions. As one consequence, the non-minimal coupling breaks the rigid relation between the scalar tilt and inflationary scale that drives the minimally coupled pure tilted cosine model to very low energies ($V_*^{1/4}\lesssim 3\,\rm{GeV}$, where $V_*$ is the value of the inflationary potential when the CMB-relevant modes exit the horizon), allowing for viable high-scale chain inflation with $V_*^{1/4}\sim 10^{11}\,\rm{GeV}$. Furthermore, non-minimally coupled chain inflation at high scales produces a peaked stochastic gravitational wave signal in the dHz-kHz bands, accessible to upcoming interferometers such as the Einstein Telescope and Cosmic Explorer. Finally, the model predicts a distinct running of the spectral index that will be testable by the Simons Observatory, making it a prime target for multi-messenger cosmology.

</details>
