### [142] [Quantum spin-glass criticality in disordered frustrated dimer magnets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26151)
*Darshan G. Joshi,Matthias Vojta*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文通过键算符理论（bond-operator theory）研究三角晶格双层海森堡模型（triangular-lattice bilayer Heisenberg model）中自旋玻璃相（spin-glass phase）与无特征顺磁相（featureless paramagnetic phase）之间的量子相变（quantum phase transition），发现键无序（bond disorder）通过偶极纹理（dipolar textures）的干涉破坏反铁磁序并导致自旋玻璃序，近临界自旋玻璃表现出反常的弱玻璃化特征，且振幅模（Higgs mode）的谱权重因干涉效应被强烈抑制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解Mott绝缘体中自旋玻璃相与顺磁相之间的量子相变机制，特别是在存在键无序（bond disorder）的三角晶格双层海森堡模型中的临界行为与低能激发特性。

Method: 采用键算符理论（bond-operator theory）的变体方法，对三角晶格双层海森堡模型中的键无序效应进行理论分析，计算热力学可观测量（thermodynamic observables）与激发谱（excitation spectra）。

Result: 键无序经由偶极纹理（dipolar textures）的干涉破坏反铁磁序而产生自旋玻璃序；近临界区域中无序导致的强不均匀性抑制非共线序（non-collinearities），产生反常弱的玻璃化行为；低能激发呈现强烈的空间局域化趋势；振幅模（Higgs mode）在玻璃相中的谱权重因干涉效应被强烈抑制。

Conclusion: 键无序驱动的自旋玻璃-二聚体量子相变（spin-glass to dimer quantum phase transition）在近临界区域表现出独特的低能局域化与弱玻璃化特征，振幅模的谱权重抑制是干涉效应的重要标志。

Abstract: We study quantum phase transitions of Mott insulators between spin-glass and featureless paramagnetic phases. Specifically, we consider a triangular-lattice bilayer Heisenberg model with bond disorder. The clean system has two phases, a dimer quantum paramagnet and a non-collinear antiferromagnet, separated by a quantum critical point. Bond disorder destroys the antiferromagnetic phase via the interference of dipolar textures and results in spin-glass order, such that the system features a quantum phase transition between spin-glass and dimer phases. We study the vicinity of this transition using a variant of bond-operator theory and calculate thermodynamic observables as well as excitation spectra. Bond disorder leads to strong inhomogeneities near the transition which suppresses non-collinearities and leads to anomalously weak glassiness in the near-critical quantum spin glass. We characterize the low-energy excitations which have a strong tendency towards spatial localization, and we track the behavior of the amplitude (i.e. Higgs) mode across the glass phase whose spectral weight we find to be strongly suppressed due to interference effects.

</details>


### [143] [Hidden symmetry at the diabolical points of a biaxial spin](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26308)
*Shadan Ghassemi Tabrizi*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本论文证明，在旋转框架下，双轴自旋哈密顿量（biaxial spin Hamiltonian）可映射为有限紧束缚链（tight-binding chain）。退化点（diabolical point）仅出现在磁场切断该链的位置；在Kececioglu与Garg发现的每个精确退化格点处，链在两个不同旋转框架的两个独立键上被双重切断。这些切断由对易哈密顿量但彼此不对易的投影算子承载，实现了Garg预言的隐藏对称性。由这些算子构造的单一配对算子决定了退化能级的配对关系及其多重性，并导出一个显式负行列式固定每个锥体的取向，使得每一退化点的低能级携带Chern荷（Chern charge）-1。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解释双轴自旋哈密顿量中精确退化格点的起源及其隐藏对称性（hidden symmetry）的机制，并澄清此前连续性及拓扑论证的不足。

Method: 将旋转框架下的双轴自旋哈密顿量映射为有限紧束缚链，利用链中跃迁振幅（hopping amplitude）随外磁场调制的性质，分析磁场切断链的位置与退化点之间的关系。通过投影算子（projector）构造对易但不互相对易的对称算子，揭示隐藏对称性；进一步利用单一配对算子确定能级配对及多重性，并导出负行列式固定锥体取向。

Result: 在每个精确退化点，链在两个不同旋转框架的两个独立键上被双重切断；投影算子承载该双重切断并实现隐藏对称性。单一配对算子的秩给出每个退化格点的多重性，取代了此前的连续性论证。显式负行列式固定了每个锥体的取向，使退化点低能级携带Chern荷-1。

Conclusion: 该模型退化点的结构可由紧束缚链的切断及投影算子对称性完全刻画，所有退化点的低能级均携带确定的Chern荷-1，为理解量子退化的拓扑性质提供了精确代数框架。

Abstract: In a rotated frame the biaxial spin Hamiltonian $k_1S_x^2+k_2S_y^2-\mathbf{h}\cdot\mathbf{S}$ is a finite tight-binding chain whose hopping amplitudes are tuned by the applied field. A chain with no vanishing hopping has a nondegenerate spectrum, so a degeneracy can occur only where the field severs the chain. We show that at every point of the exact diabolical-point lattice found by Kececioglu and Garg the chain is severed twice over, in two different rotated frames and at two bonds that are fixed independently. The two severings are carried by projectors that commute with the Hamiltonian but not with each other. In that form they realize the hidden symmetry anticipated by Garg. A single operator built from them pairs the degenerate levels; its rank gives the multiplicity of every lattice point, replacing an earlier continuity and topological argument. Because the two partners of a doublet occupy disjoint stretches of the chain, an exact and manifestly negative determinant fixes the orientation of every cone. At every degeneracy of the model the lower level therefore carries Chern charge -1 in the convention used here.

</details>


### [144] [Quantum Geometry-Driven RKKY: From Flat to Dispersive Bands](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26516)
*Zhenggang Zhou,Chuang Li,Lun-Hui Hu*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文发现平带系统中的 RKKY 交换作用可以违背“量子度量（quantum metric）唯一决定空间关联尺度”的预期：在孤立填充平带中，交换通过虚带间跃迁进行，其衰减长度由平带几何与带隙共同决定；在 Chern 平带中，该长度随量子度量长度呈非单调变化；当引入色散时，几何选择效应可使 RKKY 尾部由 $1/R^2$ 变为 $1/R^3$。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有研究表明在平带系统中，量子度量（quantum metric）会约束超流权重（superfluid weight）和相干长度（coherence length）等物理可观测量，暗示存在一个单一的几何尺度决定空间关联。本文旨在检验这一预期是否普遍成立，尤其是在孤立填充平带的 RKKY 交换过程中。

Method: 作者从理论上分析了孤立填充平带中 RKKY 交换作用的微扰机制：由于带内通道缺失，交换通过虚带间跃迁（virtual interband transitions）发生，其核（kernel）由实空间平带投影子与空带投影子的逆带隙加权乘积给出。通过解析延拓动量空间投影子至复动量平面，分析了核的指数衰减行为及其特征长度。

Result: RKKY 交换的衰减长度由解析延拓后的投影子在复动量平面中最接近的奇点决定，因而同时依赖于平带几何和带隙。在 Chern 平带中，小带隙下该衰减长度随量子度量长度呈非单调变化：先减小后增大。引入色散后，在门电压调控下，反对极点（antipodal Fermi points）处布洛赫态（Bloch states）的重叠可被强制为零，产生 $1/R^3$ 的 RKKY 尾部，替代传统的 $1/R^2$。

Conclusion: 本工作表明平带系统中的量子度量并非空间关联尺度的唯一决定因素；RKKY 交换的衰减长度是平带几何与带隙共同作用的结果。此外，几何选择效应（geometric selection effect）可以在色散系统中改变 RKKY 相互作用的幂律行为，拓展了对平带系统中磁交换作用的理解。

Abstract: In flat-band systems, quantum metric bounds physical observables like superfluid weight and coherence length, suggesting a single geometric scale for spatial correlations. Here, we show that the RKKY exchange in an isolated filled flat band can violate this expectation. With the intraband channel absent, the exchange proceeds via virtual interband transitions across the gap; the kernel becomes the inverse-gap-weighted trace product of the real-space flat-band projector and empty-band projectors. Because the corresponding momentum-space projectors are analytic, the kernel decays exponentially, with a decay length $ξ_\text{RKKY}$ set by the closest singularities of the analytically continued projectors in the complex momentum plane. Thus, this length depends on both the flat-band geometry and the band gap. Applying this formalism to Chern flat-band systems, we find that for small gaps, $ξ_\text{RKKY}$ depends non-monotonically on the quantum metric length: it first decreases, then increases, revealing that stronger quantum geometry can shorten the magnetic exchange range. Upon restoring dispersion, a nontrivial inversion representation can force the overlap between Bloch states at antipodal Fermi points to vanish under gate tuning, producing a $1/R^3$ RKKY tail instead of the conventional $1/R^2$---a geometric selection effect.

</details>


### [145] [Algebraic Diagrammatic Construction of the Multichannel Dyson Equation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26609)
*Thibault Demartini,J. Arjan Berger,Guillaume Blanchon,Thomas Duguet,Denis Lacroix,Pina Romaniello,Vittorio Somà*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文阐明了多通道戴森方程（multichannel Dyson equation, MCDE）近似的物理内容，证明其介于ADC(2)和ADC(3)截断之间，并通过一维哈伯德模型（Hubbard model）数值测试，展示其在准粒子峰（quasiparticle peaks）和卫星峰（satellites）描述上优于ADC(2)。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Riva等人（Phys. Rev. Lett. 131, 216401, 2023）近期提出了多通道戴森方程（multichannel Dyson equation, MCDE）作为多体系统中单体格林函数（one-body Green function）的新近似方案，但该方案的物理内容尚需进一步澄清。

Method: 从代数图解构造（algebraic diagrammatic construction, ADC）截断方案的扩展版本出发，推导并恢复了MCDE近似，从而将其定位在ADC(2)与ADC(3)截断之间。随后在周期性一维哈伯德模型（one-dimensional Hubbard model）的4、6、8格点晶格上进行了数值测试。

Result: MCDE近似在光谱强度分布（spectral strength distribution）中，对准粒子峰（quasiparticle peaks）和所谓的卫星峰（satellites）的处理均优于ADC(2)截断。

Conclusion: MCDE近似位于ADC(2)与ADC(3)之间，是一种新的有效逼近方案，在多体系统的格林函数计算中能提供比ADC(2)更优的光谱描述。

Abstract: The multichannel Dyson equation (MCDE) was recently introduced as a new approximation scheme to compute the one-body Green function in many-body systems, as reported by Riva et al. in Physical Review Letters, volume 131, article 216401, published in 2023. The physical content of this novel approximation scheme is further clarified by recovering it from an extended version of the algebraic diagrammatic construction (ADC) truncation scheme. It is thus demonstrated that the MCDE approximation lies in between the so-called ADC(2) and ADC(3) truncations of the dynamical self energy. Building on this clarification, the MCDE approximation is tested on the periodic one-dimensional Hubbard model with 4, 6, and 8 site lattices and shown to deliver an improved treatment over ADC(2) of both the quasiparticle peaks and the so-called satellites in the spectral strength distribution.

</details>


### [146] [Disentangling bulk and surface states in the electronic structure of PtBi$_2$(0001)](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26804)
*Stefanie Suzanne Brinkman,Xin Liang Tan,Anders Christian Mathisen,Fabian Göhler,Øyvind Finnseth,Chul-Hee Min,Grigory Shipunov,Falk Pabst,Manuel Alonso Lemos,Balasubramanian Thiagarajan,Craig Polley,Masashi Arita,Kenya Shimada,Anna Isaeva,Jorge I. Facio,Hendrik Bentmann*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本研究通过角分辨光电子能谱（ARPES）实验和第一性原理计算（first-principles calculations），系统解析了三方晶系PtBi₂的体态和表面能带结构，识别了两种不同表面终止面上的多种表面态，并建立了与实验吻合良好的表面电子结构图像。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近期关于三方晶系PtBi₂中存在表面局域拓扑超导（surface-localized topological superconductivity）的实验报道突显了理解其表面电子结构的重要性，因此有必要对PtBi₂的表面能带进行系统研究。

Method: 采用角分辨光电子能谱（angle-resolved photoemission spectroscopy, ARPES）实验，结合基于光子能量和偏振依赖的测量手段，将体态色散与表面态色散分开；同时辅以第一性原理计算（first-principles calculations）进行对比分析。

Result: 在PtBi₂(0001)的两种不同表面终止面上均识别出多种表面态，实验与计算结果吻合良好；通过计算分析了表面态和体态的轨道组成（orbital composition），并与偏振依赖的ARPES测量结果进行了比较。

Conclusion: 研究结果共同提供了PtBi₂在两种表面终止面上一致的表面电子结构图像，为理解其表面局域拓扑超导特性奠定了基础。

Abstract: Recent reports of surface-localized topological superconductivity in trigonal PtBi$_2$ highlight the importance of understanding its surface electronic structure. We investigate the bulk and surface band structure of PtBi$_2$ using angle-resolved photoemission spectroscopy (ARPES) and first-principles calculations. Through photon-energy- and polarization-dependent measurements, we disentangle bulk dispersions from surface states on the two distinct surface terminations of PtBi$_2$(0001). For both terminations, we assign several different surface states and find good agreement between experiment and calculations. Based on our calculations, we analyze the orbital composition in the surface and bulk bands and compare the results to polarization-dependent ARPES measurements. Together, our results provide a coherent picture of the surface electronic structure of PtBi$_2$ across both surface terminations.

</details>


### [147] [Electronic and magnetic properties of small one-dimensional Wigner crystals from an ab initio approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26858)
*Daniele Lagasco,Jan Arjan Berger*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种从头算（ab initio）方法，用于研究小尺寸一维维格纳晶体（one-dimensional Wigner crystals）的电子与磁性质。该方法通过在一维无限深势阱中约束电子，以粒子在盒中（particle-in-a-box）波函数为单粒子基，采用全组态相互作用（full configuration interaction）捕获电子关联，从而准确得到电子密度分布和交换耦合常数（exchange coupling constant），并在一维维格纳二聚体（dimer）上取得了与文献一致的结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 该研究受实验中在碳纳米管中观测到少电子维格纳晶体的推动，旨在发展一种从头算理论方法，以准确描述此类系统的基本电子和磁性质，特别是电子电荷分布与交换耦合常数。

Method: 将电子约束在一维无限深势阱中以模拟实验装置；在斯莱特行列式（Slater determinants）基下表示哈密顿量，并进行全组态相互作用（full configuration interaction）以完整捕获电子关联；采用粒子在盒中（particle-in-a-box）波函数作为单粒子基集，自动满足边界条件；最后通过将从头算方法映射到海森堡哈密顿量（Heisenberg Hamiltonian）来提取交换耦合常数。

Result: 该方法能够准确得到小尺寸一维维格纳晶体的电子密度分布，清晰地展示了电子的局域化特征；在维格纳二聚体上计算得到的交换耦合常数与文献结果高度吻合。

Conclusion: 所提出的从头算方法能够有效且准确地描述一维维格纳晶体的电子局域化及磁交换相互作用，其有效性通过与文献结果的对比得到了验证。

Abstract: We present an \emph{ab initio} method to study the electronic and magnetic properties of small one-dimensional Wigner crystals. In particular, we focus on the calculation of the electronic charge distribution and the exchange coupling constant. Our theoretical studies are motivated by the experimental observation of few-electron Wigner crystals in a carbon nanotube [Science 364, 870 (2019)]. We model the experimental setup by confining electrons in a one-dimensional potential well with infinite side barriers. We represent the Hamiltonian of the system in a basis of Slater determinants and perform full configuration interaction to ensure we capture all the electron correlation for a given basis set. As the one-particle basis set we use particle-in-a-box wave functions which by construction satisfy the boundary conditions. With our approach, we obtain accurate electronic density profiles of small one-dimensional Wigner crystals. These profiles clearly show the localisation of the electrons. Finally, we present a simple approach to obtain the exchange coupling constant by mapping our \textit{ab initio} method on a Heisenberg Hamiltonian. We illustrate our approach on a Wigner dimer. We obtain excellent agreement with a result in the literature.

</details>


### [148] [Microscopic Emergence of Ancilla Lattice Physics in the Emery Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26869)
*Mikołaj Walicki,Johannes Knolle,Krzysztof Wohlfeld*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文针对Lieb晶格上的三带Emery模型，在填充数n=2附近推导出一个可控的低能有效理论，通过一系列Schrieffer–Wolff变换建立了到辅助晶格（ancillary lattice）结构的微观映射，并系统分析了常规费米液体（FL）与非常规分数量子化费米液体（FL*）相的实现条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究者希望建立多轨道材料中辅助物理（ancilla physics）的微观基础，并澄清在Lieb晶格Emery模型中实现非常规分数量子化费米液体（FL*）相所需的具体条件。

Method: 从正电荷转移（positive charge-transfer）区间出发，通过一系列Schrieffer–Wolff变换，将三带Emery模型严格映射到一个辅助晶格（ancillary lattice）结构上，从而推导出可控的低能有效理论。

Result: 在类似铜氧化物（cuprate）的参数下，该框架自然地再现了每个格点电子数偏移一个的常规费米液体（FL）相；实现FL*相需要非标准的铜氧化物参数区间以及额外的相互作用来稳定背景辅助层中的量子自旋液体（quantum spin liquid）。

Conclusion: 这些结果为多轨道材料中辅助物理（ancilla physics）提供了微观基础，并明确了实现FL*相的条件；研究还讨论了在Lieb晶格冷原子模拟中复现辅助模型物理的可能性。

Abstract: We derive a controlled low-energy effective theory for the three-band Emery model on the Lieb lattice near (n=2) filling. Starting from the positive charge-transfer regime, a sequence of Schrieffer--Wolff transformations rigorously establishes a direct microscopic mapping onto an ancillary lattice structure. For realistic cuprate-like parameters, this framework naturally reproduces the conventional Fermi-liquid (FL) phase at a filling shifted by one electron per site. Our analysis identifies the precise microscopic conditions required to access the exotic fractionalized Fermi-liquid (FL$^*$) phase. We show that realizing FL$^*$ requires nonstandard cuprate parameter regimes together with additional interactions that stabilize a quantum spin liquid in the background ancilla layer. These results establish a microscopic foundation for ancilla physics in multiorbital materials and clarify the conditions for realizing FL$^*$. We discuss the possibility to replicate ancilla model physics in Lieb lattice cold atom simulations.

</details>


### [149] [The electric field gradient tensor as a symmetry-adapted order parameter in Landau theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26934)
*L. Scalise,A. W. Carbonari*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本研究将电场梯度（electric field gradient, EFG）系统地纳入朗道理论（Landau theory），通过群论分解与诱导方法，建立了EFG在结构相变和电子相变中响应的对称性分类框架，并通过五十年来的四极矩实验数据和第一性原理计算验证了该框架的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 四极矩超精细谱学（包括NQR、NMR、TDPAC和穆斯堡尔谱学）长期将核位点处的电场梯度（EFG）作为结构相变与电子相变序参量的经验代理，但EFG从未被系统地纳入朗道理论（Landau theory）框架中，缺乏严格的对称性理论基础。

Method: 将EFG定义为晶格位点上严格无迹的对称二阶张量（rank-2 tensor），通过位点对称群（site-symmetry group）分解并在Wyckoff轨道上诱导，确定其在母空间群（parent space group）中的不可约表示（irreducible representation）内容。在此基础上，依据对称性区分主响应（线性）和次响应（二次）通道，建立可证伪的EFG响应分类。

Result: 框架被五十年来的四极矩实验所验证，再现了临界指数（critical exponent）、一级相变不连续性以及零结果（null result）。第一性原理计算满足所预测的宇称定理和零定理。α-石英的全电子计算确认了轨道选择规则：仅与软模不可约表示变换一致的EFG组合随畸变振幅线性变化，正交组合则被抑制两个数量级。

Conclusion: 该框架提供了EFG响应的可证伪分类（主响应、次响应与禁止响应），并为BaFe₂As₂中⁷⁵As位点在向列相变（nematic transition）中的行为提出了五项可证伪预测，其中包括一项此前未被提出的零结果。

Abstract: Quadrupolar hyperfine spectroscopies, including Nuclear Quadrupole Resonance (NQR), Nuclear Magnetic Resonance (NMR), Time-Differential Perturbed Angular Correlations (TDPAC), and Mössbauer spectroscopy, have long used the electric field gradient (EFG) at a nuclear site as an empirical proxy for order parameters in structural and electronic phase transitions, yet the EFG has never been systematically incorporated into Landau theory. Here we provide that framework. The EFG is an exactly traceless, symmetric rank-2 tensor defined at a crystallographic site. Decomposing it under the site-symmetry group and inducing over the Wyckoff orbit determines its irreducible representation content in the parent space group. Whenever the representation of a zone-center transition is present, symmetry requires the corresponding EFG combination to vanish above the transition and grow linearly with the order parameter below it, inheriting its critical exponent, sign, and domain structure. Symmetry-orthogonal channels are quadratic, recovering the classic empirical relations. This yields a falsifiable classification of primary, secondary, and forbidden EFG responses. The framework is validated against five decades of quadrupolar experiments, reproducing critical exponents, first-order discontinuities, and a null result, and by first-principles calculations satisfying the predicted parity and zero theorems. All-electron calculations for $α$-quartz confirm the orbit-selection rule: only the EFG combination transforming as the soft-mode irreducible representation varies linearly with distortion amplitude, while the orthogonal combination remains suppressed by two orders of magnitude. A proposed study of the $^{75}$As site across the nematic transition in BaFe$_2$As$_2$ provides five falsifiable predictions, including a previously unstated null result.

</details>


### [150] [Interactions between structural and magnetic domains through the Verwey transition in stoichiometric and doped magnetite](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27020)
*Mateusz Ślęzak,Zbigniew Kąkol,Ryszard Zalecki,Andrzej Kozłowski,Naveen Kumar Chogondahalli Muniraju,Neven Barišić,Stuart Gilder,Wojciech Tabiś*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文通过测量磁铁矿在Verwey相变（$T_{\rm V}\approx 120$ K）过程中，在不同外加磁场条件下的磁响应，系统研究了其磁化强度在加热通过相变温度时的变化机理。研究发现，磁场诱导的单斜$c$轴重新取向可能使相变展宽，主要发生在化学计量比的磁铁矿中，从而有助于阐明Verwey相变机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 磁铁矿（magnetite）在Verwey相变温度（$T_{\rm V}\approx 120$ K）附近的磁响应机制尚未完全阐明，例如交流磁化率（alternating-current susceptibility）随加热通过$T_{\rm V}$时升高，但磁化强度（magnetization）可能增大或减小。由于$T_{\rm V}$处涉及多种相互交织的过程，需要一个多角度的磁学变化视图来澄清这些过程的本质，并将相变的特定特征确立为诊断工具。

Method: 本文测量了磁铁矿在加热通过$T_{\rm V}$时，在广泛外加磁场（applied-field）条件下的磁响应。研究样品包括化学计量比的合成单晶、掺杂（Zn、Ti、Al）合成单晶以及天然多晶样品（polycrystalline sample），旨在解释磁化强度随温度变化通过$T_{\rm V}$的方式及其原因。

Result: 研究发现，磁场诱导的单斜$c$轴（monoclinic $c$ axis）重新取向可能使相变展宽（broaden the transition），该效应主要出现在化学计量比的磁铁矿中。

Conclusion: 上述发现有助于阐明Verwey相变（Verwey transition）的机制。

Abstract: The magnetic response of magnetite as it cycles through the Verwey transition at $T_{\rm V}\approx 120$ K remains incompletely understood, despite numerous studies of both structural and magnetic domains. For example, although the alternating-current susceptibility increases upon heating through the Verwey temperature $T_{\rm V}$, the magnetization may either increase or decrease. Since many intertwined processes occur at $T_{\rm V}$, a multifaceted view of the magnetic changes may help clarify the nature of these processes and establish specific features of the transition as diagnostic tools. To this end, we report measurements of the magnetic response of magnetite upon heating through $T_{\rm V}$ under a wide range of applied-field conditions. We studied synthetic stoichiometric and doped (Zn, Ti, and Al) single crystals, as well as a natural polycrystalline sample, with the aim of explaining how and why the magnetization changes as a function of temperature through $T_{\rm V}$. The magnetic-field-induced reorientation of the monoclinic $c$ axis may broaden the transition, primarily in stoichiometric magnetite. These findings help clarify the mechanism of the transition.

</details>


### [151] [Interaction-induced sign reversal of the orbital magnetic susceptibility in Chern insulators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27112)
*Ke Huang,Xiao Li*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文证明，相互作用电子体系（interacting electrons）的轨道磁化率（orbital magnetic susceptibility）不能简单通过将单粒子公式（single-particle formula）应用于自洽HF能带（self-consistent HF bands）来正确计算，特别在拓扑系统（topological systems）如扭曲MoTe₂的Chern绝缘相中，相互作用诱导的修正会反转磁化率的符号。作者发展了一套代数框架，通过“反向Peierls替代”（reverse Peierls substitution）将有限磁场下的HF自洽问题转化为函数方程，从而系统计算了相互作用的贡献。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有研究表明，轨道磁化（orbital magnetization）可通过单粒子公式直接计算，但轨道磁化率（orbital magnetic susceptibility）在拓扑系统中的行为尚不明确。本文旨在澄清这一差异，并揭示相互作用对磁化率的本质修正。

Method: 提出“反向Peierls替代”（reverse Peierls substitution），将每个磁平移不变算符（magnetic-translation-invariant operator）映射为唯一的二元函数，从而将有限磁场下的HF自洽性转化为可系统展开的函数方程。在一阶展开下，得到关于Fock势场致变化δX的线性方程，从而分解出类单粒子贡献和本征相互作用诱导的磁化率。采用辅助希尔伯特空间（auxiliary Hilbert space）构造将此框架推广至周期和莫尔系统（moiré systems）。

Result: 在扭曲MoTe₂的Chern绝缘相中，相互作用诱导的修正使磁化率符号反转，表明单粒子公式在此情形定性失效。该框架在绝缘体中复现了Středa公式（Středa formula），并在有能隙的Dirac模型（gapped Dirac model）和扭曲MoTe₂ Chern绝缘体上的有限场HF计算中定量验证了理论。

Conclusion: 轨道磁化率不能简单继承轨道磁化的计算方式，必须纳入相互作用诱导的修正，尤其在拓扑系统中此修正可能改变符号。本文发展的代数框架为有限磁场下HF问题的处理提供了系统性方法，并适用于周期和莫尔系统。

Abstract: It has been well established that the orbital magnetization of interacting electrons can be simply evaluated by applying the single-particle formula to self-consistent HF bands. However, we show that such procedure fails qualitatively for orbital magnetic susceptibility, especially in topological systems: in a Chern-insulating phase of twisted MoTe$_2$, the interaction-induced correction reverses the sign of the susceptibility. This result follows from an algebraic framework that solves the HF problem at finite magnetic field, where noncommuting canonical momenta obstruct a direct calculation: a \emph{reverse Peierls substitution} maps every magnetic-translation-invariant operator to a unique bivariate function, converting the finite-field self-consistency into a function equation that can be expanded systematically in $B$. At first order, this yields a linear equation for the field-induced change $δX$ of the Fock potential, leading to an intrinsically interaction-induced susceptibility in addition to a single-particle-like one. The framework reproduces the Středa formula for insulators, and an auxiliary-Hilbert-space construction carries it to periodic and moiré systems. Finite-field HF calculations in a gapped Dirac model and in the twisted-MoTe$_2$ Chern insulator confirm the theory quantitatively.

</details>


### [152] [Direct minimization versus iterative embedding in the ghost-Gutzwiller method: a comparative study of magnetism in Mott insulators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27156)
*Antonio Maria Tagliente,Ivan Pasqua,Michele Fabrizio*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文系统比较了幽灵-古茨威勒方法（ghost-Gutzwiller method）中两种求解方式——迭代嵌入方案（iterative embedding）与直接变分最小化（direct variational minimization）——在处理对称性不变莫特绝缘体（symmetry-invariant Mott insulator）时的行为差异，发现两者虽形式等价但在实际计算中效果迥异，并明确了各自的适用条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在迭代量子嵌入方法（iterative quantum embedding methods）中，准确描述假设的对称性不变莫特绝缘体一直是一个长期挑战。本文旨在针对幽灵-古茨威勒方法中的这一问题，探究两种形式等价但实际表现不同的求解方式背后的局限性。

Method: 本研究基于单带哈伯德模型（single-band Hubbard model）的莫特相变（Mott transition），对比了幽灵-古茨威勒方法中的两种求解途径：其一是类似于动力学平均场论（dynamical mean-field theory, DMFT）的迭代嵌入方案；其二是直接对变分能量泛函（variational energy functional）进行最小化。

Result: 迭代方案计算高效但较为脆弱，在莫特相（Mott phase）中需要特殊处理（ad-hoc recipes），且在外加塞曼场（Zeeman field）下失效，导致能量不连续并出现虚假的全极化绝缘体（fully-polarized insulator）。直接最小化避免了这些伪影，能够稳定得到真正的顺磁解（paramagnetic solution）。当允许对称性破缺（如反铁磁相）时，迭代方案则可正确求解，与动力学平均场论结果高度一致。

Conclusion: 研究界定了迭代嵌入方案可被信赖的条件，以及必须改用直接最小化求解的情形，为幽灵-古茨威勒方法的正确使用提供了明确的指导准则。

Abstract: Accurately describing a hypothetical symmetry-invariant Mott insulator presents a long-standing ing challenge in iterative quantum embedding methods. We address this issue within the ghost- Gutzwiller method, which can be solved either through an iterative embedding scheme, analogous to dynamical mean-field theory, or by directly minimizing its variational energy functional. Across the Mott transition of the single-band Hubbard model, these formally equivalent approaches behave very differently: the iterative scheme is computationally efficient but fragile, necessitating ad-hoc recipes in the Mott phase that fail in a Zeeman field, leading to a discontinuous energy and a spurious fully-polarized insulator. Direct minimization avoids these artifacts, stabilizing a genuinely paramagnetic solution. Conversely, when symmetry breaking is allowed, as in an antiferromagnetic phase, the iterative scheme yields the correct solution, closely aligning with dynamical mean-field theory. Our findings delineate the conditions under which the iterative embedding can be trusted and when direct minimization is instead required.

</details>


### [153] [Quantum-Geometric Raman Response in Multiorbital Flat-Band Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27200)
*Wai Ting Tai,Martin Claassen*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文发展了一套非弹性光散射（inelastic light scattering）中平带系统的完整理论，证明量子几何张量（quantum geometric tensor）即使在严格平带极限下也能产生非零的拉曼散射顶点（Raman vertex），从而将量子几何确立为平带系统中非弹性光散射的内禀贡献。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 平带材料（flat-band materials）承载丰富的集体现象，但其在非弹性光散射中的完整理论尚缺失。朴素理论认为在色散消失的极限下拉曼散射顶点（Raman scattering vertices）会恒为零，这一不完整的认识亟需修正。

Method: 发展了一套系统的微扰理论（perturbative theory），在光子能量远离带间跃迁（interband transitions）共振的极限下处理平带的拉曼散射。将带间库仑散射（interband Coulomb scattering）与光-物质耦合（light-matter coupling）平等处理，将拉曼散射顶点分解为与相互作用无关的几何项（由量子几何张量直接表达）以及由虚带间库仑散射产生的有效共振与非共振项。

Result: 证明了在严格平带极限下，虚带间过程（virtual interband processes）通过量子几何张量产生有限的亚能隙拉曼顶点（subgap Raman vertex）。框架与完整的多轨道计算在大光子失谐（large photon detuning）下定量一致。

Conclusion: 量子几何是平带系统中非弹性光散射的内禀贡献；偏振分辨拉曼光谱（polarization-resolved Raman spectroscopy）可作为探测关联平带平台（correlated flat-band platforms）集体激发（collective excitations）的量子几何敏感探针。

Abstract: Flat-band materials host rich collective phenomena, yet a complete theory of their signatures in inelastic light scattering remains lacking. While naive theories of interacting flat bands would predict that Raman scattering vertices vanish identically in the limit of vanishing dispersion, we show that this picture is incomplete upon including the multiorbital character of such systems. We show that virtual interband processes generate a finite subgap Raman vertex controlled by the quantum geometric tensor even in the strict flat-band limit. We develop a systematic perturbative theory for Raman scattering from flat bands in the limit where the photon energy is far from resonance with interband transitions. Treating interband Coulomb scattering and light-matter coupling on equal footing, we decompose the Raman scattering vertices into an interaction-independent geometric term expressible directly in terms of the quantum geometric tensor, together with effective resonant and non-resonant pieces generated by virtual interband Coulomb scattering. We then study the polarization-resolved Raman response from collective excitations of an interacting flat band with nontrivial quantum geometry, and demonstrate quantitative agreement of our framework with a full multi-orbital calculation at large photon detuning from interband transitions. These results establish quantum geometry as an intrinsic contribution to inelastic light scattering in flat-band systems, and suggest polarization-resolved Raman spectroscopy as a quantum geometry-sensitive probe of the collective excitations of correlated flat-band platforms.

</details>

### [154] [On the Betti numbers, Poincaré polynomials, and Euler characteristics of $\overline{\mathcal M}_{0,n}$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26755)
*Giordano Cotti*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 本文重新审视了稳定 n 点有理曲线 Deligne-Mumford 模空间 $\overline{\mathcal M}_{0,n}$ 的庞加莱多项式、贝蒂数和欧拉示性数。作者给出了 Aluffi-Marcolli-Nascimento 与 Eur-Ferroni-Matherne-Pagaria-Vecchi 两种闭式公式的初等推导，证明它们隐含于 Getzler-Manin 的生成级数结果中。此外，还通过完全贝尔多项式与 Lambert W 函数得到了欧拉示性数的新线性递推关系、Hessenberg 行列式表示以及完整渐近展开。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为 $\overline{\mathcal M}_{0,n}$ 的庞加莱多项式提供更简洁的初等推导，揭示两种新闭式公式与经典生成级数结果之间的内在联系，并进一步获得欧拉示性数的新结构表达与渐近估计。

Method: 运用生成函数（generating function）的基本操作、二项式级数（binomial series）以及斯特林数（Stirling numbers）的标准恒等式，从 Getzler-Manin 的生成级数中提取两种闭式公式。对于欧拉示性数，利用 Lambert W 函数（Lambert $W$-function）的泰勒展开与完全贝尔多项式（complete Bell polynomials）建立表示，并推导 Hessenberg 行列式公式与渐近展开。

Result: 给出了两种庞加莱多项式闭式公式的初等推导；获得了与这些多项式相关的精细不变量（区分求和项与双变量细化）的线性递推关系；得到了两个此前未记录形式的贝蒂数公式。欧拉示性数方面，建立了完全贝尔多项式表示，导出了与 Keel-Manin 二次递推不同的新线性递推以及 Hessenberg 行列式公式，并得到了完整的渐近展开，改进了 Manin-Zagier 的渐近估计。

Conclusion: 两种新庞加莱多项式公式均可回溯到 Getzler-Manin 经典结果；Lambert W 函数与贝尔多项式为欧拉示性数提供了新的代数结构，并导出了新的递推关系与完整渐近展开。

Abstract: In this paper, we revisit the Poincaré polynomials, Betti numbers, and Euler characteristics of the Deligne-Mumford moduli spaces $\overline{\mathcal M}_{0,n}$ of stable $n$-pointed rational curves. We give elementary derivations of two recent closed formulas for their Poincaré polynomials, due respectively to Aluffi-Marcolli-Nascimento (arXiv:2406.13095) and to Eur-Ferroni-Matherne-Pagaria-Vecchi (arXiv:2504.16776). Our approach shows that both formulas are already implicit in the generating-series results of Getzler and Manin, and can be extracted from them by elementary manipulations of generating functions, the binomial series, and standard identities for Stirling numbers. Beyond these new derivations, the same method also yields new linear recurrence relations for refined invariants associated with these Poincaré polynomials, namely distinguished summands and a bivariate refinement. As a further consequence, we obtain two additional formulas for the Betti numbers, not previously recorded in this form.
  We also study the Euler characteristics $χ(\overline{\mathcal M}_{0,n})$. Using the Taylor expansion of a suitable branch of the Lambert $W$-function, we show that their sequence is obtained by evaluating complete Bell polynomials at an explicit auxiliary integer sequence. This Bell-polynomial representation yields Hessenberg determinantal formulas and a new linear recursion, distinct from the well-known quadratic Keel-Manin recursion. It also provides an explicit extraction of the Euler characteristics from the Lambert $W$-function expression considered by Aluffi-Marcolli-Nascimento. Finally, we refine the Manin-Zagier asymptotic estimate for these Euler characteristics by computing the full asymptotic expansion.

</details>

### [155] [Role of particle density in the Hall response of synthetic fermionic ladders: Lifshitz and Meissner-vortex transitions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26615)
*Matteo Ferraretto,Matteo Acciai,Massimo Capone*

Main category: cond-mat.quant-gas

TL;DR: 本文研究了非相互作用费米子M腿梯子在人工磁通下的霍尔响应（Hall response），重点分析了霍尔失衡（Hall imbalance）随密度和磁通的变化，揭示了与能带结构Lifshitz相变（Lifshitz transition）的关联，并探讨了与Meissner-涡旋相变的联系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在包含合成维度（synthetic dimension）的一维光学晶格中，人工磁通可实现费米子梯子的霍尔效应研究。此前研究多关注密度无关的霍尔响应，而本文旨在揭示较大人工磁通下霍尔失衡对密度的显著依赖性及其物理机制。

Method: 采用非相互作用费米子M腿梯子模型，结合人工磁通，通过解析和数值方法分析霍尔失衡随粒子密度和磁通的变化；在腿间耦合较大的极限下，解析得到了霍尔失衡消失或变号的特定磁通条件，并基于能带结构中费米点数（Fermi points）随磁通和密度变化的Lifshitz相变解释这一行为。

Result: 发现较大人工磁通下霍尔失衡表现出密度依赖性，与先前报道的密度无关行为形成对比：在粒子（或空穴）密度极低时，霍尔失衡可显著增强；在特定磁通下，霍尔失衡可消失或变号。此外，计算了位点分辨的腿电流和梯级电流，揭示了费米子梯子中Meissner-涡旋相变与玻色子情形的异同。

Conclusion: 霍尔失衡的密度依赖性源于能带的Lifshitz相变，该相变改变了费米点数；费米子梯子的Meissner-涡旋相变在与玻色子对应类比中既有相似性也有差异，为冷原子实验中探测霍尔效应和相变提供了新的可观测物理量。

Abstract: We characterize the Hall response of non-interacting fermionic $M$-leg ladders in the presence of an artificial magnetic flux, that can be realized in one-dimensional optical lattices supplemented with a synthetic dimension.
  We focus on the Hall imbalance, which can be directly measured in experiments with ultracold fermionic atoms. At relatively large synthetic flux we find a dependence of the density that contrasts with previously reported density-independent behavior. In particular, the Hall imbalance can be significantly enhanced in the limit of small density of particles (or holes), or it can vanish and change sign at specific fluxes, which we obtain analytically in the large inter-leg coupling regime. This behavior is explained in terms of Lifshitz transitions of the band structure, where the number of Fermi points changes as a function of the flux and density. Finally, we explore the connection between these transitions and the so-called Meissner-vortex transition for fermionic ladders by computing the site-resolved leg and rung currents and discussing the similarities and differences with the bosonic counterpart.

</details>


### [156] [Critical non-thermal fixed point and the dynamical condensation phase transition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26620)
*Anne-Solène Bornens,Elisabeth Gliott,Nicolas Cherroret*

Main category: cond-mat.quant-gas

TL;DR: 本文通过非微扰量子动理学框架，统一描述了三维玻色气体在冷却淬火（cooling quench）后跨越玻色-爱因斯坦凝聚（Bose-Einstein condensation）相变的远非平衡动力学。研究发现平衡凝聚阈值同时充当动力学临界点，将不同淬火条件下（高于、低于、恰在阈值处）的长时间非平衡演化划分为不同的远非平衡普适类（universality class），并分别由热固定点、粗化固定点及此前未识别的临界固定点所控制，从而建立起凝聚相变的远非平衡对应物。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 三维玻色气体在经历冷却淬火跨越玻色-爱因斯坦凝聚相变后的远非平衡动力学（far-from-equilibrium dynamics）尚缺乏统一的理论描述。作者旨在揭示平衡相变临界点在非平衡演化中的组织作用，并建立不同淬火条件下的动力学标度律。

Method: 采用非微扰量子动理学框架（non-perturbative quantum kinetic framework），通过追踪动量分布（momentum distribution）的时空演化，分析不同淬火条件下的动力学过程。

Result: 平衡凝聚阈值同时充当动力学临界点；高于阈值时表现为单时间尺度弛豫至热固定点（thermal fixed point）；低于阈值时经历从瞬态弱湍流（weak-turbulence regime）到由涡线扩散复合（diffusive recombination of vortex lines）控制的粗化固定点（coarsening fixed point）的交叉；恰在阈值处则由一种此前未识别的临界固定点（critical fixed point）主导，其特征是临界涨落的超扩散传播（superdiffusive spreading）和一组独特的动力学指数。

Conclusion: 所发现的动力学标度律（dynamical scaling laws）构成了凝聚相变的远非平衡对应物（far-from-equilibrium counterpart），其中平衡临界点同时也组织着长时间非平衡动力学。

Abstract: Using a non-perturbative quantum kinetic framework, we develop a unified description of the far-from-equilibrium dynamics of three-dimensional Bose gases following cooling quenches across the Bose-Einstein condensation transition. By tracking the spatio-temporal evolution of the momentum distribution, we show that the equilibrium condensation threshold simultaneously acts as a dynamical critical point, separating distinct far-from-equilibrium universality classes governed by different non-equilibrium attractors. While quenches above the transition exhibit a single-timescale relaxation toward a thermal fixed point, quenches below the transition display a crossover from a transient weak-turbulence regime to a coarsening fixed point governed by the diffusive recombination of vortex lines. Quenches directly to the condensation threshold, finally, are controlled by a previously unidentified critical fixed point characterized by the superdiffusive spreading of critical fluctuations and a distinct set of dynamical exponents. Together, these dynamical scaling laws establish a far-from-equilibrium counterpart of the condensation phase transition, in which the equilibrium critical point also organizes the long-time non-equilibrium dynamics.

</details>


### [157] [Engineering SU($N$)-Symmetric Hubbard Models with Microwave-Shielded Dipolar Molecules](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27107)
*Jing-Lun Li,Ragheed Alhyder,Andreas Schindewolf,Kaden R. A. Hazzard,Mikhail Lemeshko,Georgios M. Koutentakis*

Main category: cond-mat.quant-gas

TL;DR: 本文提出通过调节微波耦合的拉比频率（Rabi frequency），使光晶格中相邻格点上的两个超冷极性分子（monomers）共振转化为一个场连接二聚体（field-linked doublon），从而实现可控的格点内相互作用和 doublon—monomer-pair 相干转换，为构建具有 SU($N$) 对称性的可调控扩展 Hubbard 模型提供了可行方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 超冷极性分子虽能提供强长程相互作用，且微波屏蔽（microwave shielding）可使其具有可调谐性和近乎核自旋无关的 SU($N$) 对称性，但现有的光晶格极性分子扩展 Hubbard 模型（extended Hubbard model）尚缺乏可控的有限格点内相互作用（on-site interactions），而这是强关联物理（strongly correlated physics）的关键要素。

Method: 通过调节微波耦合的拉比频率（Rabi frequency），使处于相邻晶格格点上的两个单体分子（monomers）与其中一个格点上的场连接二聚体（field-linked doublon）发生共振，从而实现 doublon—monomer-pair 的相干转换（coherent conversion）。

Result: 表征了关键的 Hubbard 参数（Hubbard parameters）以及二聚体寿命（dimer lifetime），展示了格点内相互作用（on-site interactions）和格点间相互作用（off-site interactions）可分别通过微波振幅（microwave amplitude）和微波取向（microwave orientation）近乎独立地调节。

Conclusion: 该结果为实现具有 SU($N$) 对称性且包含可控 doublon 涨落（doublon fluctuations）的扩展 Hubbard 模型提供了路线图，为在强偶极区（strongly dipolar regime）进行量子模拟（quantum simulation）开辟了途径。

Abstract: Ultracold polar molecules provide strong, long-range interactions that microwave shielding makes tunable and nearly nuclear-spin independent, giving an emergent SU($N$) symmetry. However, extended Hubbard models of polar molecules in optical lattices lack, so far, controllable finite on-site interactions, a key ingredient of strong correlated physics. We show that tuning the Rabi frequency of the microwave coupling can bring two individual molecules (monomers) on neighboring lattice sites into resonance with a field-linked dimer (doublon) on one of the sites, enabling coherent doublon--monomer-pair conversion. In this model, we characterize the key Hubbard parameters and the dimer lifetime, demonstrating that the on-site and off-site interactions can be tuned nearly independently through the microwave amplitude and orientation, respectively. Our results provide a roadmap for implementing SU($N$)-symmetric extended Hubbard models with controllable doublon fluctuations, providing access to quantum-simulation in the strongly dipolar regime.

</details>

### [158] [A behavior-environment information loop drives sensory navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26295)
*Kevin S. Chen,Matthew P. Leighton,Damon A. Clark,Thierry Emonet*

Main category: physics.bio-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出一个基于转移熵（transfer entropy）的信息论框架，用于量化生物体在导航过程中感知输入与行为输出之间的双向信息流。通过对细菌、线虫、果蝇以及机器学习智能体的轨迹分析，发现双向信息流能够可靠预测导航效率，揭示了生物与人工系统中共同的“行为-环境”反馈回路，并区分了不同系统的导航策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生物体在环境中定位关键资源时，其行为必须与感知输入紧密耦合。现有研究缺乏一个统一的量化框架来描述这种耦合关系，尤其是感知→行为的“反应性”（reactive）信息流与行为→感知的“主动性”（active）信息流之间的区分与协同机制。

Method: 引入转移熵（transfer entropy）来量化感知输入与行为输出之间的双向信息流：感知→行为的信息流定义导航策略的“反应性”成分，行为→感知的信息流定义“主动性”成分。通过一个最小模型（minimal model）连接宏观导航性能与微观信息流，并将该框架应用于细菌、线虫、果蝇的实验轨迹数据以及机器学习智能体的导航行为分析。

Result: 跨系统分析表明，双向信息流能够可靠预测导航效率，揭示了一个共同的行为-环境反馈回路（behavioral-environment feedback loop）。分解主动与反应性信息流进一步暴露了不同的导航策略，包括细菌趋化（chemotaxis）、果蝇嗅觉导航的空间依赖性策略，以及强化学习（reinforcement learning）智能体的习得策略。

Conclusion: 双向信息流作为理解生物和人工系统中导航行为的一个统一原理（unifying principle）得以确立。该框架不仅能预测导航性能，还能从轨迹中解析不同系统所采取的导航策略，为跨物种与跨领域的导航机制比较提供了理论工具。

Abstract: As organisms navigate the environment to locate critical resources, their behavioral actions must be tightly coupled to their sensory inputs. Here, we introduce an information-theoretic framework that quantifies this coupling using transfer entropy, which measures information flow between sensory inputs and behavioral outputs. Information flow from sensory inputs to behavior defines a "reactive" component of a navigational strategy, whereas information flow from behavior to sensory inputs defines an "active" component, whereby actions shape subsequent sensory experiences. Analyzing these bidirectional information flows enables us to both predict navigational performance and dissect navigation strategies from trajectories. Using a minimal model that captures the active and reactive components, we connect macroscopic performance to microscopic information flows. We then apply the framework to experimentally measured trajectories of bacteria, worms, and flies, as well as to machine learning agents navigating sensory landscapes. Across systems, bidirectional information flow reliably predicts navigation efficiency, revealing a common behavioral-environment feedback loop. Decomposing active and reactive information flows further exposes distinct strategies underlying bacterial chemotaxis, the spatial dependency of the navigation strategy in fly olfactory navigation, and the learned policies of a reinforcement-trained agent. Together, these results establish bidirectional information flow as a unifying principle for understanding navigation in biological and artificial systems.

</details>


### [159] [Navigation driven by bidirectional information transmission between sensing and actuation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26798)
*Avishek Das,Pieter Rein ten Wolde*

Main category: physics.bio-ph

TL;DR: 本文通过解析求解两类生物导航模型，发现导航性能完全由感知与驱动之间双向信息传递的强度和时标决定，与导航器的具体参数无关，并提出了“行为状态方程”（BEST）这一系统无关的信息-功能映射关系，在大肠杆菌趋化随机模拟中得到了无参数拟合的验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生物功能广泛依赖于感知（sensing）与驱动（actuation）之间的反馈循环，例如细胞导航。目前尚不清楚这种双向信息传递的准确性如何控制导航行为，因此需要建立信息与功能之间的定量联系。

Method: 解析求解两类通用导航模型（空间感知细胞和时间感知细胞）；在浅梯度线性响应区域内推导导航性能与双向信息传递的关系；使用TE-PWS算法精确计算转移熵（transfer entropy），并对大肠杆菌（Escherichia coli）的趋化（chemotaxis）过程进行随机模拟（stochastic simulation）以验证理论。

Result: 在浅梯度线性响应（linear-response）区域内，导航性能完全由双向信息传递的强度与时间尺度决定，与导航器的具体参数无关，该关系被称为行为状态方程（Behavioral Equations of State, BESTs）。大肠杆菌趋化模拟结果在无需拟合或缩放参数的情况下符合BEST的预测。

Conclusion: 感知与驱动之间的双向信息传递（bidirectional information transmission）是导航行为的一个组织原则（organizing principle），BEST为实验可检验的数据塌缩（data collapse）提供了理论依据。

Abstract: A wide variety of biological functions are driven by feedback between sensing and actuation. A paradigmatic example is cellular navigation. During navigation, the sensory system maps the environmental input signal onto a sensory output, which then drives an actuation response, changing the future sensory input. How the accuracy of this bidirectional information transmission controls navigation is not currently understood. Here, we study how information controls navigation by analytically solving two generic models that describe two major classes of biological navigators: spatial- and temporal-sensing cells. We find that, in the linear-response regime of shallow gradients, navigation performance is fully determined by the strengths and timescales of bidirectional information transmission, without any explicit dependence on the elementary parameters of the navigator. We call these relations Behavioral Equations of State (BESTs): equalities that map information to function in a system-independent way. BESTs predict an experimentally testable data collapse for the performance of navigators with different sensing and actuation parameters. We test the validity of our theory by performing stochastic simulations of chemotaxis of the bacterium Escherichia coli, computing transfer entropies exactly with the TE-PWS algorithm. The observed performance obeys the BEST without fitting or scaling parameters. Thus, our theory identifies bidirectional information transmission between sensing and actuation as an organizing principle for navigation.

</details>

### [160] [First Search for Quirks at the LHC with FASER](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26195)
*FASER Collaboration*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 本文基于FASER探测器在LHC上收集的数据，首次对具有标准模型（Standard Model）类似QCD的“颜色内”（infracolor, IC）相互作用的粒子——quirks进行搜索。利用闪烁体电荷和定时信息，搜索不带QCD色荷且电荷为±1的慢速quirk对。在几乎无背景的搜索中未发现事件，从而首次排除了IC禁闭标度（confinement scale）在300 eV < Λ < 100 keV范围内、质量高于弱电标度（weak scale）的quirks。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LHC上此前尚未对quirks进行搜索，这类粒子具有类似QCD的“颜色内”（infracolor）相互作用，可能存在于超出标准模型的新物理中。

Method: 利用FASER探测器在2022—2024年收集的质子-质子对撞数据，积分亮度为186 fb⁻¹，质心系能量√s = 13.6 TeV。通过闪烁体电荷和定时信息，搜索不带QCD色荷、电荷为±1的慢速quirk对。

Result: 在几乎无背景的搜索中未观测到任何事件。首次排除了IC禁闭标度（confinement scale）Λ在300 eV到100 keV范围内、质量高于弱电标度（weak scale）的quirks。

Conclusion: FASER实验首次对quirks进行了LHC能级搜索，给出了对这类粒子在特定IC禁闭标度范围和质量区间的排除限，填补了相关实验空白。

Abstract: We present the first LHC search for quirks, particles with Standard Model and a QCD-like infracolor (IC) interactions. The analysis uses data collected in 2022--2024 by FASER, with a $186~\mathrm{fb}^{-1}$ integrated luminosity of proton-proton collisions at a center-of-mass energy $\sqrt{s}=13.6\,\mathrm{TeV}$. Scintillator charge and timing information are used to search for slow pairs of quirks with no QCD color and electric charge $\pm 1$. No events are seen in this nearly background-free search, resulting in the first exclusion of quirks with masses above the weak scale for IC confinement scales in the broad range of $300 \mathrm{eV}<Λ<100\mathrm{keV}$.

</details>


### [161] [Improved analysis of non-resonant Higgs boson pair production in the $b\bar{b}τ^+τ^-$ final state with $196$ fb$^{-1}$ of data collected at $\sqrt{s}$ = 13 TeV and 13.6 TeV with the ATLAS detector](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26879)
*ATLAS Collaboration*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 本论文利用ATLAS探测器在LHC上采集的140 fb⁻¹ (13 TeV)和56 fb⁻¹ (13.6 TeV)质子-质子对撞数据，对末态为b\bar{b}τ^+τ^-的非共振希格斯玻色子对产生（HH）过程进行搜索，测得HH产生截面与标准模型（Standard Model, SM）预期之比为μ_{HH}= 2.6^{+1.4}_{-1.0}，观测（预期）显著性为2.6 (1.2)倍标准差，并在95%置信水平下给出希格斯玻色子自耦合修正因子κ_λ的限定区间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 希格斯玻色子对产生（HH）是测量希格斯玻色子自耦合（Higgs self-coupling）以及检验标准模型（Standard Model, SM）中电弱对称性破缺机制的关键探针。自耦合修正因子κ_λ的精确测定有助于揭示希格斯势的形状，并探索可能存在的超出标准模型（Beyond Standard Model, BSM）的新物理信号。

Method: 基于ATLAS探测器在2015–2023年间采集的质子-质子对撞数据，总积分亮度为140 fb⁻¹（质心能量√s = 13 TeV）和56 fb⁻¹（√s = 13.6 TeV），针对b\bar{b}τ^+τ^-末态执行非共振希格斯玻色子对产生（HH）搜索。相比于先前分析，本工作受益于新增的13.6 TeV数据集，并在事件重建和分析技术方面进行改进。此外，作为分析策略的验证，采用相同的事例选择准则，在相同末态中测量了ZH和ZZ过程。

Result: HH产生截面与SM预期之比为μ_{HH}= 2.6^{+1.4}_{-1.0}，观测（预期）显著性为2.6 (1.2)倍标准差，与SM期望一致；相对于SM预期的显著性为1.65倍标准差。假设其他希格斯玻色子耦合均为SM值，观测（预期）的95%置信水平区间为κ_λ ∈ [-3.4, 1.6] ∪ [5.5, 10.1]（κ_λ ∈ [-1.7, 8.5]）。作为验证，ZH和ZZ过程的观测（预期）显著性分别为3.5 (2.4)和1.8 (2.7)倍标准差。

Conclusion: 测量结果与SM预期在统计误差范围内一致，未观察到显著的超出SM的信号。本分析通过新增高能数据和技术改进，相较于先前ATLAS结果给出了更精确的HH产生截面和κ_λ限定；同时，对ZH和ZZ过程的测量验证了分析策略的有效性。

Abstract: A search for non-resonant Higgs boson pair production (HH) in the $b\bar{b}τ^+τ^-$ final state is performed using 140 $fb^{-1}$ and 56 $fb^{-1}$ of proton-proton collision data at centre-of-mass energies of $\sqrt{s}$ = 13 TeV and 13.6 TeV, respectively, recorded by the ATLAS detector during 2015-2023 at the CERN Large Hadron Collider. Relative to the previous ATLAS searches in the same final state, the analysis benefits from the additional dataset collected at 13.6 TeV and from improvements in both event reconstruction and analysis techniques. The Higgs boson pair production cross-section divided by the Standard Model (SM) prediction is found to be $μ_{HH}= 2.6^{+1.4}_{-1.0}$, consistent with the SM expectation. This corresponds to an observed (expected) significance of 2.6 (1.2) standard deviations over the background-only hypothesis of no HH production and 1.65 standard deviations with respect to the SM prediction. Under the assumption that all other Higgs boson couplings take their SM values, the observed (expected) 95% confidence level interval for the Higgs boson self-coupling modifier is found to be $κ_λ \in [-3.4, 1.6] \cup [5.5, 10.1]$ ($κ_λ \in[-1.7, 8.5]$). As a validation of the analysis strategy, measurements of the ZH and ZZ processes are performed in the same final state using the same event selection, yielding observed (expected) significances of 3.5 (2.4) and 1.8 (2.7) standard deviations, respectively, relative to the background-only prediction.

</details>

### [162] [Gravitational Waves from Multiple Cosmic Superstrings and the Overshoot Problem](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26124)
*Luca Brunelli,Michele Cicoli,Muhammad Hassan,Seyed Ehsan Qoreishi,Francisco Gil Pedro*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文分析了后暴胀时期弦宇宙学中多种宇宙超弦（cosmic superstrings）的演化动力学，发现由包裹内循环的NS5-和D3-膜早期衰变产生的引力波辐射背景能有效阻止模场（modulus）过冲最小值，而基本弦（fundamental strings）存活更久并在模场振荡时贡献约1/3总能量密度，其衰变产生的多峰高频引力波谱可作为弦理论的观测特征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究后暴胀时期多种宇宙超弦的动力学演化，以及它们通过衰变产生的引力波信号是否能为弦理论（string theory）提供可观测的检验特征。

Method: 对由模场（modulus）在陡势垒上滚动所控制的多种宇宙超弦（cosmic superstrings）构成的动力系统（dynamical system）进行完整分析。

Result: 包裹内循环的NS5-和D3-膜（NS5- and D3-branes）衰变产生的有效弦（effective strings）形成的引力波背景辐射摩擦可阻止模场过冲最小值；基本弦存活时间更长，在模场振荡时期贡献约1/3总能量密度；多种超弦衰变产生的引力波谱呈现高频多峰结构。

Conclusion: 即使在模场主导晚期经历稀释，多种宇宙超弦衰变产生的引力波仍可形成高频多峰信号，从而提供弦理论一般性特征的观测印记。

Abstract: Post inflationary string cosmology can feature an initial population of multiple species of cosmic superstrings whose tension is controlled by a modulus rolling over a steep potential toward a late-time minimum. We perform a full analysis of the associated dynamical system, finding that overshooting the minimum is prevented by the friction of a radiation background of gravitational waves produced from the early decay of effective strings arising from NS5- and D3-branes wrapped around internal cycles. On the other hand, fundamental strings survive longer and decay when the modulus is oscillating around the minimum and they have about $1/3$ of the total energy density. The spectrum of gravitational waves generated by the decays of these multiple cosmic superstrings, even if diluted by a late epoch of modulus domination, can still result in a high-frequency, multi-peaked signal, offering an observational signature of generic features of string theory.

</details>


### [163] [Iterative Gauging is Deconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26126)
*Sebastian Franco,Diego Rodriguez-Gomez*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文证明量子场论中通过迭代规范（iterative gauging）对称性产生额外空间维度的机制，实际上是维度解构（dimensional deconstruction），即额外维度编码在箭图规范理论（quiver gauge theory）结构中的过程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解释为何迭代规范对称性能产生一个涌现的额外空间维度，并确定其背后的精确机制。

Method: 通过证明标准解构作用量在对希格斯支（Higgs branch）上的戈德斯通场（Goldstone fields）进行对偶化后，与迭代规范产生的作用量完全等价，从而建立二者之间的对应关系。

Result: 建立了清晰的词典：箭图节点对应规范场，箭图链接对应为耦合磁对称性（magnetic symmetries）而引入的规范场。此外，从规范电对称性（electric symmetry）而非磁对称性出发，会产生用对偶变量（dual variables）书写的解构理论，且所有耦合常数被反转。

Conclusion: 迭代规范对称性产生额外维度的现象本质上是维度解构，并且通过选择规范电对称性还是磁对称性，可以得到互为对偶的理论表述。

Abstract: A recent construction showed that iteratively gauging symmetries of a quantum field theory can generate an emergent extra spatial dimension. Here we identify the precise mechanism underlying this phenomenon: it is dimensional deconstruction, the procedure by which an extra dimension is encoded in the structure of a quiver gauge theory. We demonstrate this by showing that the standard deconstruction action, upon dualizing the Goldstone fields on the Higgs branch, is exactly equivalent to the action produced by iterative gauging. The dictionary is transparent: quiver nodes correspond to gauge fields, and quiver links correspond to the gauge fields introduced to couple to magnetic symmetries. We further show that starting by gauging the electric rather than the magnetic symmetry produces the deconstructed theory written in the dual variables, with all coupling constants inverted.

</details>


### [164] [Toward an Observable Algebra for JT-de Sitter Space: Gap Protection and Modular Dressings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26194)
*Hassan ElSayed*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 该论文研究了全球德西特空间中区域代数的基本补（fundamental complement）与算子代数结构的关系，提出了基于最大互补间隙角长度的判定准则，并引入表示依赖的交换子模型（commutant model）以刻画区域代数的冯·诺依曼子代数归属与不连续变化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索全球德西特空间（global de Sitter space）中基本补与区域算子代数（regional operator algebra）之间的内在联系，为理解德西特时空中的量子信息结构和全息原理提供代数基础。

Method: 在二维全球德西特时空的时间对称圆上分析开弧的有限并集，利用最大互补间隙的角长度判定基本补；在更高维情形中引入开半球（open hemisphere）包含准则。进一步在抽象Möbius协变手性共形网（Möbius-covariant chiral conformal net）中，通过共享辅助时钟将每个区域代数与其真空模流（vacuum modular flow）交叉，推导连续核（continuous core）包含的精确判据。最后，受Bousso-Penington全息处方启发，引入表示依赖的交换子模型。

Result: 证明了基本补由最大互补间隙唯一决定：仅当间隙角长度至少为π时该间隙贡献基本补，且至多一个间隙满足此条件。在更高维情形中，判定准则等价于包含一个开半球。连续核包含的判据显示，当较大区域包含额外类空间隔时真空关联（vacuum correlations）阻碍该判据成立；但通过在分离弧的有限布尔代数（Boolean algebra）上使用分裂乘积态（split product state）可得到兼容族。对于两弧族，添加任意短的对跖分量（antipodal component）会消除基本补，并使分配代数不连续地变为完整参考代数。

Conclusion: 基本补的存在与区域代数的冯·诺依曼代数类型（Type-II$_1$因子）结构密切相关，区域代数的分配在特定参数变化下呈现不连续跃变，表明德西特时空中的局域代数具有丰富的非平凡结构。

Abstract: We investigate the relation between fundamental complements and regional operator algebras in global de Sitter space. For a finite union of open arcs on the time-symmetric circle of global dS$_2$, we show that the fundamental complement is determined by the largest complementary gap: a gap contributes precisely when its angular length is at least π, and at most one gap can do so. In higher-dimensional global de Sitter space, the corresponding criterion is containment of an open hemisphere.
  We then study the operator-algebraic consequences in an abstract Möbius-covariant chiral conformal net. Each regional algebra is crossed with its vacuum modular flow using a shared auxiliary clock. We derive an exact criterion for inclusions between the resulting continuous cores and show that vacuum correlations obstruct this criterion when the larger region contains an additional spacelike arc. A compatible family can nevertheless be obtained on the finite Boolean algebra generated by a fixed collection of separated arcs by using a split product state.
  Motivated by the holograms prescription of Bousso and Penington, we introduce a representation-dependent commutant model for regional algebras. When the protected gap overlaps a fixed reference arc, the assigned algebra is a proper von Neumann subalgebra of the reference Type-II$_1$ factor. For an explicit two-arc family, adding an arbitrarily short antipodal component removes the fundamental complement and changes the assigned algebra discontinuously to the full reference algebra.

</details>


### [165] [CasimirRFM: A Mathematica package for Riemann-flat compactifications with Casimir energies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26140)
*Bruno Valeixo Bento*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: CasimirRFM 是一个 Mathematica 软件包，用于研究黎曼平坦流形（Riemann-flat manifolds）上的紧致化，并通过 Ewald 求和对高维场论和超引力中的一圈 Casimir 能量进行高效数值计算，支持一般无质量谱与扭曲边界条件，同时提供流形群论结构分析、模空间度规计算以及 Casimir 势和局域能量密度的可视化工具。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在高维场论和超引力中，紧致化（compactification）后的一圈 Casimir 能量计算对理论低能有效作用量至关重要，但该计算涉及复杂格点和群论分析，缺乏通用高效的数值工具。

Method: 采用 Ewald 求和技术（Ewald summation）对格点和（lattice sums）进行高效数值计算；利用有限群理论构造和分析定义流形的有限群，确定不变度量和上同调基，识别相容的自旋结构，并计算模空间度规（moduli-space metrics）；实现对引力子、p-形式、旋量及 Rarita-Schwinger 表示中 holonomy 元素迹的求值。

Result: 通过 Type IIB 超引力在 $T^6/\mathbb{Z}_8$ 上的紧致化实例，展示了 Casimir 势的计算结果和局域化 Casimir-膜贡献的可视化效果。

Conclusion: CasimirRFM 为 Riemann-flat 流形紧致化中的一圈 Casimir 能量计算提供了一个系统化的数值实现框架，有效整合了群论分析与物理可观测量计算，有助于高维超引力模型的精确分析。

Abstract: CasimirRFM is a Mathematica package for the study of compactifications on Riemann-flat manifolds and the computation of one-loop Casimir energies in higher-dimensional field theories and supergravity. It implements an efficient numerical evaluation of lattice sums using Ewald summation, and computes both lower-dimensional Casimir potentials and local higher-dimensional Casimir energy densities, allowing for general massless spectra and twisted boundary conditions. The package provides tools for constructing and analysing the finite groups defining these manifolds, determining invariant metrics and cohomology bases, identifying compatible spin structures, and computing moduli-space metrics. Moreover, it evaluates the traces of holonomy elements in the graviton, $p$-form, spinor, and Rarita-Schwinger representations. We describe the mathematical formulation and numerical implementation of the package, and illustrate its use through a compactification of Type IIB supergravity on $T^6/\mathbb{Z}_8$, including the computation of the Casimir potential and the visualization of localised Casimir-brane contributions.

</details>


### [166] [Intersection Bounds for BPS Strings in Six-Dimensional Supergravity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26197)
*Hee-Cheol Kim,Kai Xu*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文从有效场论（EFT）角度出发，利用Zariski分解（作为吸引子机制在荷格点中的对应）和E-弦世界sheet理论中的流代数嵌入，推导出六维$\mathcal{N}=(1,0)$超引力中张量模空间内的BPS弦荷生成元交截数的普适上界，并证明这些交截数在对偶性下是有限的。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 先前规范反常消除条件虽能约束支持规范代数的生成元之间的交截，但对于自交截数为$-2$和$-3$的E-弦生成元的交截界尚不完整。本文旨在填补这一空白，从纯有效场论视角导出普适且完整的边界条件。

Method: 论文将Zariski分解重新解释为吸引子机制（attractor mechanism）在荷格点（charge lattice）中的对应物，并结合E-弦世界sheet理论（E-string worldsheet theory）中的流代数嵌入（current algebra embeddings），对张量荷生成元的交截数（intersection numbers）施加普适约束。研究过程最初由人工智能引导发现，随后通过EFT论证给出解析证明。

Result: 得到了关于E-弦生成元自交截数为$-2$和$-3$情形的强普适边界条件；确立了张量荷交截数在对偶性（duality）下是有限的。

Conclusion: 本文从有效场论出发，系统补全了六维$\mathcal{N}=(1,0)$超引力中张量模空间结构的关键约束条件，证明了BPS生成元交截数的有限性，并展示了AI引导在理论物理问题探索中的有效性。

Abstract: In six-dimensional $\mathcal{N}=(1,0)$ supergravity, the structure of tensor moduli space is governed by primitive BPS string charges known as BPS generators and their intersection pairing. We derive bounds on the intersection numbers of these generators from a purely effective field theory (EFT) perspective. Although gauge anomaly cancellation constrains intersections between generators supporting gauge algebras, bounds for E-strings intersecting generators with self-intersection numbers $-2$ and $-3$ have previously remained incomplete. We show that the Zariski decomposition, interpreted as the charge lattice counterpart of the attractor mechanism, together with current algebra embeddings on the E-string worldsheet theory, yields strong universal bounds on these intersection numbers. These results establish the finiteness of tensor charge intersection numbers up to duality. The underlying structure was identified through AI-guided investigation and is proven here analytically using EFT arguments.

</details>


### [167] [Nucleon spectra and wave functions from holographic models with dual Einstein-dilaton and Starobinsky-dilaton gravities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26319)
*Adão S. da Silva Junior,Juan M. Z. Pretel,Henrique Boschi-Filho*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 该研究通过爱因斯坦-狄拉顿（Einstein-dilaton）和斯塔罗宾斯基-狄拉顿（Starobinsky-dilaton）两种全息对偶（holographic dual）模型重新分析核子谱（nucleon spectra），引入新参数以改善与实验数据的吻合，并首次将斯塔罗宾斯基-狄拉顿引力模型应用于核子谱分析，与软墙模型（soft-wall model）及实验数据进行了比较。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有爱因斯坦-狄拉顿全息模型（即改进的全息QCD）在描述禁闭（confinement）和胶球谱（glueball spectra）方面取得进展，近期被推广至介子（mesons）和核子（nucleons）研究。本文旨在通过引入新参数优化该模型对核子谱的描述，并将这一思路拓展至斯塔罗宾斯基-狄拉顿引力框架，以构建新的改进全息模型。

Method: 在爱因斯坦-狄拉顿全息模型中引入新的参数，重新分析核子谱；并将相同方法推广至斯塔罗宾斯基-狄拉顿引力模型，定义一个新的改进全息模型，用于重新分析核子谱。

Result: 两种模型均被用于核子谱的计算，并与软墙模型及实验数据进行了对比分析。

Conclusion: 通过引入新参数改进了爱因斯坦-狄拉顿模型对核子谱的描述，并成功将斯塔罗宾斯基-狄拉顿引力模型构建为新的全息QCD模型用于核子谱研究，两种模型的结果均与实验数据进行了比较。

Abstract: We study the nucleon spectra in two holographic set-ups: Einstein-dilaton and Starobinsky-dilaton gravity models. The Einstein-dilaton holographic model, also known as improved holographic QCD, have been proposed some time ago to describe confinement and glueball spectra. Recently, it has been applied to the case of mesons and nucleons. In this work, we reconsider the Einstein-dilaton holographic model to discuss the nucleon spectra introducing new parameters that allow us to improve the comparison with experimental data. Then, we extend this idea to the context of Starobinsky-dilaton gravity defining another improved holographic model. We use this new holographic model to reanalyse the nucleon spectra and also compare them with soft-wall model and experimental data.

</details>


### [168] [D-branes and nonlinear interactions in the $AdS$ pure spinor string](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26343)
*Brenno Carlini Vallilo*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 该论文为闭弦通道中$AdS_5\times S^5$上的纯旋量弦（pure spinor string）建立了零场边界条件的坐标无关代数描述，推导了与狄拉克-玻恩-因费尔德（Dirac-Born-Infeld）类运动方程一致的约束系统。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 发展一种坐标无关的代数框架，以统一描述闭弦通道中$AdS_5\times S^5$纯旋量弦的零场边界条件，并构建与之相容的半BPS（half-BPS）洛伦兹D膜（D-brane）几何。

Method: 利用$\mathfrak{psu}(2,2|4)$的对合自同构（involutive automorphisms）交换$\mathbb Z_4$分次的奇部，其固定子代数（fixed subalgebras）给出候选D膜几何。进一步构造物质与鬼边界相互作用的局部拟设（local ansatz），推导切向超联络（tangential superconnection）、横向嵌入（transverse embedding）与四个鬼胶合耦合（ghost-gluing couplings）的一致性方程。

Result: 七个代数代表元实现了洛伦兹零通量D1、D3、D5及D7探针扇区。所得一致性方程组构成一组定义在膜世界体（brane world-volume）上的狄拉克-玻恩-因费尔德类运动方程。

Conclusion: 所提代数方法为$AdS_5\times S^5$纯旋量弦的边界条件提供了统一的坐标无关描述，导出的方程系统与标准的狄拉克-玻恩-因费尔德类结构相一致。

Abstract: We develop a coordinate-independent algebraic description of zero-field boundary conditions for the pure spinor string on $AdS_5\times S^5$ in the closed-string channel. The construction is organized by involutive automorphisms of $\mathfrak{psu}(2,2|4)$ that exchange the odd sectors of the $\mathbb Z_4$ grading. Their fixed subalgebras provide candidate half-BPS, Lorentzian D-brane geometries with vanishing background world-volume flux, consistent with the standard $AdS_2$, $AdS_3\times S^1$, $AdS_4\times S^2$, and related embeddings. Together, the seven representatives realize the Lorentzian zero-flux D1, D3, D5, and D7 probe sectors considered here. We then construct a local ansatz for the matter and ghost boundary interactions and derive a system of consistency equations for the tangential superconnection, transverse embedding, and four ghost-gluing couplings. The resulting system is a set of Dirac-Born-Infeld-like equations of motion on the brane world-volume.

</details>


### [169] [Generalized Bergshoeff-de Roo identification in the Supergravity frame](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26415)
*Walter H. Baron,Fabian A. Portilla*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文回顾并改进了基于广义Bergshoeff-de Roo识别（Generalized Bergshoeff-de Roo identification）的方法，利用微分同胚（diffeomorphism）、洛伦兹（Lorentz）和规范不变性（gauge invariance）以及T-对偶（T-duality），在超引力（Supergravity）框架下直接导出弦理论有效作用量（effective action）中的高导数耦合项（higher-derivative couplings）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 弦理论的有效作用量因底层理论具有众多对称性而结构高度受限，需要利用这些对称性来系统性地推导其高导数耦合项。

Method: 采用并改进广义Bergshoeff-de Roo识别（Generalized Bergshoeff-de Roo identification）方法，利用微分同胚（diffeomorphism）、洛伦兹（Lorentz）和规范不变性（gauge invariance）以及T-对偶（T-duality）等对称性，先在场论对偶（Double Field Theory）框架中导出耦合项，再将其直接置于标准超引力（Supergravity）框架中生成。

Result: 成功将先前在双场论（Double Field Theory）框架中发展的广义Bergshoeff-de Roo识别方法加以回顾与改进，使其能够在标准超引力（Supergravity）框架下直接生成高导数耦合项。

Conclusion: 通过重新审视并细化广义Bergshoeff-de Roo识别方法，实现了在标准超引力框架内直接推导弦理论有效作用量中的高导数耦合，从而简化了推导过程并拓展了该方法的适用范围。

Abstract: The effective action of string theory has a highly constrained structure due to the numerous symmetries of the underlying theory. Recently, a method known as the Generalized Bergshoeff-de Roo identification was developed, which exploits diffeomorphism, Lorentz and gauge invariance, along with T-duality, to derive higher-derivative couplings in the effective action within the framework of Double Field Theory. This method has been revisited and further refined to generate couplings directly within the standard Supergravity framework.

</details>


### [170] [Massive and massless particles in Mielke--Baekler geometries](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26532)
*Carles Batlle,Roberto Casalbuoni,Daniele Dominici,José Figueroa-O'Farrill,Joaquim Gomis*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究了Mielke–Baekler几何中自旋粒子的世界线作用量，重点关注具有挠率的平坦（平直）联络（即Weitzenböck联络）情形，并揭示了不同动力学扇区（“正则”与“临界”）中物理自由度的差异，以及自旋导致的Wess–Zumino项如何引发Papapetrou型强迫项和诺特对称性的变化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Mielke–Baekler几何是三维（three-dimensional）允许联络具有挠率（torsion）的洛伦兹时空，可推广闵可夫斯基和（反）德西特时空。作者旨在构建这类时空中有质量与无质量自旋粒子的世界线作用量，并系统分析其动力学结构与对称性。

Method: 采用余伴随轨道（coadjoint orbits）方法结合非线性实现（nonlinear realisations）技术，构造粒子的世界线作用量。通过引入描述自旋的Wess–Zumino项，区分正则扇区（regular sector）与临界扇区（critical sector），并分别在Weitzenböck联络和Levi-Civita联络下分析动力学。

Result: 在无挠率（trivial）情形之外，仅反德西特（anti-de~Sitter）时空允许不变的Weitzenböck联络（即平坦但有挠率的联络）。自旋的引入导致两个动力学扇区出现，其物理自由度数目不同。有质量粒子在临界扇区中出现Papapetrou型强迫项（Papapetrou-type forcing term）。无质量无自旋粒子的作用量具有共形对称性（conformal transformations）；有自旋时，正则扇区中仅Killing子集保留为诺特对称性（Noether symmetries），临界扇区中共形Killing贡献可通过规范变换消除。

Conclusion: Mielke–Baekler几何为研究具有挠率的时空中的自旋粒子动力学提供了丰富的框架，其依赖于联络选择的动力学扇区结构揭示了自旋–挠率耦合的新特征，并建立了与共形对称性及经典自旋–引力相互作用的联系。

Abstract: The Mielke--Baekler geometries are three-dimensional
  reductive homogeneous spacetimes together with a choice of invariant
  connection which is compatible with a lorentzian metric. The
  spacetimes generalise Minkowski and (anti)de~Sitter spacetimes in
  that the invariant metric connection can have torsion, a peculiarity
  of three dimensions. Using coadjoint orbits and the techniques of
  nonlinear realisations, we construct worldline actions for massive
  and massless spinning particles moving in these spacetimes. We pay
  particular attention to the so-called teleparallel branch, in which
  the curvature of the invariant connection vanishes. Apart from the trivial Minkowski case, this singles
  out anti-de~Sitter spacetime, as the only of these lorentzian
  manifolds admitting an invariant Weitzenböck connection; that is, a
  flat connection with torsion. The introduction of a Wess--Zumino
  term describing spin has, as a main consequence, the appearance of
  dynamical sectors (denoted ``regular'' and ``critical''), with a
  different number of physical degrees of freedom. In particular, in
  the massive case, we discuss the formulation of the dynamics in
  terms of either the Weitzenböck or the Levi-Civita connection, and
  the emergence of a Papapetrou-type forcing term in the critical sector.
  For the massless particle we study the Noether
  symmetries of the action, which, for the spinless case, include the
  conformal transformations. For nonzero spin, only the Killing subset
  survives as genuine Noether transformations in the regular sector,
  while in the critical sector any conformal Killing contribution can
  be set to zero by a gauge transformation.

</details>


### [171] [Static topological dilatonic black hole with multiple horizons and its thermodynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26535)
*M. M. Stetsko*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文在爱因斯坦-膨胀子理论（Einstein-dilaton theory）中引入由麦克斯韦场不变量无穷级数表示的非线性电磁场，构建了一类静态拓扑带电黑洞解。该解可具有任意数量的视界，能量条件分析显示非线性电磁场在视界外满足所有能量条件，而膨胀子场仅满足零能量条件（NEC）。热力学分析表明，该解的相行为比标准爱因斯坦-麦克斯韦-膨胀子（EMD）黑洞更为丰富，具有额外的稳定相和三重点，并通过欧几里得方法讨论了全局稳定性，同时通过扩展相空间方法（将宇宙学常数视为热力学变量）得到了Smarr关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准爱因斯坦-麦克斯韦-膨胀子（EMD）理论中的黑洞最多仅有两个视界，热力学相行为相对简单。本文旨在通过引入非线性电磁场，获得具有更丰富视界结构和热力学相行为的黑洞解，以探索超出标准EMD框架的新物理现象。

Method: 在爱因斯坦-膨胀子理论中，采用由麦克斯韦场不变量（Maxwell field invariant）的无穷级数表示的非线性电磁场（nonlinear electromagnetic field）来构造静态拓扑带电黑洞解。热力学分析分别通过标准方法和欧几里得方法（Euclidean method）进行，后者允许考虑巨正则系综（Grand Canonical Ensemble）和正则系综（Canonical Ensemble）。近临界行为分析则采用扩展相空间方法（extended phase space approach），将宇宙学常数视为热力学变量。

Result: 所得非线性推广的黑洞解可具有任意数量的视界。能量条件方面，非线性电磁场在视界外满足所有能量条件，膨胀子场则仅满足零能量条件（NEC）。热力学行为比标准EMD黑洞更为丰富，欧几里得描述揭示了额外的稳定相和三相点的存在。全局稳定性与标准EMD解或Reissner-Nordström黑洞存在共同特征，但因非线性场而出现新的特性。扩展相空间方法成功导出了Smarr关系（Smarr relation）。

Conclusion: 非线性电磁场的引入显著丰富了爱因斯坦-膨胀子理论中黑洞解的视界结构与热力学相行为，包括出现多视界、额外稳定相和三重点等新现象。不同方法获得的热力学结果相互一致，扩展相空间方法进一步验证了该解的Smarr关系。

Abstract: We obtain a static topological charged black hole solution within Einstein-dilaton theory with nonlinear electromagnetic field represented by an infinite series over Maxwell field invariant. In contrast with standard Einstein-Maxwell-dilaton (EMD) theory where the black hole might have no more than two horizons, the nonlinear generalization might give rise to a solution with arbitrary number of horizons. We examine energy conditions and show that for the nonlinear electromagnetic field itself all the energy conditions are fulfilled outside of the black hole, whereas for the dilaton field the only condition which is not violated outside is the Null Energy Condition (NEC). We also study thermodynamics of the black hole and show that the thermal behaviour of the solution is considerably richer than for its standard EMD cousin. To have complete description of the thermodynamics we also use the Euclidean method, which allows us to consider the so-called Grand Canonical and Canonical Ensembles. The results obtained by different approaches show their consistency. The Euclidean description naturally allowed us to consider global stability, which is shown to have some common features with standard EMD solution, or even Reissner-Nordström black hole. But there some new peculiarities caused by the nonlinear field, namely we might have additional stable phases and a triple point. To examine near critical behaviour we consider the extended phase space approach, where the cosmological constant is supposed to be a thermodynamic variable. Making use of the extended formalism we also obtain the Smarr relation.

</details>


### [172] [World-Line Actions in Weyl Geometry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26868)
*Cezar Condeescu,Andrei Micu*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文从规范理论（gauge theory）视角出发，构建了Weyl几何中粒子的世界线作用量，论证了在对称相（symmetric phase）中由于维数、加性、Weyl不变性等条件无法同时满足，因此无法定义合理的固有时间（proper time）；只有在自发对称性破缺（spontaneous symmetry breaking）后粒子获得质量，才能恢复固有时间的定义。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探讨在Weyl几何（Weyl geometry）中是否存在可满足Weyl不变性的固有时间定义，从而理解对称相下时间概念的局限性。

Method: 从规范理论出发，构造了Weyl几何中粒子的世界线作用量（world-line action），引入开放的Wilson线（open Wilson line）处理一般Weyl场带来的非局域性；通过引入世界线上的einbein来构建经典等价的二次型作用量，并分析非局域性的来源。

Result: 该作用量无量纲、Weyl不变、具有加性且通常非局域；在可积情况下Wilson线可局域化。固有时间的定义所需条件（仿射参数化、时间量纲、加性及Weyl不变性）无法同时满足，因此在对称相中不存在令人满意的固有时间概念。自发对称性破缺后粒子获得质量，作用量变为Riemann几何形式，固有时间可重新定义。

Conclusion: Weyl几何对称相中无法建立合理的固有时间，只有通过自发对称性破缺进入Riemann几何（Riemannian geometry）后才能恢复固有时间的定义。

Abstract: In this note we construct, from a gauge theory perspective, the world-line action for a particle moving on a time-like curve in Weyl geometry. The action we find is dimensionless, Weyl invariant, additive and, in general, non-local due to an open Wilson line which we have to add in order to account for a general Weyl field. In special cases, this Wilson line can be local, but the geometry becomes integrable. The action can not be used to measure the proper time as it is dimensionless and no mass parameter is allowed in the symmetric phase of the theory. We show that the usual conditions for defining proper time: affine parametrization, dimension of time and additivity supplemented by the requirement of Weyl invariance can not be fulfilled simultaneously and therefore no satisfactory notion of proper time exists in the symmetric phase. Under spontaneous symmetry breaking the particle acquires a mass, the action becomes Riemannian and the proper time can be again defined. We also construct a classically equivalent quadratic action by using an einbein on the world-line and show that the non-locality can be seen to arise from integrating out a constrained field.

</details>


### [173] [Quantum Fisher information of the Klein--Gordon, $φ^4$, and Dirac vacua](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26919)
*Preslav Asenov,Alessio Serafini*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文计算了三种量子场论（Klein-Gordon场、φ⁴相互作用场和自由Dirac场）真空态关于质量参数的量子Fisher信息（quantum Fisher information, QFI），揭示了QFI在不同时空维度下的标度行为与发散结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索量子Fisher信息（QFI）作为量子场论（quantum field theory）中参数估计精度的度量，研究真空态关于质量参数的QFI行为，以揭示场论结构及其与全息对偶（holographic duality）等概念的联系。

Method: 在(d+1)维欧几里得时空（Euclidean spacetime）上考虑三种场论：Klein-Gordon场、φ⁴相互作用场和自由Dirac场。对φ⁴理论，采用微扰论（perturbation theory）计算至相互作用常数的一阶；对其他理论进行精确或解析计算。

Result: 自由Klein-Gordon场的QFI呈m^{d-2}依赖关系，在d=2时与维度无关，与全息对偶预言一致；φ⁴相互作用在d=3时导致QFI发散，在d=0时减少可用信息；自由Dirac场的真空QFI在d=2和d=3时紫外发散（UV-divergent），在d=1时依赖于质量，在d=0时为零。

Conclusion: 不同场论的真空QFI对质量参数的响应依赖于时空维度与相互作用结构，其结果可用于检验全息对偶并揭示场论的信息几何（information geometry）性质。

Abstract: The quantum Fisher information (QFI) of the vacuum of three quantum field theories is evaluated with respect to the mass parameter of each theory. All field theories are considered on a $(d+1)$-dimensional Euclidean spacetime. We consider the Klein-Gordon field, a quartic interaction ($φ^4$) field theory, and the free Dirac field. In the case of the quartic interaction theory, the QFI is given perturbatively to first order in the interaction constant. For the free KG case, we find a $m^{d-2}$ dependence of the QFI, and thus no dependence for $d=2$, in agreement with the holographic duality characterizing the theory. The introduction of a quartic interaction is shown to lead to a QFI divergence in $d=3$ and to reduce the available information in the $d=0$ system. The vacuum QFI with respect to the free Dirac field mass is shown to be UV-divergent for $d=2$ and $d=3$, mass-dependent for $d=1$, and zero for $d=0$.

</details>


### [174] [Extended Phase Space Thermodynamics and Joule-Thomson Expansion of Regular AdS Black Holes in a String Cloud](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26931)
*Y. Elaima,A. Daassou,H. Lekbich,F. Oubbad*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究了浸没在弦云背景（string cloud background）中的静态球对称正则黑洞（regular black hole）的扩展相空间热力学（extended phase space thermodynamics）。通过将负宇宙常数（negative cosmological constant）视为热力学压力（thermodynamic pressure），发现量子启发的正则化核心与弦云密度显著影响黑洞的热稳定性（thermal stability），并揭示了由正则化核心导致的严格标度不变性（scale invariance），以及弦云参数对范德瓦尔斯临界压缩比（Van der Waals critical compressibility ratio）的改变。临界指数（critical exponents）与平均场理论（mean-field theory）一致，并通过焦耳-汤姆逊膨胀（Joule-Thomson expansion）定义了等焓加热与冷却区域。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了理解非奇异时空中的黑洞热力学行为，研究弦云背景对正则黑洞热力学性质的影响，并探索其与已知临界现象（如范德瓦尔斯流体）之间的关联。

Method: 采用扩展相空间热力学方法，将负宇宙常数视为压力；通过分析正则化核心的标度不变性与弦云密度的影响，计算了临界压缩比与临界指数；并利用焦耳-汤姆逊膨胀刻画等焓加热与冷却区域。

Result: 正则化核心导致严格的标度不变性；弦云参数显著改变通用的范德瓦尔斯临界压缩比；计算得到的临界指数与平均场理论预测一致。

Conclusion: 该正则黑洞的整体热力学特征可以通过扩展相空间热力学及焦耳-汤姆逊膨胀进行完整描述，表明其热力学行为在平均场框架内是自洽的。

Abstract: We investigate the extended phase space thermodynamics of a static, spherically symmetric regular black hole immersed in a string cloud background. By identifying the negative cosmological constant as a thermodynamic pressure, our analysis indicates that the quantum-inspired regularizing core and the string cloud density significantly affect the thermal stability of the black hole. We specifically illustrate a stringent scale invariance linked to the regularizing core, while the dimensionless string cloud parameter substantially alters the universal Van der Waals critical compressibility ratio. The significant geometric inconsistencies result in computed critical exponents that align with those of mean-field theory. We specify the Joule-Thomson expansion to define the isenthalpic heating and cooling regimes, so providing a comprehensive thermodynamic characterization of this non-singular spacetime.

</details>


### [175] [Quantum Field Theory Of Cosmological Perturbations Induced By Ultralight Dark Matter](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27133)
*Tomislav Prokopec,Marco Vecchioni*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文发展了一个第一性原理场论框架，研究超轻标量场（ULDM）在物质主导时期对线性宇宙学扰动的量子影响。通过推导引力子场动力学方程并对源项进行正则化，发现ULDM的经典凝聚态对引力波传播无影响，但压缩态量子压力导致的时变引力子有效质量可在特定原初引力波模式中驱动参数共振，不过该增长对非相对论性ULDM在物质-辐射等时刻可忽略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 原初扰动在物质主导时期的增长主要由暗物质驱动，而超轻标量场（ultralight scalar field, ULDM）作为暗物质候选者已被广泛研究，但以往模型通常采用经典高占据数近似，忽略其量子本质。本文旨在从第一性原理出发，构建一个保留ULDM量子性质的场论框架，以准确评估其对线性宇宙学扰动的影响。

Method: 本文发展了一个第一性原理场论框架（first-principles field-theoretic framework），明确保留ULDM的量子属性。在物质主导时期，推导出引力子场动力学的封闭方程，并在绝热（WKB）近似下，对ULDM场的通用高斯初始态计算并正则化其源项，对非局域项采用中点工作假设（middle-point working assumption）。最后通过规范固定（gauge-fixing）得到最终动力学方程。

Result: 规范固定后发现，与先前结论相反，ULDM的经典凝聚态（classical condensate）对引力波传播无影响。然而，压缩态（squeezed state）的量子压力（quantum pressure）导致的时变引力子有效质量（time-dependent graviton effective mass）可在特定原初引力波模式中驱动参数共振（parametric resonance）。该共振增长在幂律压缩谱（power-law squeezing spectrum）假设下，对于质量范围 m ∼ 10⁻²¹–10⁻²⁴ eV 的非相对论性ULDM，在物质-辐射等时刻可忽略不计。

Conclusion: ULDM的经典凝聚态不影响引力波传播，而量子压缩态可在引力子有效质量中诱导参数共振，但该效应在常规ULDM参数范围内（非相对论性、质量约10⁻²¹–10⁻²⁴ eV）于物质-辐射等时刻可忽略，说明传统经典近似在此场景下是合理的。

Abstract: The growth of primordial perturbations during the matter-dominated era is primarily driven by dark matter. Ultralight scalar fields (ULDM) are a promising candidate for this role, conventionally modeled as operating in a classical, high-occupation regime. In this work, we develop a first-principles field-theoretic framework to investigate the impact of ULDM on linear cosmological perturbations during matter domination, explicitly retaining its quantum nature. Deriving a closed equation for the graviton field dynamics, we compute and regularize its source terms for a generic Gaussian initial state of the ULDM field within the adiabatic (WKB) approximation, employing the middle-point working assumption for non-local terms. After gauge-fixing we find that, contrary to previous claims, the classical condensate of ULDM has no influence on gravitational wave propagation. However, the time-dependent graviton effective mass induced by quantum pressure of the squeezed state can drive parametric resonance in specific primordial gravitational wave modes. We demonstrate this growth is negligible for non-relativistic ULDM at matter-radiation equality under the assumption of a power-law squeezing spectrum for masses in the range $m \sim 10^{-21}{-}10^{-24}$ eV.

</details>


### [176] [Quadratic Axion Couplings in String Theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27190)
*Naman Agarwal,Andrew R. Frey,Ratul Mahanta,Evan McDonough*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文提出轴子（axion）与类轴子粒子（axion-like particle）在弦论（string theory）中可通过经典、微扰（perturbative）和非微扰（non-perturbative）机制产生与电磁动能项（electromagnetic kinetic term）的二次耦合 $θ^2 F^2$。研究表明这类二次耦合在弦论轴子景观（axiverse）中普遍存在，其耦合强度通常小于 $1/f^2$（$f$ 为轴子衰变常数），但远大于QCD轴子通过带电π子圈图产生的类似耦合，为探测弦论轴子景观提供了新途径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有轴子搜索多聚焦于与光子的线性耦合（linear coupling）$θF \tilde{F}$，而二次耦合（quadratic coupling）$θ^2 F^2$ 所对应的新现象学和新探测机会尚未被充分探索。本文旨在从弦论角度提出二次耦合的产生机制，并评估其在理论框架中的普遍性与可观测性。

Method: 作者将二次耦合的产生机制分为三类：经典的（classical）、微扰的（perturbative）和非微扰的（non-perturbative），并在基准例子（benchmark examples）中计算各类机制的耦合强度。特别关注瞬子（instanton）等非微扰量子贡献对耦合强度的影响。

Result: 微扰与非微扰量子贡献（如瞬子）产生的耦合强度被抑制，$g \ll 1$（单位为 $1/f^2$），但显著大于QCD轴子通过带电π子圈图产生的同类耦合。分析表明二次轴子-规范场耦合在弦论中普遍存在。

Conclusion: 弦论轴子景观中广泛存在二次轴子-规范场耦合，应将其作为探测弦论轴子景观（包括暗物质（dark matter）和暗能量（dark energy）候选者）以及研究轴子谱学（spectroscopy）的重要实验探针。

Abstract: Axions and axion-like particles are a compelling candidate for physics beyond the standard model. While many axion searches are focused on the linear coupling to photons $θF \tilde{F}$, the possibility of a quadratic coupling to the electromagnetic kinetic term, $θ^2 F^2$, leads to novel phenomenology and new opportunities for testing axion-like particles. In this work we propose mechanisms for generating this coupling in string theory, which can be broadly classified as classical, perturbative, and non-perturbative. In benchmark examples, we find that both perturbative and non-perturbative quantum contributions such as instantons lead to couplings that are suppressed, $g \ll 1$ in units of $1/f^2$ where $f$ is axion decay constant, though easily larger than analogous coupling of the QCD axion that is generated through loops of charged pions. These analyses suggest that quadratic axion couplings to gauge fields are ubiquitous in string theory, and should be taken seriously as a probe of the string theory axiverse, both of string theory candidates for dynamical axions, such as dark matter or dark energy, and for spectroscopy of the string theory axiverse.

</details>

### [177] [Nanoscale Storage of Incompatible Elements at Olivine Grain Boundaries in Natural Basalts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26329)
*Wenhao Zhao,Reid Cooper,Stephen Parman,Austin Akey,Greg Hirth*

Main category: astro-ph.EP

TL;DR: 本研究通过电子背散射衍射、透射电子显微镜和原子探针断层扫描的关联分析，对来自Piton de la Fournaise（留尼汪）和Mauna Loa（夏威夷）的未变形橄榄石集合体中的晶界（grain boundary）进行了系统表征。晶界为晶体结构，宽度约1 nm，无连续玻璃膜；Ca、Al、P、Na、Ti选择性富集，而Mg、Fe、Ni、Mn保持均匀。Ca的界面过剩量为2.94-2.97 atoms/nm²，位于玄武质橄榄石结晶温度下Hiraga等人（2004）提出的1523 K平衡分离等温线附近。化学宽度（约6-15 nm）显著大于结构核心宽度（约1 nm），表明结构核心与化学偏析之间解耦。橄榄石晶界因此构成一个独特的纳米尺度储库，需纳入橄榄石富集集合体的微量元素质量平衡中。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 晶界在多晶橄榄石中普遍存在，但其结构和微量元素化学特征仍缺乏约束，限制了对其在地幔地球化学过程中所起作用的理解。

Method: 关联使用电子背散射衍射（electron backscatter diffraction, EBSD）、透射电子显微镜（transmission electron microscopy, TEM）和原子探针断层扫描（atom probe tomography, APT），对来自Piton de la Fournaise和Mauna Loa的未变形橄榄石集合体中的晶界进行表征。

Result: 晶界为晶体结构，结构宽度约1 nm，无连续玻璃膜。Ca、Al、P、Na、Ti选择性富集，Mg、Fe、Ni、Mn保持均匀。Ca界面过剩量为2.94-2.97 atoms/nm²（0.327-0.330单层），位于Hiraga等人（2004）的1523 K平衡分离等温线上限附近。Ca化学宽度约6-9 nm，最强富集元素化学宽度约10-15 nm，显示结构核心与化学偏析的解耦。

Conclusion: 橄榄石晶界构成一个独特的纳米尺度储库（nanoscale reservoir），在橄榄石富集集合体的微量元素质量平衡中，需将晶界与晶体内部和残余熔体并列考虑。晶界形成可能发生在晶体生长过程中，或通过聚并（synneusis）及随后高温停留产生类似特征。

Abstract: Grain boundaries are pervasive in polycrystalline olivine, yet their structure and trace-element chemistry remain poorly constrained. We characterize boundaries in undeformed olivine aggregates from Piton de la Fournaise (La Reunion) and Mauna Loa (Hawaii) by correlating electron backscatter diffraction, transmission electron microscopy, and atom probe tomography. The boundaries are crystalline, with a structural width of about 1 nm and no continuous glassy film resolved. Ca, Al, P, Na, and Ti are selectively enriched, whereas Mg, Fe, Ni, and Mn remain homogeneous. Several enriched mass windows coincide with nominal rare-earth-element ion positions, but unresolved isobars and the lack of a diagnostic isotope envelope preclude secure assignments. Divalent-cation segregation follows an ionic-size-misfit trend, while aliovalent departures require unconstrained contributions. The Ca interfacial excess is 2.94-2.97 atoms per square nanometer, equivalent to 0.327-0.330 monolayers, and lies near the upper published 1523 K equilibrium segregation isotherm of Hiraga et al. (2004), within the range of basaltic olivine crystallization temperatures. This supports boundary formation during crystal growth, although synneusis followed by high-temperature residence could produce a similar signature. Ca chemical widths are about 6-9 nm, and those of the most strongly enriched elements reach about 10-15 nm, demonstrating decoupling between the narrow structural core and broader chemical segregation. Compared with published atom-probe data on deformed olivine, these boundaries preserve broader halos, although analytical differences make the contrast tentative. Olivine grain boundaries therefore constitute a distinct nanoscale reservoir that should be included alongside crystal interiors and residual melt in trace-element mass balances of olivine-rich aggregates.

</details>
